ALL_F1',wallStyle:r.wallStyle||'wall_f1',color:r.wallColor||null,fence:r.fence,fenceH:r.fenceH,fenceLeanM:r.fenceLeanM,postSpacingM:r.postSpacingM,advertising:r.advertising});
   }
 }
 return out;
}
function apexPitSegments(){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,n=cw&&cw.pitNetwork;if(!n||!Array.isArray(n.runs))return[];
 if(Array.isArray(n._segments))return n._segments;const segs=[];
 for(const r of n.runs||[]){const pts=r.points||[];for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const a=pts[i],b=pts[i+1],dx=b.x-a.x,dz=b.z-a.z,len=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(len<.02)continue;const aw=Number.isFinite(+a.width)?+a.width:(r.width||5.9),bw=Number.isFinite(+b.width)?+b.width:(r.width||5.9),al=Number.isFinite(+a.leftWidth)?+a.leftWidth:aw*.5,bl=Number.isFinite(+b.leftWidth)?+b.leftWidth:bw*.5,ar=Number.isFinite(+a.rightWidth)?+a.rightWidth:aw*.5,br=Number.isFinite(+b.rightWidth)?+b.rightWidth:bw*.5;segs.push({runId:r.id,width:(aw+bw)*.5,leftWidth:(al+bl)*.5,rightWidth:(ar+br)*.5,x1:a.x,z1:a.z,y1:a.y,x2:b.x,z2:b.z,y2:b.y,s1:a.pitS,s2:b.pitS,len})}}
 n._segments=segs;return segs;
}
function nearestPitState(x,z,hint=null){
 const segs=apexPitSegments();if(!segs.length)return null;let best=null,bestD=Infinity;
 const test=(sg,idx)=>{const dx=sg.x2-sg.x1,dz=sg.z2-sg.z1,l2=dx*dx+dz*dz||1,t=clamp(((x-sg.x1)*dx+(z-sg.z1)*dz)/l2,0,1),px=sg.x1+dx*t,pz=sg.z1+dz*t,dd=(x-px)*(x-px)+(z-pz)*(z-pz);if(dd<bestD){const len=Math.sqrt(l2),tx=dx/len,tz=dz/len,nx=-tz,nz=tx;bestD=dd;best={x:px,z:pz,y:lerp(sg.y1,sg.y2,t),tx,tz,nx,nz,lat:(x-px)*nx+(z-pz)*nz,dist:Math.sqrt(dd),width:sg.width,pitS:lerp(sg.s1,sg.s2,t),runId:sg.runId,seg:sg,index:idx}}};
 if(Number.isFinite(hint)){const h=Math.max(0,Math.min(segs.length-1,Math.floor(hint)));for(let k=-34;k<=34;k++){const i=h+k;if(i>=0&&i<segs.length)test(segs[i],i)}if(bestD>18*18)for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i++)test(segs[i],i)}else for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i++)test(segs[i],i);
 return best;
}
function syncPlayerDriveNetwork(trackState){
 if(!player)return null;const prev=player.driveNetwork||'TRACK',pit=nearestPitState(player.x,player.z,player.pitIndex),env=envProfile(trackState&&Number.isFinite(trackState.s)?trackState.s:player.s),mainDist=Math.abs(trackState&&Number.isFinite(trackState.lat)?trackState.lat:player.lat),inside=!!(pit&&pit.dist<=pit.width*.56+.45);
 let mode=prev;
 const pitCycle=player.pitStop&&player.pitStop.active&&['IN_LANE','SERVICE','EXITING'].includes(player.pitStop.phase);
 if(pitCycle&&pit)mode='PIT';
 else if(prev==='PIT'){
   // No expulsamos al coche del PIT sólo porque la pista principal quede cerca.
   // Salimos cuando abandonamos realmente el corredor del pit y ya estamos cerca de pista.
   if(!pit)mode='TRACK';
   else if(pit.dist>pit.width*.90+2.8&&mainDist<env.half+env.curb+1.8)mode='TRACK';
 }else if(inside){
   // En la zona de mezcla ambas líneas pueden casi coincidir. En cuanto el coche sigue
   // físicamente el eje PIT mejor que el eje TRACK, la red cambia sin esperar varios metros.
   if(mainDist>env.half*.52||pit.dist+.12<mainDist)mode='PIT';
 }
 player.driveNetwork=mode;
 if(pit){player.pitIndex=pit.index;player.pitS=pit.pitS;player.pitLat=pit.lat;player.pitState=pit}else{player.pitState=null;player.pitLat=0}
 return mode==='PIT'?pit:null;
}
function playerDriveSurfacePose(){
 if(player&&player.driveNetwork==='PIT'&&player.pitState){const p=player.pitState,rd=Math.hypot(p.nx,0,p.nz)||1;return {...p,centerY:p.y,bank:0,tanBank:0,surfaceRx:p.nx/rd,surfaceRy:0,surfaceRz:p.nz/rd}}
 return trackSurfacePoseAt(player.s,player.lat);
}
// v5.230.48 / FASE 1: un único perfil de explanación, independiente del circuito.
// El asfalto conserva su peralte; desde cada borde, la escapatoria es horizontal
// transversalmente hasta el muro REAL del SVG, si existe. No prolongar un muro a
// través de huecos o accesos: guideContinuousSample respeta subpaths y tramos.
function apexPhase1Config(){
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData&&trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir)||{},t=j.terrain||{};
 return {blendM:Number.isFinite(+t.embankmentBlendM)?clamp(+t.embankmentBlendM,8,120):26,
  flatToWall:t.runoffFlatToWall!==false,maxSlopeDeg:Number.isFinite(+t.maxCutFillSlopeDeg)?clamp(+t.maxCutFillSlopeDeg,15,55):34};
}
function apexRoadSideHeight(q,env,side){
 return q.y+Math.tan(Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0)*env.half*side;
}
function apexRoadFlatLimit(s,env,side){
 const base=env.half+env.curb+env.runoff,cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,
       samples=cw&&(side>0?cw.wallLeftSamples:cw.wallRightSamples);
 if(!apexPhase1Config().flatToWall||!Array.isArray(samples)||!samples.length)return base;
 const hit=guideContinuousSample(samples,s,trackData.km*1000,18,24);
 // No atravesar huecos de muro. Si el muro no cubre este punto,
 // la escapatoria de respaldo termina en la anchura configurada por envProfile.
 if(!hit||!Number.isFinite(hit.lat)||Math.sign(hit.lat)!==side)return base;
 return Math.max(env.half+.12,Math.abs(hit.lat));
}
function apexRoadShoulderYAt(s,lat){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),env=envProfile(s),side=lat>=0?1:-1;
 return q.y+Math.tan(Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0)*clamp(lat,-env.half,env.half);
}
// La zona UNDER/OVER nunca excava ni levanta el terreno natural para seguir
// la plataforma. También se comprueban las huellas SVG: en un cruce a distinta
// cota, el brazo opuesto puede ser la línea 2D más cercana.
function apexPhase1StructureAtWorld(x,z,s){
 const byS=authoredStructureZoneAtS(s);
 if(byS&&(byS.kind==='UNDER'||byS.kind==='OVER'))return byS;
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;
 if(cw&&Array.isArray(cw.landmarks))for(const it of cw.landmarks){
  if(!it||(it.type!=='bridge'&&it.type!=='tunnel')||it.pedestrianBridge||!Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)||it.worldFootprint.length<3)continue;
  if(apexPointInsideClosedContour(x,z,it.worldFootprint))return{kind:it.type==='bridge'?'OVER':'UNDER',name:it.name};
 }
 return null;
}
// Se usa tanto al generar la malla una vez como al apoyar puntos del entorno.
// Fuera del corredor, un perfil suave de corte/relleno conecta hasta la placa SVG.
// v5.230.49 · EXPLANACION UNIVERSAL SVG/JSON. Ninguna condición por ID de GP.
// Un índice de segmentos comparte geometría entre pista, pitlane, viales y bocas
// de túnel. Se construye durante la carga y no busca todos los viales por frame.
function apexGradeSpatialIndex(){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;
 if(!cw)return null;
 if(cw._apexGradeIndex)return cw._apexGradeIndex;
 const size=48,buckets=new Map(),segments=[],tunnels=[];
 const add=(kind,pts,ys,width,extra=null)=>{
  if(!Array.isArray(pts)||pts.length<2)return;
  for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++){
   const a=pts[i-1],b=pts[i],ax=Array.isArray(a)?+a[0]:+a.x,az=Array.isArray(a)?+a[1]:+a.z,
     bx=Array.isArray(b)?+b[0]:+b.x,bz=Array.isArray(b)?+b[1]:+b.z,len=Math.hypot(bx-ax,bz-az);
   if(!Number.isFinite(len)||len<.12)continue;
   const ay=ys?+ys[i-1]:(Number.isFinite(a.y)?a.y:0),by=ys?+ys[i]:(Number.isFinite(b.y)?b.y:0);
   const seg={kind,ax,az,bx,bz,ay,by,width,extra,len};
   const j=segments.length;segments.push(seg);
   const margin=Math.max(36,width*.5+32);
   for(let ix=Math.floor((Math.min(ax,bx)-margin)/size);ix<=Math.floor((Math.max(ax,bx)+margin)/size);ix++)
    for(let iz=Math.floor((Math.min(az,bz)-margin)/size);iz<=Math.floor((Math.max(az,bz)+margin)/size);iz++){
     const key=ix+','+iz,entry=buckets.get(key);if(entry)entry.push(j);else buckets.set(key,[j]);
    }
  }
 };
 const pn=cw.pitNetwork;
 if(pn&&Array.isArray(pn.runs))for(const r of pn.runs||[])add('PIT',r.points,null,Math.max(3.8,+r.width||5.9));
 for(const it of cw.landmarks||[])if(it&&it.type==='road'&&Array.isArray(it.worldPath))
  add('ROAD',it.worldPath,it.worldPathY,Math.max(3,+it.width||8),it);
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 for(const z of cw.structureZones||[])if(z&&z.kind==='UNDER'){
  let span=mod(z.s1-z.s0,L);if(span<1||span>L*.48)continue;
  const pts=[],ys=[];
  for(let d=0;d<span;d+=3.8){const s=mod(z.s0+d,L),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s);pts.push([q.x,q.z]);ys.push(q.y)}
  const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(z.s1);pts.push([q.x,q.z]);ys.push(q.y);
  const ep=envProfile(z.s0),roadHalf=Number.isFinite(+ep.half)?+ep.half:Math.max(5,(+z.width||18)*.34);
  const half=Math.max(3.5,(+z.width||0)*.5,roadHalf+1);
  const geom={...z,s0:z.s0,s1:z.s1,span,half,roofH:Math.max(3.2,+z.height||5.6),topRiseM:Math.max(Math.max(3.2,+z.height||5.6)+.45,Number.isFinite(z.roofRiseM)?z.roofRiseM:0),pts};
  tunnels.push(geom);add('TUNNEL',pts,ys,2*half,geom);
 }
 // El cruce procedimental de otros circuitos sólo participa si carece de SVG.
 const su=trackData.gradeCrossing;
 if(su&&!tunnels.length){const c=su,q0=renderRibbonFrameAt(mod(c.underS-c.underHalf,L)),q1=renderRibbonFrameAt(mod(c.underS+c.underHalf,L));const z={kind:'UNDER',s0:mod(c.underS-c.underHalf,L),s1:mod(c.underS+c.underHalf,L),half:envProfile(c.underS).half+1,roofH:2.8,span:c.underHalf*2};tunnels.push(z);add('TUNNEL',[[q0.x,q0.z],[q1.x,q1.z]],[q0.y,q1.y],z.half*2,z)}
 cw._apexGradeIndex={size,buckets,segments,tunnels};return cw._apexGradeIndex;
}
function apexNearestGradeSegment(x,z,kind=null,maxD=86){
 const idx=apexGradeSpatialIndex();if(!idx)return null;
 const ix=Math.floor(x/idx.size),iz=Math.floor(z/idx.size),n=Math.ceil(maxD/idx.size),seen=new Set();let best=null,best2=maxD*maxD;
 for(let i=-n;i<=n;i++)for(let j=-n;j<=n;j++)for(const id of idx.buckets.get((ix+i)+','+(iz+j))||[]){
  if(seen.has(id))continue;seen.add(id);const sg=idx.segments[id];if(kind&&!(kind==='PAVED'?(sg.kind==='PIT'||sg.kind==='ROAD'):sg.kind===kind))continue;
  const dx=sg.bx-sg.ax,dz=sg.bz-sg.az,len2=dx*dx+dz*dz,t=clamp(((x-sg.ax)*dx+(z-sg.az)*dz)/len2,0,1),px=sg.ax+t*dx,pz=sg.az+t*dz,d2=(x-px)**2+(z-pz)**2;
  if(d2<best2){best2=d2;best={...sg,dist:Math.sqrt(d2),x:px,z:pz,y:lerp(sg.ay,sg.by,t)}}
 }
 return best;
}
function apexTunnelCutAt(x,z){
 const g=apexNearestGradeSegment(x,z,'TUNNEL',38);
 if(!g||g.dist>g.width*.5+.18)return null;
 const t=g.extra,L=trackData&&trackData.km*1000;
 if(t&&Number.isFinite(t.s0)&&Number.isFinite(t.s1)&&L>0){
   // Use the longitudinal track interval of the SVG path, not rounded caps
   // produced by a nearest-point-on-segment query near the mouth.
   const ns=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:46,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:42});
   if(!wrapRangeContains(ns.s,t.s0,t.s1,L))return null;
 }
 return g;
}

// v5.230.62 · BOCA DE TÚNEL UNIVERSAL, alineada con la misma sección del render.
// Se deriva exclusivamente de cada zona UNDER ya importada: no hay IDs, nombres
// ni excepciones de circuito. El terreno conserva sus cotas, pero cerca de cada
// extremo se abre una ventana corta y precisa para que la malla no atraviese el vano.
function apexTunnelPortalDescriptors(){
 const idx=apexGradeSpatialIndex();if(!idx||!idx.tunnels||!idx.tunnels.length)return[];
 if(Array.isArray(idx._universalPortals))return idx._universalPortals;
 const out=[];
 for(const t of idx.tunnels){
   const span=Math.max(1,Number(t.span)||0);
   for(const end of [0,1]){
     const s=end===0?t.s0:t.s1,q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),env=envProfile(s),st={s,q,env},
       w=tunnelApertureHalfWidth(st),
       insideSign=end===0?1:-1,
       insideDepth=clamp(span*.15,3.0,5.6),
       outsideDepth=clamp(w*.10,1.15,1.85),
       h=tunnelRoofHeightForState(st),
       topRise=Math.max(h+.42,Number(t.topRiseM)||0);
     out.push({t,s,end,q,env,st,w,h,topRise,insideSign,insideDepth,outsideDepth,visual:apexTunnelVisualProfile(t)});
   }
 }
 idx._universalPortals=out;return out;
}
function apexPortalLocalAtWorld(x,z,margin=0){
 let best=null,bestScore=Infinity;
 for(const p of apexTunnelPortalDescriptors()){
   const dx=x-p.q.x,dz=z-p.q.z,along=(dx*p.q.tx+dz*p.q.tz)*p.insideSign,den=p.q.ox*p.q.ox+p.q.oz*p.q.oz||1,lat=(dx*p.q.ox+dz*p.q.oz)/den,
     m=Math.max(0,margin||0);
   if(along<-p.outsideDepth-m||along>p.insideDepth+m||Math.abs(lat)>p.w+m)continue;
   const score=Math.abs(along)*.28+Math.abs(lat)*.05;
   if(score<bestScore){bestScore=score;best={...p,along,lat};}
 }
 return best;
}
function apexPortalInfluenceAtWorld(x,z,margin=0){return apexPortalLocalAtWorld(x,z,Math.max(0,margin||0));}
function apexPortalTerrainCutAtWorld(x,z,pad=.12){return apexPortalLocalAtWorld(x,z,Math.max(0,pad||0));}

// v5.230.62 · MOLDE GEOMÉTRICO EXACTO DEL TÚNEL.
// No elevamos ni rebajamos el terreno. La misma sección que dibuja paredes/techo
// define el hueco interior que se resta de la malla de terreno. Por tanto el borde
// del terreno termina exactamente donde empiezan las paredes grises y el techo.
function apexTunnelExactLocalAtWorld(x,z,tunnel=null){
 const L=trackData&&trackData.km*1000;if(!L)return null;
 let t=tunnel,g=null;
 if(!t){g=apexNearestGradeSegment(x,z,'TUNNEL',58);if(!g)return null;t=g.extra||null;}
 const ns=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:58,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:54});
 if(!ns||!Number.isFinite(ns.s))return null;
 if(t&&Number.isFinite(t.s0)&&Number.isFinite(t.s1)&&!wrapRangeContains(ns.s,t.s0,t.s1,L))return null;
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(ns.s),env=envProfile(ns.s),st={s:ns.s,q,env},w=tunnelApertureHalfWidth(st),
   dx=x-q.x,dz=z-q.z,den=q.ox*q.ox+q.oz*q.oz||1,
   lat=(dx*q.ox+dz*q.oz)/den,roofH=tunnelRoofHeightForState(st),
   baseY=q.y+Math.tan(Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0)*lat,roofY=baseY+roofH;
 return{t,g,ns,q,env,st,w,lat,roofH,baseY,roofY};
}
function apexTunnelTerrainRenderY(x,z,naturalY,pad=.18){return naturalY;}
function apexTunnelTerrainIntrudesAtWorld(x,z,y,pad=.18){
 const e=apexTunnelExactLocalAtWorld(x,z);return !!(e&&Math.abs(e.lat)<e.w+Math.max(0,pad||0)&&Number.isFinite(y)&&y<e.roofY-.015);
}
function apexTunnelClipLerp(A,B,t){return{x:lerp(A.x,B.x,t),y:lerp(A.y,B.y,t),z:lerp(A.z,B.z,t)};}
function apexTunnelClipPolyScalar(poly,scalar,keepPositive=true,eps=1e-5){
 if(!poly||poly.length<3)return[];const out=[];
 for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){
  const A=poly[i],B=poly[(i+1)%poly.length],fa=scalar(A),fb=scalar(B),
    inA=keepPositive?fa>=-eps:fa<=eps,inB=keepPositive?fb>=-eps:fb<=eps;
  if(inA)out.push(A);
  if(inA!==inB){const den=fa-fb,t=Math.abs(den)<1e-9?.5:clamp(fa/den,0,1);out.push(apexTunnelClipLerp(A,B,t));}
 }
 return out.length>=3?out:[];
}
function apexTunnelClipMetrics(P,t){
 const L=trackData&&trackData.km*1000,ns=nearestTrackState(P.x,P.z,null,{radius:62,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:58});
 if(!ns||!Number.isFinite(ns.s)||!L)return null;
 // En los triángulos de la boca permitimos ~2 m de tolerancia longitudinal; el
 // portal exacto se encarga del último corte frontal.
 if(t&&Number.isFinite(t.s0)&&Number.isFinite(t.s1)&&!wrapRangeContains(ns.s,t.s0,t.s1,L)){
   const d0=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(ns.s,t.s0,L)),d1=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(ns.s,t.s1,L));if(Math.min(d0,d1)>2.2)return null;
 }
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(ns.s),env=envProfile(ns.s),st={s:ns.s,q,env},w=tunnelApertureHalfWidth(st),
   dx=P.x-q.x,dz=P.z-q.z,den=q.ox*q.ox+q.oz*q.oz||1,lat=(dx*q.ox+dz*q.oz)/den,
   roofH=tunnelRoofHeightForState(st),baseY=q.y+Math.tan(Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0)*lat;
 return{lat,w,roofY:baseY+roofH};
}
// Resta de un triángulo de terreno el prisma interior del túnel. Conservamos tres
// regiones disjuntas: exterior izquierda, exterior derecha y terreno POR ENCIMA del
// techo entre ambas paredes. Así la montaña continúa sobre el túnel, pero nunca puede
// atravesar paredes/techo hacia el habitáculo.
function apexClipTerrainTriangleByTunnel(tri,t){
 if(!tri||tri.length<3||!t)return tri?[tri]:[];
 const metric=P=>apexTunnelClipMetrics(P,t),C={x:(tri[0].x+tri[1].x+tri[2].x)/3,y:(tri[0].y+tri[1].y+tri[2].y)/3,z:(tri[0].z+tri[1].z+tri[2].z)/3},mc=metric(C);
 if(!mc)return[tri];
 const near=Math.abs(mc.lat)<=mc.w+3.2||tri.some(P=>{const m=metric(P);return m&&Math.abs(m.lat)<=m.w+3.2});if(!near)return[tri];
 const leftF=P=>{const m=metric(P);return m?-(m.lat+m.w):1;},
   rightF=P=>{const m=metric(P);return m?(m.lat-m.w):1;},
   cLeftF=P=>{const m=metric(P);return m?(m.lat+m.w):1;},
   cRightF=P=>{const m=metric(P);return m?(m.w-m.lat):1;},
   roofF=P=>{const m=metric(P);return m?(P.y-m.roofY):1;};
 const pieces=[];
 let p=apexTunnelClipPolyScalar(tri,leftF,true);if(p.length>=3)pieces.push(p);
 p=apexTunnelClipPolyScalar(tri,rightF,true);if(p.length>=3)pieces.push(p);
 p=apexTunnelClipPolyScalar(tri,cLeftF,true);if(p.length>=3)p=apexTunnelClipPolyScalar(p,cRightF,true);if(p.length>=3)p=apexTunnelClipPolyScalar(p,roofF,true);if(p.length>=3)pieces.push(p);
 return pieces;
}
function apexClipTerrainQuadByTunnel(A,B,C,D){
 const cx=(A.x+B.x+C.x+D.x)*.25,cz=(A.z+B.z+C.z+D.z)*.25,g=apexNearestGradeSegment(cx,cz,'TUNNEL',46);
 if(!g||g.dist>g.width*.5+4.0)return[[A,B,C,D]];const t=g.extra||null;if(!t)return[[A,B,C,D]];
 const out=[];for(const tri of [[A,B,C],[A,C,D]])for(const p of apexClipTerrainTriangleByTunnel(tri,t))if(p&&p.length>=3)out.push(p);return out;
}
// v5.230.58 · AISLAMIENTO TOTAL TÚNEL / TERRENO SVG.
// El túnel es una estructura situada BAJO la superficie, no una herramienta de
// modelado del relieve. Si un punto pertenece a una zona UNDER, devolvemos la cota
// natural sin tocarla: nada de roofY, topMin, embudos, taludes laterales ni blends
// de boca. La malla de terreno conserva literalmente las cotas definidas por el SVG.
// La apertura visual la forman exclusivamente paredes, techo y caras de portal.
function apexPhase1GroundBlend(x,z,natural,ns=null){
 if(!trackData)return natural;
 const cfg=apexPhase1Config(),st=ns||nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:190,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:165});
 let out=natural,mainProtected=false;
 if(st&&Number.isFinite(st.s)&&Number.isFinite(st.lat)&&Math.abs(st.lat)<195){
  const env=envProfile(st.s),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(st.s),lat=Math.abs(st.lat),side=st.lat>=0?1:-1,
        structure=apexPhase1StructureAtWorld(x,z,st.s),edgeY=apexRoadSideHeight(q,env,side),limit=apexRoadFlatLimit(st.s,env,side),
        tunnel=apexTunnelCutAt(x,z),under=(structure&&structure.kind==='UNDER')||!!tunnel,
        over=(structure&&structure.kind==='OVER')&&!under;

  // Regla absoluta: UNDER jamás modifica la placa de terreno.
  // Esta salida temprana evita también que PIT/u otras explanaciones posteriores
  // puedan retocar accidentalmente el terreno dentro de la huella del túnel.
  if(under)return natural;

  if(!over){
   if(lat<=env.half){out=q.y+Math.tan(q.bank||0)*st.lat-.18;mainProtected=true;}
   else if(lat<=limit){out=edgeY-.18;mainProtected=true;}
   else{
    const rise=Math.abs(natural-edgeY),blend=Math.max(cfg.blendM,1.5*rise/Math.tan(cfg.maxSlopeDeg*Math.PI/180));
    if(lat<limit+blend)out=lerp(edgeY-.14,natural,terrainEase((lat-limit)/blend));
   }
  }
 }
 // PIT puede explanar su propia plataforma fuera de una zona UNDER. ROAD nunca
 // modifica la placa: los viales se renderizan después apoyados sobre el relieve.
 const sg=apexNearestGradeSegment(x,z,'PAVED',96);
 if(sg&&sg.kind==='PIT'){
  const half=sg.width*.5,near=sg.dist<=half+.20;
  if(!mainProtected||!st||Math.abs(st.lat)>envProfile(st.s).half+.2){
   if(near){out=sg.y-.18;}
   else{
    const blend=Math.max(cfg.blendM,Math.abs(natural-sg.y)/Math.tan(cfg.maxSlopeDeg*Math.PI/180));
    if(sg.dist<half+blend){const h=lerp(sg.y-.14,natural,terrainEase((sg.dist-half)/blend));
     if(Math.abs(h-sg.y)<Math.abs(out-sg.y)||Math.abs(out-sg.y)>.9*blend)out=h;
    }
   }
  }
 }
 return out;
}
function apexPhase1WallFootY(x,z){
 const st=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:140,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:120});
 if(!st||!Number.isFinite(st.s))return apexWorldGroundY(x,z);
 return apexRoadShoulderYAt(st.s,st.lat);
}
function apexNearestTrackSurfaceY(x,z){
 const ns=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:140,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:120}),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(ns.s);
 const env=envProfile(ns.s);return q.y+Math.tan(Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0)*clamp(ns.lat,-env.half,env.half);
}
function apexAlignedCircuitSurfaceY(x,z,opts=null){
 const preferPit=!(opts&&opts.preferPit===false),trackY=apexNearestTrackSurfaceY(x,z);
 if(preferPit){
   const pit=nearestPitState(x,z,null);
   if(pit&&pit.dist<=Math.max(2.4,(pit.width||5.9)*.88+1.45))return pit.y;
 }
 return trackY;
}
function apexWorldGroundY(x,z){
 const ns=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:190,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:165}),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(ns.s),env=envProfile(ns.s),st={s:ns.s,q,env},base=terrainGroundY(st),natural=apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z,base);
 const pg=apexPlateTerrainGrid();
 return pg?natural:apexPhase1GroundBlend(x,z,natural,ns);
}
// v5.230.40 · Geometría X/Z autoritativa SVG, elevación reanclada al JSON activo.
// Se ejecuta al preparar el circuito, jamás durante el dibujo. Un cambio de perfil
// longitudinal o de cotas vuelve a apoyar muros, vallas y pasarelas en el suelo.
function apexRegroundImportedWorld(){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;if(!cw||!cw.authoritative)return;
 const vp=trackData.verticalProfile,vs=vp&&vp.samples||[],json=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir)||{},tc=json.terrain||{},stamp=[trackData.layout,trackData.pts.length,vs.length,vs.length?vs[0][1]:0,vs.length?vs[Math.floor(vs.length*.5)][1]:0,vs.length?vs[vs.length-1][1]:0,tc.contourReference||'',tc.contourScale||1,tc.embankmentBlendM||'',tc.maxCutFillSlopeDeg||'',tc.runoffFlatToWall!==false].join('|');
 if(cw._apexGroundStamp===stamp)return;
 // Mantener los huecos EXACTOS: no conectamos subpaths ni regeneramos la planta.
 // La misma cota corregida se comparte entre los extremos contiguos por una caché local.
 const heights=new Map(),ground=(x,z)=>{
   const key=Math.round(x*100)+'/'+Math.round(z*100);
   if(heights.has(key))return heights.get(key);
   const y=apexWorldGroundY(x,z);heights.set(key,y);return y;
 };
 for(const w of cw.wallSegments||[]){w.y1=apexPhase1WallFootY(w.x1,w.z1);w.y2=apexPhase1WallFootY(w.x2,w.z2);}
 for(const it of cw.landmarks||[]){
   if(!it||!it.anchor)continue;
   const a=it.anchor;
   if(Number.isFinite(a.x)&&Number.isFinite(a.z)){
     a.y=ground(a.x,a.z);
     if(a._apexGroundCache)a._apexGroundCache.clear();
   }
   if(Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)&&it.type!=='road'&&it.type!=='tunnel'&&it.type!=='bridge')it.worldPathY=it.worldPath.map(p=>ground(p[0],p[1]));
   if(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&Array.isArray(it.worldY))it.worldY=it.worldFootprint.map(p=>it.type==='surfaceZone'||it.type==='pit'?apexAlignedCircuitSurfaceY(p[0],p[1]):ground(p[0],p[1]));
   if(Array.isArray(it.forestPoints))for(const p of it.forestPoints)p[2]=ground(p[0],p[1]);
 }
 // Los muros detallados pueden superar los 2.000 segmentos; las colisiones conservan
 // absolutamente todos. Sólo la MALLA VISUAL une tramos contiguos de <=6 m.
 // runId/side delimitan cada muro: los accesos peatonales y cortes SVG nunca se tapan.
 cw._wallRenderSegments=[];
 let cur=null,total=0;
 for(const s of cw.wallSegments||[]){
   const span=Math.hypot(s.x2-s.x1,s.z2-s.z1),same=cur&&cur.runId===s.runId&&cur.side===s.side&&cur.kind===s.kind&&cur.fence===s.fence&&cur.advertising===s.advertising&&
     Math.hypot(cur.x2-s.x1,cur.z2-s.z1)<.08&&total+span<=6.5&&Math.floor(cur.s/72)===Math.floor(s.s/72);
   if(same){cur.x2=s.x2;cur.z2=s.z2;cur.y2=s.y2;total+=span;}
   else{cur={...s};cw._wallRenderSegments.push(cur);total=span;}
 }
 cw._apexGroundStamp=stamp;
}
function apexStringHash(s){let h=2166136261>>>0;for(const ch of String(s||'')){h^=ch.charCodeAt(0);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)}return h>>>0}
function apexForestScatter(poly,seedText,count=18){
 if(!Array.isArray(poly)||poly.length<3)return[];let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;for(const p of poly){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1])}let seed=apexStringHash(seedText),out=[],tries=0;const rnd=()=>{seed=(Math.imul(seed,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return seed/4294967296};while(out.length<count&&tries<count*18){tries++;const x=minX+rnd()*(maxX-minX),z=minZ+rnd()*(maxZ-minZ);if(apexPointInPolyXZ(x,z,poly))out.push([x,z,apexWorldGroundY(x,z)])}return out;
}
function apexSvgLandmarkExact(el,index,ctx){
 const sourceRole=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-role')||'').trim().toLowerCase(),rawType=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-type')||roleNameToImportType(sourceRole)||'').trim();if(!rawType||sourceRole==='advertising')return null;
 const rawPts=apexSvgSampleMounted(ctx.root,el,{step:5}),pts=rawPts.map(ctx.toWorld);if(!pts.length)return null;let cx=0,cz=0;for(const p of pts){cx+=p[0];cz+=p[1]}cx/=pts.length;cz/=pts.length;
 const st=nearestTrackState(cx,cz,null),sideAttr=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-side')),side=Number.isFinite(sideAttr)&&sideAttr!==0?Math.sign(sideAttr):Math.sign(st.lat)||1,anchor=proposalWorldAnchor(st.s,st.lat,0,parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-maxds'))||1750);anchor.x=cx;anchor.z=cz;anchor.groundQ=poseAt(st.s);
 const type=rawType==='building'?'block':rawType,variant=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-variant')||'').trim().toLowerCase(),appearance=apexSvgReadAppearance(el,type,sourceRole),node={type,role:sourceRole||rawType,sourceId:String(el.id||''),sourceRole,variant,anchor,name:String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-name')||el.id||`${rawType} ${index+1}`),side,trackLat:st.lat,height:(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-height'))||0),color:el.getAttribute('data-apex-color')||undefined,apexFamily:appearance.family,apexStyle:appearance.style,style:appearance.style,material:appearance.material||undefined,roofStyle:appearance.roofStyle||undefined,portalStyle:appearance.portalStyle||undefined,interiorStyle:appearance.interiorStyle||undefined,hasLights:appearance.lights,hasPanels:appearance.panels,hasServiceWalk:appearance.serviceWalk,lightSpacingM:appearance.lightSpacingM,panelSpacingM:appearance.panelSpacingM,serviceWalkWidthM:appearance.serviceWalkWidthM,garageBays:appearance.garageBays,hasGlass:appearance.glass,hasBalcony:appearance.balcony,hasPitwall:appearance.pitwall,garageStyle:appearance.garageStyle||undefined,hasRoof:appearance.covered,seatColor:appearance.seatColor||undefined,seatRows:appearance.seatRows};
 // data-apex-width/depth/length/height son magnitudes físicas en metros. Sólo las
 // coordenadas geométricas del SVG pasan por ctx.scale.
 const wAttr=(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-width'))||0),dAttr=(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-depth'))||0),lAttr=(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-length'))||0);
 if(type==='gantry'){node.startGantry=sourceRole==='start-gantry';node.finishGantry=sourceRole==='finish-gantry';node.text=node.startGantry?'START':(node.finishGantry?'FINISH':node.name)}
 if(type==='block'||type==='parking'||type==='terrainZone'||type==='surfaceZone'||type==='water'||type==='marking'||type==='forestZone'){
   node.width=wAttr||20;node.depth=dAttr||18;node.worldFootprint=pts;node.worldY=pts.map(p=>(type==='surfaceZone'?apexAlignedCircuitSurfaceY(p[0],p[1]):apexWorldGroundY(p[0],p[1])));node.grounded=true;
   if(type==='block'){node.height=node.height||12;node.buildingStyle=node.apexStyle||'building_generic';}else node.height=node.height||.04;
   if(type==='forestZone'){node.variant='fir';node.color=node.color||'#4b7b50';node.forestPoints=apexForestScatter(pts,node.name||node.sourceId,Math.max(10,Math.min(36,Math.round(pts.length*.7))))}
   if(type==='terrainZone')node.color=node.color||'#667a4d';
   if(type==='surfaceZone'){node.surfaceKind=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-surface')||sourceRole.replace(/^surface-/,'').replace(/^runoff-/,'')||'ASPHALT').toUpperCase();const cm={ASPHALT:'#676d72',GRAVEL:'#ad9068',SAND:'#bea166',GRASS:'#2e6737'};node.color=node.color||cm[node.surfaceKind]||cm.ASPHALT;}
   if(type==='water'){node.waterKind=sourceRole==='sea'?'SEA':'LAKE';node.color=node.color||(node.waterKind==='SEA'?'#2b739d':'#347ca5');}
   if(type==='marking'){node.markingKind=sourceRole==='start-line'?'START_LINE':(sourceRole==='finish-line'?'FINISH_LINE':(sourceRole==='urban-stripes'?'URBAN_STRIPES':(sourceRole==='curb-left'?'CURB_LEFT':(sourceRole==='curb-right'?'CURB_RIGHT':'CROSSWALK'))));node.color=node.color||((sourceRole==='curb-left'||sourceRole==='curb-right')?'#d92b36':'#f2f3f4');}
 }else if(type==='road'||type==='bridge'||type==='tunnel'){
   node.width=Math.max(4,wAttr||10);node.depth=dAttr||node.width;node.height=node.height||(type==='bridge'?1.6:(type==='tunnel'?1:.15));node.worldPath=pts;node.worldPathY=pts.map(p=>apexWorldGroundY(p[0],p[1]));node.worldFootprint=polyFootprintFromWorldPath(pts,node.width);node.roofRiseM=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-roof-rise-m')))?parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-roof-rise-m')):null;node.clearance=Math.max(0,parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-clearance'))||0);
   node.roadKind=sourceRole.startsWith('city-road')?'CITY':(sourceRole==='service-road'?'SERVICE':'ROAD');node.pedestrianBridge=sourceRole==='pedestrian-bridge';
   if(type==='tunnel'){node.tunnelStyle=node.apexStyle||'tunnel_concrete';node.portalStyle=node.portalStyle||'portal_rectangular';node.interiorStyle=node.interiorStyle||'concrete_panels';}
   else if(type==='road')node.roadStyle=node.apexStyle||'road_standard';else if(type==='bridge')node.bridgeStyle=node.apexStyle||'bridge_concrete';
   node.color=node.color||(type==='road'?'#747c83':(type==='bridge'?'#98a1a8':'#7f878f'));
 }else if(type==='barrier'){
   const kindMap={'barrier-tecpro':'TECPRO','barrier-safer':'SAFER','barrier-tirewall':'TYRE','barrier-concrete':'CONCRETE','barrier-guardrail':'GUARDRAIL'};
   {const styleKind=String(node.apexStyle||'').replace(/^barrier_/,'').toUpperCase();node.barrierKind=kindMap[sourceRole]||String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-barrier')||styleKind||'CONCRETE').toUpperCase();}node.width=Math.max(.3,wAttr||.8);node.height=node.height||1.05;node.worldPath=pts;node.worldPathY=pts.map(p=>apexWorldGroundY(p[0],p[1]));node.collision=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-collision')||'true').toLowerCase()!=='false';node.grounded=true;
   const wcfg=(readStoredCircuitJson(ctx.spec&&ctx.spec.dir)||{}).walls||{};node.fence=el.hasAttribute('data-apex-fence')?String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-fence')).toLowerCase()!=='false':(wcfg.catchFence!==false&&['CONCRETE','CONCRETE_FENCE','GUARDRAIL','SAFER','SAFER_FENCE'].includes(node.barrierKind));node.fenceH=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-fence-height'))||(+wcfg.fenceHeightM||1.3);
 }else if(type==='grandstand'||type==='pit'){
   // v5.230.30 · La planta viene del dibujo SVG. length/depth se derivan sólo para LOD/culling.
   node.worldFootprint=pts;node.worldY=pts.map(p=>type==='pit'?apexAlignedCircuitSurfaceY(p[0],p[1]):apexWorldGroundY(p[0],p[1]));node.grounded=true;
   let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;for(const p of pts){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1])}
   const spanX=Math.max(1,maxX-minX),spanZ=Math.max(1,maxZ-minZ);node.length=lAttr||Math.max(spanX,spanZ);node.depth=dAttr||Math.min(spanX,spanZ);node.height=node.height||(type==='pit'?19:14);if(type==='pit'){node.pitStyle=node.apexStyle||'pit_modern_f1';node.garageBays=Math.max(1,Math.round(Number.isFinite(+node.garageBays)?+node.garageBays:Math.max(8,Math.min(36,node.length/9))));node.hasGlass=node.hasGlass!==false;node.hasBalcony=node.hasBalcony!==false;node.hasPitwall=node.hasPitwall!==false;node.roofStyle=node.roofStyle||'flat';node.garageStyle=node.garageStyle||'f1';}else{node.grandstandStyle=node.apexStyle||'grandstand_open';const gs=String(node.grandstandStyle||'grandstand_open').toLowerCase();if(node.hasRoof===null||node.hasRoof===undefined)node.hasRoof=/covered|roof|arena|vip/.test(gs);node.seatRows=Math.max(3,Math.round(Number.isFinite(+node.seatRows)?+node.seatRows:(/compact/.test(gs)?6:(node.height||14)>15?11:9)));node.roofStyle=node.roofStyle||'cantilever';node.seatColor=node.seatColor||'';}
 }
 else if(type==='ferris'){node.radius=(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-radius'))||12)*ctx.scale;node.height=node.height||0;node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='tree'){node.width=wAttr||6;node.height=node.height||12;node.foliage=node.color||'#3f7f4e';node.treeVariant=variant||'deciduous';node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='tower'){node.width=wAttr||8;node.height=node.height||48;node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='tunnelPortal'){node.span=wAttr||18;node.deckWidth=dAttr||26;node.height=node.height||6.2}
 else if(type==='gantry'){node.width=wAttr||20;node.span=node.width;node.height=node.height||6}
 else if(type==='billboard'){node.width=wAttr||16;node.span=node.width;node.height=node.height||4.2}
 else if(type==='flagPole'){node.height=node.height||24;node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='distanceBoard'){node.mark=parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-apex-mark'))||100;node.width=wAttr||2.9;node.depth=dAttr||1.1;node.height=node.height||4;node.latExtra=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-lat-extra'))||1.1;node.cornerS=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-corner-s')))?mod(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-corner-s'))*ctx.scale,ctx.km*1000):null;node.grounded=true}
 else {node.width=wAttr||12;node.depth=dAttr||10;node.height=node.height||8}
 return node;
}

function apexImportedCurbZones(landmarks,L){
 const out=[];if(!(L>0))return out;
 for(const it of landmarks||[]){const path=it&&(Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2?it.worldPath:(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=2?it.worldFootprint:null));if(!it||it.type!=='marking'||!['CURB_LEFT','CURB_RIGHT'].includes(it.markingKind)||!path)continue;
   let hint=null,unwrapped=[],last=null,acc=0;
   for(const p of path){const ns=nearestTrackState(p[0],p[1],hint,{radius:120,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:90});hint=ns.index;let ss=mod(ns.s,L);if(last!==null){let ds=ss-last;if(ds>L*.5)ds-=L;if(ds<-L*.5)ds+=L;acc+=ds}else acc=ss;unwrapped.push(acc);last=ss;}
   if(unwrapped.length<2)continue;let a=Math.min(...unwrapped),b=Math.max(...unwrapped);if(b-a<1.2)continue;
   out.push({side:it.markingKind==='CURB_LEFT'?'left':'right',startS:mod(a,L),endS:mod(b,L),lengthM:b-a,sourceId:it.sourceId||'',status:'SVG_AUTHORITATIVE'});
 }
 return out;
}
function apexCurbZoneContains(s,z,L){const a=mod(z.startS,L),b=mod(z.endS,L),x=mod(s,L);return a<=b?(x>=a&&x<=b):(x>=a||x<=b)}
function apexImportedStructureZones(landmarks,L){
 const zones=[];
 for(const it of (landmarks||[])){
   if(!it||!(it.type==='tunnel'||it.type==='bridge')||it.pedestrianBridge||!it.anchor)continue;
   const pts=Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length?it.worldPath:(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)?it.worldFootprint:[]);
   let center=Number.isFinite(it.anchor.s)?mod(it.anchor.s,L):0,minD=0,maxD=0;
   for(const p of pts){const ns=nearestTrackState(p[0],p[1],null,{radius:180,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:150}),d=wrapDeltaS(ns.s,center,L);minD=Math.min(minD,d);maxD=Math.max(maxD,d)}
   if(maxD-minD<8){minD=-8;maxD=8}
   zones.push({kind:it.type==='tunnel'?'UNDER':'OVER',scenery:it.type==='tunnel'?'SVG_TUNNEL':'SVG_BRIDGE',s0:mod(center+minD,L),s1:mod(center+maxD,L),name:it.name||it.type,sourceId:it.sourceId||'',width:it.width||0,height:it.height||0,roofRiseM:Number.isFinite(it.roofRiseM)?it.roofRiseM:null,style:it.type==='tunnel'?String(it.tunnelStyle||it.apexStyle||'tunnel_concrete').toLowerCase():String(it.bridgeStyle||it.apexStyle||'bridge_concrete').toLowerCase(),material:it.material||'',portalStyle:it.portalStyle||'',interiorStyle:it.interiorStyle||'',hasLights:it.hasLights,hasPanels:it.hasPanels,hasServiceWalk:it.hasServiceWalk,lightSpacingM:Number.isFinite(+it.lightSpacingM)?+it.lightSpacingM:null,panelSpacingM:Number.isFinite(+it.panelSpacingM)?+it.panelSpacingM:null,serviceWalkWidthM:Number.isFinite(+it.serviceWalkWidthM)?+it.serviceWalkWidthM:null,color:it.color||''});
 }
 return zones;
}

function apexGeoJsonOutlineToWorld(gj){
 const feat=gj&&Array.isArray(gj.features)?gj.features.find(f=>f&&f.properties&&f.properties.role==='outline'&&f.geometry&&f.geometry.type==='LineString'):null,coords=feat&&feat.geometry&&feat.geometry.coordinates;if(!Array.isArray(coords)||coords.length<20)throw Error('GeoJSON sin centerline utilizable');const clean=coords.filter(p=>Array.isArray(p)&&Number.isFinite(+p[0])&&Number.isFinite(+p[1]));if(clean.length<20)throw Error('GeoJSON insuficiente');const lon0=clean.reduce((a,p)=>a+(+p[0]),0)/clean.length,lat0=clean.reduce((a,p)=>a+(+p[1]),0)/clean.length,cos=Math.cos(lat0*Math.PI/180),pts=clean.map(p=>[(+p[0]-lon0)*111320*cos,(+p[1]-lat0)*110540]);if(pts.length>2&&Math.hypot(pts[0][0]-pts[pts.length-1][0],pts[0][1]-pts[pts.length-1][1])<2)pts.pop();return pts;
}
async function loadNonCalendarBaseTrack(spec,fallback){
 const jsonCfg=readStoredCircuitJson(spec.dir),jsonV=jsonCfg&&jsonCfg.verticalProfile?apexNormalizeVerticalProfile({verticalProfile:jsonCfg.verticalProfile}):null,jsonTiming=jsonCfg&&jsonCfg.timing?clonePlain(jsonCfg.timing):null,jsonBanking=apexNormalizeAuthoredBanking(jsonCfg),cacheKey='apex-v523026-extra-geometry-'+spec.dir;
 try{const c=JSON.parse(appStorage.getItem(cacheKey)||'null');if(c&&Array.isArray(c.pts)&&c.pts.length>20)return{...c,km:fallback.km||c.km,direction:fallback.direction||c.direction,turns:fallback.turns||c.turns||0,laps:Number.isFinite(fallback.laps)?fallback.laps:(c.laps||0),verticalProfile:jsonV,timingData:jsonTiming,authoredBanking:jsonBanking}}catch(e){}
 const finish=data=>{try{appStorage.setItem(cacheKey,JSON.stringify({...data,verticalProfile:null,timingData:null}))}catch(e){}return data};
 if(spec.externalSource==='generated-oval'){
   const km=4.023,direction='COUNTER_CLOCKWISE',layout=fallback.layout||'daytona-oval',turns=4,laps=200,circuitType='OVAL';
   const pts=[];const rx=682,rz=402,frontWarp=.12,backFlat=.04,n=560;
   for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const t=(i/n)*Math.PI*2;let x=Math.cos(t)*rx,z=Math.sin(t)*rz;const f=(1-Math.cos(t))*0.5; x*=1+frontWarp*Math.max(0,Math.sin(t)); z*=1-backFlat*Math.cos(t)*Math.cos(t); if(z>0)x+=Math.sin((x/rx)*Math.PI)*88; pts.push([x,z]);}
   return finish({pts,km,layout,direction,turns,laps,circuitType,startAligned:true,detailedSvg:true,geometrySource:'GENERATED-DAYTONA-OVAL',geometryAudit:'v5.230.26-extra',verticalProfile:jsonV,timingData:jsonTiming,authoredBanking:jsonBanking});
 }
 if(spec.externalSource==='f1-history'){
   const layout=fallback.layout||spec.dir.replace(/^x-/,'');const url=`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/julesr0y/f1-circuits-svg/main/circuits/minimal/black-outline/${layout}.svg`,r=await fetch(url,{cache:'force-cache'});if(!r.ok)throw Error('No se pudo descargar el trazado histórico');const svg=await r.text(),parsed=parseSvgGeometry(svg,fallback.km||5,fallback.direction||'CLOCKWISE',null);return finish({pts:parsed.pts,km:fallback.km||5,layout,direction:fallback.direction||'CLOCKWISE',turns:fallback.turns||0,laps:fallback.laps||0,circuitType:fallback.circuitType||'HISTORIC_F1',startAligned:parsed.startAligned,detailedSvg:false,geometrySource:'F1-CIRCUITS-SVG-HISTORIC',geometryAudit:'v5.230.26-extra',verticalProfile:jsonV,timingData:jsonTiming,authoredBanking:jsonBanking});
 }
 if(spec.externalSource==='track-atlas-geojson'){
   const url='https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tobi/track-atlas/main/tracks/circuit-de-la-sarthe/raw/layers/24h.geojson',r=await fetch(url,{cache:'force-cache'});if(!r.ok)throw Error('No se pudo descargar Circuit de la Sarthe');const gj=await r.json(),pts=apexGeoJsonOutlineToWorld(gj);return finish({pts,km:fallback.km||13.626,layout:fallback.layout||'24h',direction:fallback.direction||'CLOCKWISE',turns:fallback.turns||0,laps:fallback.laps||0,circuitType:fallback.circuitType||'ENDURANCE',startAligned:true,detailedSvg:true,geometrySource:'TRACK-ATLAS-GEOJSON',geometryAudit:'v5.230.26-extra',verticalProfile:jsonV,timingData:jsonTiming,authoredBanking:jsonBanking});
 }
 if(spec.nonCalendar)throw Error('Este circuito necesita un SVG importado antes de salir a pista');
 return null;
}
async function loadTrack(spec){
 await apexHydrateLargeCircuit(spec.dir);
 const fallback=raceMetaFor(spec)||{},jsonCfg=readStoredCircuitJson(spec.dir),jsonVertical=jsonCfg&&jsonCfg.verticalProfile?apexNormalizeVerticalProfile({verticalProfile:jsonCfg.verticalProfile}):null,jsonTiming=jsonCfg&&jsonCfg.timing?clonePlain(jsonCfg.timing):null,jsonBanking=apexNormalizeAuthoredBanking(jsonCfg);
 let customSvg=readStoredCustomTrackSvg(spec.dir);
 if(customSvg){
   // v5.230.10: si el SVG autoritativo ya estaba guardado por una versión anterior,
   // regeneramos automáticamente muros/pit con el nuevo parser exacto. No obliga al
   // usuario a volver a importar manualmente el mismo archivo.
   if(customSvg.authoritative===true&&typeof customSvg.rawSvg==='string'&&customSvg.rawSvg.trim()){
     const wp=readStoredCustomWorldProfile(spec.dir);
     if(!wp||wp.profileRevision<23045){try{await importEditableCircuitSvgText(customSvg.rawSvg,spec)}catch(e){console.warn('APEX: no se pudo migrar geometría SVG exacta',e)}}
   }
   const km=parseFloat(customSvg.km)||fallback.km||5.0,direction=String(customSvg.direction||fallback.direction||"CLOCKWISE").trim(),layoutId=String(customSvg.layoutId||fallback.layout||spec.dir||"custom-track").trim();
   if(Array.isArray(customSvg.points)&&customSvg.points.length>20){
     return {pts:customSvg.points.map(p=>[+p[0],+p[1]]),km,layout:layoutId,direction,turns:parseInt(customSvg.turns)||fallback.turns||0,laps:parseInt(customSvg.laps)||fallback.laps||0,circuitType:customSvg.circuitType||fallback.circuitType||"RACE",startAligned:true,detailedSvg:true,geometrySource:"USER_IMPORTED_EDITABLE_SVG_EXACT",geometryAudit:"v5.230.10-exact-svg",svgAuthoritative:customSvg.authoritative===true||customSvg.source==="EXACT_USER_SVG",verticalProfile:jsonVertical||customSvg.verticalProfile||null,timingData:customSvg.timingData||jsonTiming||null,timingControls:Array.isArray(customSvg.timingControls)&&customSvg.timingControls.length?customSvg.timingControls:(jsonTiming&&Array.isArray(jsonTiming.controls)?jsonTiming.controls:[]),cornerMarkers:Array.isArray(customSvg.cornerMarkers)?customSvg.cornerMarkers:[],authoredBanking:jsonBanking};
   }
   const startHint=customSvg.startHint&&Number.isFinite(customSvg.startHint.x)&&Number.isFinite(customSvg.startHint.y)?{x:+customSvg.startHint.x,y:+customSvg.startHint.y}:null;
   const svg=`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="${customSvg.trackPathD}"/></svg>`;
   const parsed=parseSvgGeometry(svg,km,direction,startHint),data={pts:parsed.pts,km,layout:layoutId,direction,turns:parseInt(customSvg.turns)||fallback.turns||0,laps:parseInt(customSvg.laps)||fallback.laps||0,circuitType:customSvg.circuitType||fallback.circuitType||"RACE",startAligned:parsed.startAligned,detailedSvg:true,geometrySource:"USER_IMPORTED_EDITABLE_SVG",geometryAudit:"v5.230.26-custom-svg",authoredBanking:jsonBanking};
   return data;
 }
 // BASE interna: si no hay override SVG, se usa el SVG base del registro.
 // La selección EMBEDDED/F1DB es genérica y no depende de un ID de circuito concreto.
 const embeddedBaseSvg=apexEmbeddedBaseSvgText(spec.dir);
 if(embeddedBaseSvg){
   let baseGeom=readBaseTrackSvg(spec.dir);
   if(!baseGeom){await importEditableCircuitSvgText(embeddedBaseSvg,spec,{persist:false,base:true});baseGeom=readBaseTrackSvg(spec.dir)}
   if(baseGeom&&Array.isArray(baseGeom.points)&&baseGeom.points.length>20){
     const km=parseFloat(baseGeom.km)||fallback.km||5.0,direction=String(baseGeom.direction||fallback.direction||'CLOCKWISE').trim(),layoutId=String(baseGeom.layoutId||fallback.layout||spec.dir).trim();
     return {pts:baseGeom.points.map(p=>[+p[0],+p[1]]),km,layout:layoutId,direction,turns:parseInt(baseGeom.turns)||fallback.turns||0,laps:parseInt(baseGeom.laps)||fallback.laps||0,circuitType:baseGeom.circuitType||fallback.circuitType||'RACE',startAligned:true,detailedSvg:true,geometrySource:'APEX_BASE_EMBEDDED_SVG',geometryAudit:'v5.230.62.2-sector-lod-base',svgAuthoritative:true,verticalProfile:jsonVertical||baseGeom.verticalProfile||null,timingData:baseGeom.timingData||jsonTiming||null,timingControls:Array.isArray(baseGeom.timingControls)&&baseGeom.timingControls.length?baseGeom.timingControls:(jsonTiming&&Array.isArray(jsonTiming.controls)?jsonTiming.controls:[]),cornerMarkers:Array.isArray(baseGeom.cornerMarkers)?baseGeom.cornerMarkers:[],authoredBanking:jsonBanking};
   }
 }
 if(spec.nonCalendar){const x=await loadNonCalendarBaseTrack(spec,fallback);if(x)return x}
 if(jsonCfg&&jsonCfg.track&&jsonCfg.track.requiresSvg===true)throw Error("Este circuito requiere importar su SVG antes de salir a pista");
 const key="apex-v5183-geometry-audit-"+spec.dir;
 try{let c=JSON.parse(appStorage.getItem(key));if(c&&c.pts&&c.pts.length>400)return c}catch(e){}
 const base="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/f1db/f1db/main/";
 const race=await fetch(base+"src/data/seasons/2026/races/"+spec.dir+"/race.yml",{cache:"force-cache"}).then(r=>{if(!r.ok)throw Error("No se pudo descargar la ficha del circuito");return r.text()});
 const layout=(race.match(/^circuitLayoutId:\s*([^\r\n]+)/m)||[])[1]||fallback.layout,km=parseFloat((race.match(/^courseLength:\s*([\d.]+)/m)||[])[1])||fallback.km,direction=(race.match(/^direction:\s*([A-Z_]+)/m)||[])[1]||fallback.direction||"CLOCKWISE",turns=parseInt((race.match(/^turns:\s*(\d+)/m)||[])[1])||fallback.turns||0,laps=parseInt((race.match(/^scheduledLaps:\s*(\d+)/m)||[])[1])||parseInt((race.match(/^laps:\s*(\d+)/m)||[])[1])||fallback.laps||0,circuitType=(race.match(/^circuitType:\s*([A-Z_]+)/m)||[])[1]||"RACE";
 if(!layout||!km)throw Error("Datos del circuito incompletos");const layoutId=String(layout).trim();let svg='',detailed=false;
 try{const u="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/f1db/f1-circuits-svg/main/circuits/detailed/black-outline/"+layoutId+".svg";const r=await fetch(u,{cache:"force-cache"});if(!r.ok)throw Error("sin SVG detallado");svg=await r.text();detailed=true}catch(e){svg=await fetch(base+"src/assets/circuits/black-outline/"+layoutId+".svg",{cache:"force-cache"}).then(r=>{if(!r.ok)throw Error("No se pudo descargar el trazado SVG");return r.text()})}
 const parsed=parseSvgGeometry(svg,km,direction,null),data={pts:parsed.pts,km,layout:layoutId,direction,turns,laps,circuitType,startAligned:parsed.startAligned,detailedSvg:detailed,geometrySource:detailed?"F1-CIRCUITS-SVG-DETAILED":"F1DB-OUTLINE",geometryAudit:"v5.183",verticalProfile:jsonVertical,timingData:jsonTiming,authoredBanking:jsonBanking};try{appStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(data))}catch(e){}return data;
}

function segmentIntersection2D(a,b,c,d){
 const rx=b[0]-a[0],rz=b[1]-a[1],sx=d[0]-c[0],sz=d[1]-c[1];
 const den=rx*sz-rz*sx;if(Math.abs(den)<1e-9)return null;
 const qx=c[0]-a[0],qz=c[1]-a[1],t=(qx*sz-qz*sx)/den,u=(qx*rz-qz*rx)/den;
 if(t<=.001||t>=.999||u<=.001||u>=.999)return null;
 return {t,u,x:a[0]+rx*t,z:a[1]+rz*t};
}
function apexDetectGradeCrossing(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||apexCircuitVisualFeature('autoGradeCrossing',false)!==true)return null;
 const P=trackData.pts,n=P.length,L=trackData.km*1000,step=L/n;
 let best=null;
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const a=P[i],b=P[(i+1)%n];
   for(let j=i+28;j<n;j++){
     if(j-i>n-28)continue;
     const c=P[j],d=P[(j+1)%n],hit=segmentIntersection2D(a,b,c,d);if(!hit)continue;
     const sA=mod((i+hit.t)*step,L),sB=mod((j+hit.u)*step,L),pathSep=Math.abs(sA-sB);
     if(pathSep<L*.18||pathSep>L*.82)continue;
     const ax=b[0]-a[0],az=b[1]-a[1],bx=d[0]-c[0],bz=d[1]-c[1],al=Math.hypot(ax,az)||1,bl=Math.hypot(bx,bz)||1;
     const crossAngle=Math.abs((ax/al)*(bz/bl)-(az/al)*(bx/bl));
     if(crossAngle<.28)continue;
     const underS=Math.min(sA,sB),overS=Math.max(sA,sB);
     const separation=Math.min(pathSep,L-pathSep)/L;
     const score=crossAngle*2.6+separation*.42;
     if(!best||score>best.score)best={score,underS,overS,x:hit.x,z:hit.z};
   }
 }
 if(!best)return null;
 return {underS:best.underS,overS:best.overS,x:best.x,z:best.z,underHalf:18,overHalf:20};
}
function apexGradeCrossingRole(s){
 const c=trackData&&trackData.gradeCrossing;if(!c||!trackData)return"";
 const L=trackData.km*1000,du=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(s,c.underS,L)),dv=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(s,c.overS,L));
 if(du<=c.underHalf)return"UNDER";
 if(dv<=c.overHalf)return"OVER";
 return"";
}
function wrapRangeContains(s,start,end,L){
 start=mod(start,L);end=mod(end,L);s=mod(s,L);
 return start<=end ? (s>=start&&s<=end) : (s>=start||s<=end);
}
function authoredStructureZoneAtS(s){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.customWorld)return null;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,zones=Array.isArray(trackData.customWorld.structureZones)?trackData.customWorld.structureZones:[];
 for(const z of zones){if(z&&wrapRangeContains(s,z.s0,z.s1,L))return z}
 return null;
}
function prepareTrackModel(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||trackData.pts.length<5)return;
 // v5.226.3: el SVG editable se lee/parsea UNA VEZ al cargar el circuito.
 // Nada de localStorage ni JSON.parse debe ejecutarse durante un frame.
 trackData.customWorld=trackData.spec?readEffectiveWorldProfile(trackData.spec.dir):null;
 // v5.230.13: migra automáticamente redes PIT creadas con versiones anteriores.
 // tenía dos subpaths que cruzaban el origen de vuelta y el V1 los concatenaba en el
 // orden textual equivocado. No hace falta reimportar el SVG para probar esta versión.
 if(trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork){
   const oldPn=trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork,newPn=apexMigratePitNetworkV2(oldPn);
   if(newPn!==oldPn)trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork=newPn;
 }
 if(trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.authoritative&&trackData.customWorld.profileRevision<23082){trackData.customWorld.structureZones=apexImportedStructureZones(trackData.customWorld.landmarks||[],trackData.km*1000);trackData.customWorld.curbZones=apexImportedCurbZones(trackData.customWorld.landmarks||[],trackData.km*1000);trackData.customWorld.profileRevision=23082;}
 trackData.svgAuthoritative=trackData.svgAuthoritative===true||!!(trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.authoritative);
 const P=trackData.pts,n=P.length,L=trackData.km*1000,step=L/n;
 const curve=new Float32Array(n),safe=new Float32Array(n);
 let mnx=Infinity,mxx=-Infinity,mnz=Infinity,mxz=-Infinity;
 for(const p of P){mnx=Math.min(mnx,p[0]);mxx=Math.max(mxx,p[0]);mnz=Math.min(mnz,p[1]);mxz=Math.max(mxz,p[1])}
 const ang=[];
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const a=P[(i-1+n)%n],b=P[(i+1)%n];
   ang[i]=Math.atan2(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);
 }
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   let d=angleDiff(ang[(i+2)%n],ang[(i-2+n)%n])/(step*4);
   curve[i]=d;
 }
 // suavizado corto para evitar picos del SVG.
 const sm=new Float32Array(n);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   let s=0,w=0;
   for(let k=-2;k<=2;k++){const ww=3-Math.abs(k);s+=curve[(i+k+n)%n]*ww;w+=ww}
   sm[i]=s/w;
   const kk=Math.abs(sm[i]);
   safe[i]=clamp(96/Math.sqrt(1+kk*1750),34,98);
 }
 delete trackData._continuousAutoBank;delete trackData._continuousRoadS;delete trackData._inspectionRoadS;delete trackData._inspectionSurfaceIndex;
 trackData.model={curve:sm,safe,step,mini:{mnx,mxx,mnz,mxz}};
 // v5.230.16: todas las pistas tienen referencias de curva Tn + zona geométrica inicio/fin.
 apexPrepareCornerReferenceModel();
 // v5.230.62.8.6.7: resuelve controles de cronometraje JSON anclados a curva (FIA: antes/después de Tn). SVG autoritativo sigue teniendo prioridad.
 {const authored=Array.isArray(trackData.timingControls)?trackData.timingControls:[],src=authored.length?authored:apexDefaultTimingControls(trackData.spec);trackData.timingControls=src.map(c=>{const s=apexTimingControlResolvedS(c);return Number.isFinite(s)?{...c,s}:null}).filter(Boolean);const derivedSectors=apexTimingSectorsFromControls(trackData.spec);trackData.timingData={...(trackData.timingData||{}),controlSchema:'APEX_TIMING_CONTROLS_V5_POINT_CROSSING',sectors:derivedSectors.length?derivedSectors:((trackData.timingData&&trackData.timingData.sectors)||[])};}
 // v5.230.12: sólo para PIT. Si no hay pitlane-centerline importado, construimos una
 // red provisional desde raceMeta. No se guarda en localStorage: al exportar se convierte
 // en SVG editable y al reimportar el SVG pasa a tener prioridad absoluta.
 apexEnsurePitFallback();
 // El perfil JSON complementa la geometría SVG del pit: Y, anchura variable y slots de box.
 apexApplyPitLaneJsonProfile();
 trackData.gradeCrossing=apexDetectGradeCrossing();
 // La misma malla de terreno se crea antes de apoyar los viales.
 let apexRoadTerrainGrid=null;
 if(trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.terrainBase)apexRoadTerrainGrid=apexPrepareTerrainPlateGrid();
 else if(trackData.customWorld&&Array.isArray(trackData.customWorld.terrainContours)&&trackData.customWorld.terrainContours.length)apexPrepareTerrainContourGrid();
 // v5.230.56 · Los viales largos ya no heredan una única cota del punto de anclaje
 // ni excavan el suelo. Cada vértice sigue la superficie natural del SVG. Esto
 // permite que una carretera pase SOBRE un túnel sin convertir el terreno en vacío.
 if(trackData.customWorld){
  for(const it of trackData.customWorld.landmarks||[])if(it&&it.type==='road'&&Array.isArray(it.worldPath)){
   it.worldPathY=it.worldPath.map(p=>{
    let y=null;
    if(apexRoadTerrainGrid&&apexRoadTerrainGrid.rawVals)y=apexTerrainRawInterpolated(apexRoadTerrainGrid,p[0],p[1]);
    if(!Number.isFinite(y))y=apexProceduralTerrainHeightAtWorld(p[0],p[1]);
    return finite(y,0)+.035;
   });
  }
  // El índice había podido construirse al preparar la malla con alturas antiguas.
  delete trackData.customWorld._apexGradeIndex;
 }
 // v5.213 · Referencia de conducción continua. Se construye UNA VEZ al cargar el GP:
 // sanea nodos poligonales para el AUT, genera una trazada limpia y precalcula su
 // curvatura/envelope. No modifica el SVG, los muros ni la geometría visual del circuito.
 prepareDriverReferenceModel();
 trackData.world=buildCircuitWorldProfile();
 apexRegroundImportedWorld();
 {const sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec),sectors=apexTimingSectorControls(trackData.spec);trackData.checkpoints=sectors.map(c=>c.s);trackData.sectorControls=sectors;trackData.operationalControls=apexOperationalControls(trackData.spec);trackData.timingReference={startS:sf.start,finishS:sf.finish,pathOriginIsTimingLine:false};}
 apexPrepareStraightModeZones();
}
async function apexCircuitRuntimeSelfTest(){
 const savedTrackData=trackData,rows=[];try{for(const spec of calendar.slice(0,F1_SEASON_COUNT)){try{const td=await loadTrack(spec);trackData={...td,spec};prepareTrackModel();const sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(spec),mp=apexMapPresentation(spec),preview=trackPreviewSvgFromPoints(trackData.pts,'#59f3ff',spec.dir,trackData.cornerMarkers,trackData.km,(trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork.runs)||[],sf);rows.push({id:spec.dir,ok:!!(trackData.pts&&trackData.pts.length>20&&preview&&preview.includes('<svg')),points:trackData.pts.length,startS:sf.start,finishS:sf.finish,sectors:(trackData.sectorControls||[]).length,straightMode:(trackData.straightMode&& (trackData.straightMode.status||trackData.straightMode.quality))||null,mapPresentation:mp,legacyTimingFallback:!!trackData.timingLegacyFallback});}catch(e){rows.push({id:spec.dir,ok:false,error:String(e&&e.stack||e)});}}}finally{trackData=savedTrackData;}return{ok:rows.length===F1_SEASON_COUNT&&rows.every(r=>r.ok&&!r.legacyTimingFallback),count:rows.length,rows};
}
window.apexCircuitRuntimeSelfTest=apexCircuitRuntimeSelfTest;
async function apexLapEngineSelfTest(){
 const saved={trackData,player,rivals,currentSession,raceT,lapStart,best,sessionLapLog,sessionExpired,sessionFastestLap,mode};const rows=[];try{for(const spec of calendar.slice(0,F1_SEASON_COUNT)){try{const td=await loadTrack(spec);trackData={...td,spec};prepareTrackModel();const g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(spec,99),L=g.L,eps=Math.min(2,L*.0002);currentSession='race';mode='race';sessionExpired=false;raceT=0;lapStart=0;best=1e9;sessionLapLog=[];sessionFastestLap={id:null,time:Infinity,flashUntil:0};player={laps:99,lap:0,crossedStart:false,raceFirstLapPending:false,checkpoint:0,trackDirDot:1,speed:72,autoUsedThisLap:false,lastLapAutoInvalid:false,currentLapTrace:[],lapTraceLastBin:-1,lapTraceLastTime:0,lastCompletedLap:0,timingLapStart:0,timingSectorIndex:0,timingSectorSplits:[],timingSectorTimes:[],timingSpeedTrapKph:null};rivals=[];apexArmRaceGridTimingCar(player,true);
 let t=1;for(let c=0;c<g.firstLapControlCrossings;c++){raceT=t+=90;updateLapValidation(mod(g.finish-eps,L),mod(g.finish+eps,L),0,0);}player.autoUsedThisLap=true;raceT=t+=90;updateLapValidation(mod(g.finish-eps,L),mod(g.finish+eps,L),0,0);const pOk=player.lap===2&&player.lastCompletedLap===2&&sessionLapLog.some(x=>x&&x.lap===1&&x.timingGate==='FIA_GRID_START_TO_CONTROL_LINE')&&sessionLapLog.some(x=>x&&x.lap===2&&x.timingGate==='FINISH_S_LINE_CROSSING');
 const rv={id:1,s:mod(g.start-eps,L),speed:70,finished:false,finishTime:null,crossedStart:false,raceFirstLapPending:false,lapStartTime:0,completedLaps:0,lastLap:Infinity,bestLap:Infinity,checkpoint:0,timingLapStart:0,timingSectorIndex:0,timingSectorSplits:[],timingSectorTimes:[],timingSpeedTrapKph:null};rivals=[rv];raceT=0;apexArmRaceGridTimingCar(rv,false);t=1;for(let c=0;c<g.firstLapControlCrossings;c++){raceT=t+=90;updateRivalLapTiming(rv,mod(g.finish-eps,L),mod(g.finish+eps,L),L);}raceT=t+=90;updateRivalLapTiming(rv,mod(g.finish-eps,L),mod(g.finish+eps,L),L);const rOk=rv.completedLaps===2&&Number.isFinite(rv.lastLap)&&rv.lastLap>=20;rows.push({id:spec.dir,ok:pOk&&rOk,playerLap:player.lap,playerLastCompleted:player.lastCompletedLap,rivalLaps:rv.completedLaps,rivalLastLap:rv.lastLap,startS:g.start,finishS:g.finish,controlRelation:g.controlRelation,firstLapControlCrossings:g.firstLapControlCrossings,firstFinishDistance:g.firstFinishDistance,raceDistance:g.raceDistance});}catch(e){rows.push({id:spec.dir,ok:false,error:String(e&&e.stack||e)});}}}finally{trackData=saved.trackData;player=saved.player;rivals=saved.rivals;currentSession=saved.currentSession;raceT=saved.raceT;lapStart=saved.lapStart;best=saved.best;sessionLapLog=saved.sessionLapLog;sessionExpired=saved.sessionExpired;sessionFastestLap=saved.sessionFastestLap;mode=saved.mode;}return{ok:rows.length===F1_SEASON_COUNT&&rows.every(r=>r.ok),count:rows.length,rule:'FIA_ISC_8.4.5_GRID_START_SIGNAL_AND_CONTROL_LINE',rows};
}
window.apexLapEngineSelfTest=apexLapEngineSelfTest;
function sampleModel(arr,s){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.model||!arr)return null;
 const n=arr.length,L=trackData.km*1000,f=mod(s,L)/trackData.model.step,i=Math.floor(f),t=f-i;
 return lerp(arr[i%n],arr[(i+1)%n],t);
}
// v5.213 · Modelo de referencia continuo para conducción automática.
// Motivo: varias telemetrías mostraron dos fallos comunes:
// 1) nodos crudos con saltos de tangente (Austin llegó a ~89°), y
// 2) velocidad calculada sobre el eje mientras el coche seguía una trazada lateral distinta.
// Este modelo NO deforma la pista física ni visual: sólo crea una trayectoria mundial suave
// dentro de la anchura útil, con curvatura y velocidad coherentes con ESA trayectoria.
function driverModelSample(arr,s){
 const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;if(!d||!arr||!arr.length)return null;
 const n=arr.length,L=trackData.km*1000,f=mod(s,L)/d.step,i=Math.floor(f),t=f-i;
 return lerp(arr[mod(i,n)],arr[mod(i+1,n)],t);
}
function driverModelAngleSample(arr,s){
 const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;if(!d||!arr||!arr.length)return null;
 const n=arr.length,L=trackData.km*1000,f=mod(s,L)/d.step,i=Math.floor(f),t=f-i;
 const a=arr[mod(i,n)],b=arr[mod(i+1,n)];return a+angleDiff(b,a)*t;
}
function driverPhysicalSpeedFromCurvature(kSigned,s,safety=1){
 const k=Math.abs(finite(kSigned,0));if(k<.00042)return 99;
 const wet=apexWeatherIsWet(),M=800,G=9.81,mu=wet?1.27:1.90,bank=finite(bankAt(s),0);
 const assist=(Math.sign(kSigned||1)===Math.sign(bank||kSigned||1)?1:-.42)*G*Math.sin(Math.abs(bank));
 const sf=(wet?.925:.985)*finite(safety,1);let lo=12,hi=99;
 for(let j=0;j<14;j++){
   const v=(lo+hi)*.5,down=Math.min(38000,2.72*v*v),normal=M*G*Math.cos(bank)+down;
   const latAccel=Math.max(2.5,mu*normal/M*sf+assist),maxSteer=.285/(1+v*.0105),needSteer=Math.atan(3.60*k);
   if(v*v*k<=latAccel&&needSteer<=maxSteer*.985)lo=v;else hi=v;
 }
 return clamp(lo,14,99);
}
function prepareDriverReferenceModel(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||trackData.pts.length<24||!trackData.model)return null;
 const P=trackData.pts,n=P.length,L=trackData.km*1000,step=L/n,idx=i=>mod(i,n);
 let cx=new Float32Array(n),cz=new Float32Array(n);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){cx[i]=P[i][0];cz[i]=P[i][1];}
 // Laplaciano corto. Cuatro pasadas redondean el vértice matemático sin convertir
 // las curvas normales en otra pista. El desplazamiento se limita después por anchura.
 for(let pass=0;pass<4;pass++){
   const nx=new Float32Array(n),nz=new Float32Array(n),a=pass<2?.235:.185;
   for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
     const im=idx(i-1),ip=idx(i+1);
     nx[i]=cx[i]+a*(cx[im]+cx[ip]-2*cx[i]);nz[i]=cz[i]+a*(cz[im]+cz[ip]-2*cz[i]);
   }
   cx=nx;cz=nz;
 }
 // Impide que el saneado corte fuera de la cinta. Los nodos realmente duros pueden
 // moverse algo más porque precisamente necesitan radio; los demás apenas cambian.
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const im=idx(i-1),ip=idx(i+1),p=P[i],pa=P[im],pb=P[ip];
   let tx=pb[0]-pa[0],tz=pb[1]-pa[1],tl=Math.hypot(tx,tz)||1;tx/=tl;tz/=tl;
   const h0=Math.atan2(p[0]-pa[0],p[1]-pa[1]),h1=Math.atan2(pb[0]-p[0],pb[1]-p[1]),jump=Math.abs(angleDiff(h1,h0));
   const half=Math.max(4,trackRoadWidthAt(i*step)*.5),maxMove=Math.min(Math.max(1.15,half-1.55),jump>.30?3.25:jump>.16?2.20:1.45);
   let dx=cx[i]-p[0],dz=cz[i]-p[1],dl=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
   if(dl>maxMove){dx*=maxMove/dl;dz*=maxMove/dl;cx[i]=p[0]+dx;cz[i]=p[1]+dz;}
 }
 const ch=new Float32Array(n),cc=new Float32Array(n);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const a=idx(i-2),b=idx(i+2);ch[i]=Math.atan2(cx[b]-cx[a],cz[b]-cz[a]);
 }
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++)cc[i]=angleDiff(ch[idx(i+2)],ch[idx(i-2)])/(step*4);
 // Curvatura provisional suavizada: ésta es la que entiende el planner, no la tangente cruda.
 let centerCurve=cc;
 for(let pass=0;pass<2;pass++){
   const q=new Float32Array(n);for(let i=0;i<n;i++)q[i]=(centerCurve[idx(i-1)]+2*centerCurve[i]+centerCurve[idx(i+1)])/4;centerCurve=q;
 }
 // Trazada limpia exterior->ápice->salida, calculada en mundo y suavizada como señal.
 // No se recompone cada frame, por lo que además resulta más barata que perseguir targets erráticos.
 let lane=new Float32Array(n);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const s0=i*step,half=Math.max(4,trackRoadWidthAt(s0)*.5),limit=clamp(half-1.55,1.15,3.45);
   let bestA=0,bestD=999,bestK=0;
   const maxJ=Math.max(8,Math.ceil(150/step));
   for(let j=0;j<=maxJ;j++){
     const k=centerCurve[idx(i+j)],a=Math.abs(k),d=j*step,score=a*(1-d/250);
     if(a>.00115&&score>bestA){bestA=score;bestD=d;bestK=k;}
   }
   if(!bestK){lane[i]=0;continue;}
   const sg=Math.sign(bestK)||1,outer=limit*.70,apex=limit*.61;
   let v;
   if(bestD>92)v=sg*outer;
   else if(bestD>15){let t=clamp((92-bestD)/77,0,1);t=t*t*(3-2*t);v=lerp(sg*outer,-sg*apex,t);}
   else v=-sg*apex;
   // En enlazadas cercanas sacrificamos amplitud para no cruzar la pista entre vértices.
   let opposite=false;
   for(let j=2;j<=Math.ceil(62/step);j++){const k=centerCurve[idx(i+j)];if(Math.abs(k)>.0023&&Math.sign(k)!==sg){opposite=true;break;}}
   if(opposite)v*=.58;
   const env=envProfile(s0),wallish=apexWallishAtS(s0,env);if(wallish)v*=.68;
   lane[i]=clamp(v,-limit,limit);
 }
 for(let pass=0;pass<7;pass++){
   const q=new Float32Array(n);for(let i=0;i<n;i++)q[i]=lane[idx(i-1)]*.22+lane[i]*.56+lane[idx(i+1)]*.22;lane=q;
 }
 // Límite de pendiente lateral: evita cambios de calle que generen su propia curvatura artificial.
 const maxSlope=.038,maxD=maxSlope*step;
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
   for(let i=1;i<n;i++)lane[i]=clamp(lane[i],lane[i-1]-maxD,lane[i-1]+maxD);
   for(let i=n-2;i>=0;i--)lane[i]=clamp(lane[i],lane[i+1]-maxD,lane[i+1]+maxD);
 }
 // v5.214 · PATH C2. El error de v5.213 era construir la trazada final de nuevo
 // sobre la normal CRUDA de P[i]. En un circuito importado, al llegar al micro-kink de ~46°, esa
 // normal podía girar bruscamente y `baseLat + lane` saltaba más de 1 m en ~7 m aun
 // cuando `lane` sí estaba limitado. Ahora la trayectoria nace del centro saneado y
 // de SU tangente suave; después se relaja en mundo y sólo se limita a permanecer
 // dentro de un tubo seguro alrededor de la cinta original.
 let px=new Float32Array(n),pz=new Float32Array(n),plat=new Float32Array(n);
 const pathSafe=new Float32Array(n);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const tx=Math.sin(ch[i]),tz=Math.cos(ch[i]),nx=-tz,nz=tx,env=envProfile(i*step);
   const safe=Math.max(1.0,env.half-(apexWallishAtS(i*step,env)?1.85:1.48));
   pathSafe[i]=safe;
   let x=cx[i]+nx*clamp(lane[i],-safe,safe),z=cz[i]+nz*clamp(lane[i],-safe,safe);
   // El círculo es deliberadamente más conservador que el ancho de pista. No pretende
   // redefinir la geometría: sólo impide que la relajación corte fuera del ribbon.
   let dx=x-P[i][0],dz=z-P[i][1],dl=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
   if(dl>safe){dx*=safe/dl;dz*=safe/dl;x=P[i][0]+dx;z=P[i][1]+dz;}
   px[i]=x;pz[i]=z;
 }
 // Relajación en coordenadas de mundo. Diez pasadas pequeñas producen continuidad de
 // heading/curvatura sin redondear en exceso las curvas normales.
 for(let pass=0;pass<10;pass++){
   const qx=new Float32Array(n),qz=new Float32Array(n),a=pass<4?.19:.135;
   for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
     const im=idx(i-1),ip=idx(i+1),p=P[i];
     let x=px[i]+a*(px[im]+px[ip]-2*px[i]),z=pz[i]+a*(pz[im]+pz[ip]-2*pz[i]);
     let dx=x-p[0],dz=z-p[1],dl=Math.hypot(dx,dz),safe=pathSafe[i];
     if(dl>safe){dx*=safe/dl;dz*=safe/dl;x=p[0]+dx;z=p[1]+dz;}
     qx[i]=x;qz[i]=z;
   }
   px=qx;pz=qz;
 }
 // Señal lateral estable respecto al centro saneado. Limitamos también esta salida, no
 // sólo la señal `lane` previa, para que ningún consumidor Frenet vea un salto lateral.
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const tx=Math.sin(ch[i]),tz=Math.cos(ch[i]),nx=-tz,nz=tx;plat[i]=(px[i]-cx[i])*nx+(pz[i]-cz[i])*nz;}
 const finalMaxD=.042*step;
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
   for(let i=1;i<n;i++)plat[i]=clamp(plat[i],plat[i-1]-finalMaxD,plat[i-1]+finalMaxD);
   for(let i=n-2;i>=0;i--)plat[i]=clamp(plat[i],plat[i+1]-finalMaxD,plat[i+1]+finalMaxD);
 }
 // Reconstrucción final sobre el marco SUAVE y una última relajación muy ligera.
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const tx=Math.sin(ch[i]),tz=Math.cos(ch[i]),nx=-tz,nz=tx,lat=clamp(plat[i],-pathSafe[i],pathSafe[i]);px[i]=cx[i]+nx*lat;pz[i]=cz[i]+nz*lat;}
 for(let pass=0;pass<4;pass++){
   const qx=new Float32Array(n),qz=new Float32Array(n);
   for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const im=idx(i-1),ip=idx(i+1),p=P[i];let x=px[i]+.11*(px[im]+px[ip]-2*px[i]),z=pz[i]+.11*(pz[im]+pz[ip]-2*pz[i]);let dx=x-p[0],dz=z-p[1],dl=Math.hypot(dx,dz),safe=pathSafe[i];if(dl>safe){dx*=safe/dl;dz*=safe/dl;x=p[0]+dx;z=p[1]+dz;}qx[i]=x;qz[i]=z;}
   px=qx;pz=qz;
 }
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const tx=Math.sin(ch[i]),tz=Math.cos(ch[i]),nx=-tz,nz=tx;plat[i]=clamp((px[i]-cx[i])*nx+(pz[i]-cz[i])*nz,-pathSafe[i],pathSafe[i]);}
 const ph=new Float32Array(n),pc0=new Float32Array(n);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=idx(i-2),b=idx(i+2);ph[i]=Math.atan2(px[b]-px[a],pz[b]-pz[a]);}
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++)pc0[i]=angleDiff(ph[idx(i+2)],ph[idx(i-2)])/(step*4);
 let pcurve=pc0;
 for(let pass=0;pass<4;pass++){const q=new Float32Array(n);for(let i=0;i<n;i++)q[i]=(pcurve[idx(i-1)]+2*pcurve[i]+pcurve[idx(i+1)])/4;pcurve=q;}
 // Envelope local + pase hacia atrás de frenada. Una única física para todos los GP;
 // no hay multiplicadores secretos por circuito para fabricar un tiempo de vuelta.
 const localSafe=new Float32Array(n),speed=new Float32Array(n);let rawMaxKink=0,pathMaxKink=0;
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const s0=i*step;localSafe[i]=driverPhysicalSpeedFromCurvature(pcurve[i],s0,1);speed[i]=localSafe[i];
   const rh0=Math.atan2(P[i][0]-P[idx(i-1)][0],P[i][1]-P[idx(i-1)][1]),rh1=Math.atan2(P[idx(i+1)][0]-P[i][0],P[idx(i+1)][1]-P[i][1]);rawMaxKink=Math.max(rawMaxKink,Math.abs(angleDiff(rh1,rh0)));
   pathMaxKink=Math.max(pathMaxKink,Math.abs(angleDiff(ph[idx(i+1)],ph[i])));
 }
 const brake=14.45;
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++)for(let ii=0;ii<n;ii++){const i=idx(n-1-ii),j=idx(i+1),allowed=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,speed[j]*speed[j]+2*brake*step));if(allowed<speed[i])speed[i]=allowed;}
 let lapEstimate=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)lapEstimate+=step/Math.max(12,speed[i]);
 trackData.model.driver={step,x:px,z:pz,heading:ph,curve:pcurve,lane:plat,localSafe,speed,centerCurve,rawMaxKink,pathMaxKink,lapEstimate};
 return trackData.model.driver;
}
function driverReferencePoseAt(s){
 const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;if(!d){const q=poseAt(s);return{...q,lat:0,curve:curvatureAt(s)}}
 const x=driverModelSample(d.x,s),z=driverModelSample(d.z,s),h=driverModelAngleSample(d.heading,s),lat=driverModelSample(d.lane,s),curve=driverModelSample(d.curve,s);
 const tx=Math.sin(h),tz=Math.cos(h);return{x,z,y:trackSurfaceHeightAt(s,lat),tx,tz,nx:-tz,nz:tx,lat,curve,heading:h};
}
function driverReferenceLaneAt(s){const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;return d?finite(driverModelSample(d.lane,s),0):0;}
function driverReferencePathCurvatureAt(s){const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;const v=d?driverModelSample(d.curve,s):null;return Number.isFinite(v)?v:curvatureAt(s);}
function driverReferenceCenterCurvatureAt(s){const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;const v=d?driverModelSample(d.centerCurve,s):null;return Number.isFinite(v)?v:curvatureAt(s);}
function driverReferenceSafeSpeedAt(s){const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;const v=d?driverModelSample(d.localSafe,s):null;return Number.isFinite(v)?v:autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(s);}
function driverReferenceTargetSpeedAt(s){const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;const v=d?driverModelSample(d.speed,s):null;return Number.isFinite(v)?v:99;}
function driverReferenceKinkAhead(s,maxD=112,stepD=3){
 const d=trackData&&trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver;if(!d)return geometricKinkAhead(s,maxD,stepD);
 let prev=driverReferencePoseAt(s).heading,prevDelta=0,best={active:false,d:999,angle:0,from:prev,to:prev,jerk:0};
 for(let dist=stepD;dist<=maxD;dist+=stepD){const h=driverReferencePoseAt(s+dist).heading,delta=angleDiff(h,prev),jump=Math.abs(delta),jerk=Math.abs(angleDiff(delta,prevDelta)),active=jump>Math.PI/12||(jump>Math.PI/32&&jerk>Math.PI/55),score=jump+jerk*.65,bestScore=(best.angle||0)+(best.jerk||0)*.65;if((active&&!best.active)||(active===best.active&&score>bestScore))best={active,d:dist,angle:jump,from:prev,to:h,jerk};prevDelta=delta;prev=h;}
 return best;
}

function lapFrac(s){
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 return mod(s,L)/L;
}
function bump(x,c,w,h){
 let d=Math.abs(x-c);d=Math.min(d,1-d);
 if(d>=w)return 0;
 let t=1-d/w;
 return h*t*t*(3-2*t);
}
function profileSmooth01(t){t=clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t)}
function profileKnotsSample(f,knots){
 f=mod(f,1);
 for(let i=0;i<knots.length-1;i++){
   const a=knots[i],b=knots[i+1];
   if(f>=a[0]&&f<=b[0]){
     const span=Math.max(1e-6,b[0]-a[0]),t=profileSmooth01((f-a[0])/span);
     return lerp(a[1],b[1],t);
   }
 }
 return knots[knots.length-1][1];
}

// v5.230.62.8.6.7 · Los perfiles de geometría viven exclusivamente en BASE JSON.
function apexGeometryElevationAtFrac(f,g=trackGeometryProfileSpec()){
 if(!g||!Array.isArray(g.elev)||g.elev.length<2)return 0;
 const v=profileKnotsSample(f,g.elev),mx=Math.max(...g.elev.map(k=>Math.abs(+k[1]||0)));
 return mx<=1.5&&finite(g.elevRange,0)>1.5?v*finite(g.elevRange,0):v;
}
function authoredGradeCrossingBaseElevationAtFrac(f){return apexGeometryElevationAtFrac(f)}
function authoredGradeCrossingElevationAt(s){
 const f=lapFrac(s),L=trackData.km*1000;
 let y=authoredGradeCrossingBaseElevationAtFrac(f);
 const c=trackData.gradeCrossing;
 if(c){
   const fu=mod(c.underS,L)/L,fo=mod(c.overS,L)/L;
   const yu=authoredGradeCrossingBaseElevationAtFrac(fu),yo=authoredGradeCrossingBaseElevationAtFrac(fo);
   // Structural target for the road-centre separation: enough for a believable underpass
   // without the previous 14 m "mountain". Broad 150–180 m blending avoids a local hump.
   const cfg=apexCircuitVisualFeature('gradeCrossing',{})||{},targetGap=finite(+cfg.targetGapM,6.7),currentGap=yo-yu,delta=targetGap-currentGap,overBlend=Math.max(20,finite(+cfg.overBlendM,180)),underBlend=Math.max(20,finite(+cfg.underBlendM,150)),overWeight=clamp(finite(+cfg.overWeight,.62),0,1),underWeight=clamp(finite(+cfg.underWeight,.38),0,1);
   y+=bump(f,fo,overBlend/L,delta*overWeight);
   y-=bump(f,fu,underBlend/L,delta*underWeight);
 }
 return y;
}
function trackGeometryProfileSpec(id=(trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:"")){
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},g=j.geometryProfile;if(g){return{elevRange:Number(g.elevationRangeM)||0,widthMin:Number(g.widthMinM)||0,widthMax:Number(g.widthMaxM)||0,bankMax:Number(g.bankMaxDeg)||0,elev:Array.isArray(g.elevationKnots)?g.elevationKnots:null,width:Array.isArray(g.widthKnots)?g.widthKnots:null,bankZones:Array.isArray(g.bankZones)?g.bankZones:null,note:String(g.note||''),quality:g.quality||''}}return null;
}
function trackRoadWidthAt(s){
 if(!trackData)return 12.4;
 const cw=trackData.customWorld,L=trackData.km*1000;
 if(cw){const le=guideNearestSample(cw.edgeLeftSamples,s,L,34),re=guideNearestSample(cw.edgeRightSamples,s,L,34);let w=0;if(le&&re)w=Math.abs(le.lat-re.lat);else if(le)w=Math.abs(le.lat)*2;else if(re)w=Math.abs(re.lat)*2;if(Number.isFinite(w)&&w>4)return clamp(w,4.5,32)}
 const vw=apexSampleVerticalProfile(s,3);if(Number.isFinite(vw)&&vw>4)return clamp(vw,4.5,32);
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir)||{},rp=j.roadWidthProfile;if(rp&&Array.isArray(rp.samples)&&rp.samples.length){const v=profileKnotsSample(lapFrac(s),rp.samples);if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>4)return clamp(v,4.5,32)}
 const g=trackGeometryProfileSpec();if(g&&Array.isArray(g.width)&&g.width.length){const v=profileKnotsSample(lapFrac(s),g.width);if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>4)return clamp(v,4.5,32)}return 12.4;
}
function auditedElevationAt(s){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.spec)return 0;
 const id=trackData.spec.dir;
 if(apexCircuitVisualFeature('autoGradeCrossing',false)===true)return authoredGradeCrossingElevationAt(s);
 const g=trackGeometryProfileSpec(id);
 if(!g||!Array.isArray(g.elev)||g.elev.length<2)return 0;
 return profileKnotsSample(lapFrac(s),g.elev)*finite(g.elevRange,0);
}
function elevationAt(s){
 const imported=apexSampleVerticalProfile(s,1);if(Number.isFinite(imported))return imported;
 // Si alguien sólo importa un SVG con datum local (sin JSON), la pista también
 // debe usar la línea de meta como cero. Se aplica a cualquier trazado.
 const tc=apexTerrainContourConfig(),local=tc.reference==='LOCAL_ABSOLUTE'||tc.reference==='START_FINISH_ZERO';
 return auditedElevationAt(s)-(local?auditedElevationAt(0):0);
}
function apexProfiledBankAt(s){
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData&&trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir)||{},b=j.banking||{},rules=Array.isArray(b.profileRules)?b.profileRules:null;if(!rules||!rules.length)return null;
 const f=lapFrac(s),k=curvatureAt(s),sgn=Math.sign(k)||1;let mag=clamp(Math.abs(k)*720,0,finite(+b.maxBankDeg,3.15));
 for(const r of rules){const a=finite(+r.from,0),z=finite(+r.to,1);if(f>=a&&f<=(z>=1?1:z)){if(Number.isFinite(+r.multiplier))mag*=+r.multiplier;if(Number.isFinite(+r.capDeg))mag=Math.min(mag,+r.capDeg);break}}
 const c=trackData.gradeCrossing,damp=b.crossingDamping||{},L=trackData.km*1000;if(c&&damp){const du=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(s,c.underS,L)),dv=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(s,c.overS,L)),ur=finite(+damp.underRadiusM,0),or=finite(+damp.overRadiusM,0);if((ur>0&&du<ur)||(or>0&&dv<or))mag*=clamp(finite(+damp.multiplier,1),0,1)}
 return sgn*mag*Math.PI/180;
}
function circularFracDistance(a,b){let d=Math.abs(a-b);return Math.min(d,1-d)}
function apexAutomaticBankRawAt(s){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.spec)return 0;const id=trackData.spec.dir;const cref=apexCornerReferenceAtS(s);
 const profiled=apexProfiledBankAt(s);if(Number.isFinite(profiled)){let v=profiled;return v}
 const g=trackGeometryProfileSpec(id);if(!g)return 0;const k=curvatureAt(s),sgn=Math.sign(k)||1,L=trackData.km*1000;let mag=clamp(Math.abs(k)*690,0,finite((readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{}).banking?.backgroundMaxBankDeg,Array.isArray(g.bankZones)&&g.bankZones.length?Math.min(finite(g.bankMax,2),3):finite(g.bankMax,2)));if(Math.abs(k)<.00045)mag*=.18;
 if(Array.isArray(g.bankZones)){for(const z of g.bankZones){const legacy=mod((+z.c||0)*L,L),marks=trackData.cornerMarkers||[];let nearest=null,bd=Infinity;for(const m of marks){const d=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(m.s,legacy,L));if(d<bd){bd=d;nearest=m}}const rr=nearest?apexCornerRegionForTurn(nearest.turn):null;if(rr){const along=mod(s-rr.startS,L);if(along<=rr.lengthM){const rrRef=apexCornerReferenceAtS(s),t=rrRef.region&&rrRef.region.turn===rr.turn?rrRef.gain:0;mag=Math.max(mag,finite(z.deg,0)*t)}}else{const d=circularFracDistance(lapFrac(s),z.c),w=Math.max(.002,z.w);if(d<w){const t=profileSmooth01(1-d/w);mag=Math.max(mag,finite(z.deg,0)*t)}}}}
 return sgn*mag*Math.PI/180;
}
function apexAutomaticBankAt(s){
 const L=trackData.km*1000;let cached=trackData._continuousAutoBank;
 if(!cached){
 const n=Math.max(16,Math.ceil(L/2)),step=L/n,raw=new Float64Array(n),vals=new Float64Array(n),radius=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(12/step));
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++)raw[i]=apexAutomaticBankRawAt(i*step);
 // Smooth rule boundaries, fallback zones and sign changes, with periodic wrap at finish.
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){let total=0,weight=0;for(let k=-radius;k<=radius;k++){const w=radius+1-Math.abs(k);total+=raw[mod(i+k,n)]*w;weight+=w;}vals[i]=total/weight;}
 cached=trackData._continuousAutoBank={step,vals};
 }
 const f=mod(s,L)/cached.step,i=Math.floor(f),t=f-i,n=cached.vals.length;
 return lerp(cached.vals[i%n],cached.vals[(i+1)%n],t);
}
function bankAt(s){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.spec)return 0;
 const authored=apexAuthoredBankAt(s);if(Number.isFinite(authored))return authored;
 const imported=apexSampleVerticalProfile(s,2);if(Number.isFinite(imported))return imported*Math.PI/180;
 return apexAutomaticBankAt(s);
}

function rawCenterPoseAt(s){
 const P=trackData&&trackData.pts,n=P?P.length:0,L=trackData?trackData.km*1000:0;
 if(!P||n<2||!Number.isFinite(L)||L<=0){
   return{x:0,z:0,tx:0,tz:1,nx:-1,nz:0,i:0,t:0};
 }
 s=Number.isFinite(s)?s:0;
 const step=L/n,f=mod(s,L)/step;
 let i=Math.floor(f);
 if(!Number.isFinite(i))i=0;
 i=mod(i,n);
 const t=clamp(f-Math.floor(f),0,1);
 const a=P[i],b=P[(i+1)%n];
 if(!a||!b){
   const aa=P[0],bb=P[1]||P[0];
   let tx=bb[0]-aa[0],tz=bb[1]-aa[1],d=Math.hypot(tx,tz)||1;tx/=d;tz/=d;
   return{x:aa[0],z:aa[1],tx,tz,nx:-tz,nz:tx,i:0,t:0};
 }
 const x=lerp(a[0],b[0],t),z=lerp(a[1],b[1],t);
 let tx=b[0]-a[0],tz=b[1]-a[1];const d=Math.hypot(tx,tz)||1;tx/=d;tz/=d;
 return{x,z,tx,tz,nx:-tz,nz:tx,i,t};
}
function poseFlatAt(s){
 const c=rawCenterPoseAt(s);
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||trackData.pts.length<3)return c;
 // v5.148: el SVG de algunos circuitos contiene nodos
 // poligonales muy duros. En la primera chicane el tangente bruto saltaba ~72° entre
 // dos segmentos contiguos: el coche seguía físicamente recto y Frenet interpretaba
 // de golpe que estaba cruzado respecto a una pista que acababa de girar 72°.
 // La posición central sigue siendo la del SVG; sólo suavizamos el MARCO Frenet
 // mediante una cuerda corta alrededor de s. Así heading, normal, lat y control giran
 // progresivamente sin deformar la longitud ni mover el circuito.
 const id=trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir||"",half=5.0;
 const a=rawCenterPoseAt(s-half),b=rawCenterPoseAt(s+half);
 let tx=b.x-a.x,tz=b.z-a.z,d=Math.hypot(tx,tz);
 if(!Number.isFinite(d)||d<1e-5){tx=c.tx;tz=c.tz;d=1}else{tx/=d;tz/=d}
 return{x:c.x,z:c.z,tx,tz,nx:-tz,nz:tx,i:c.i,t:c.t,rawTx:c.tx,rawTz:c.tz};
}
function rawTrackHeadingAt(s){
 const q=rawCenterPoseAt(s);return Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz);
}
function geometricKinkAhead(s,maxD=72,stepD=3){
