 if(!trackData)return{active:false,d:999,angle:0,from:0,to:0,jerk:0};
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 let prev=rawTrackHeadingAt(s),prevDelta=0,best={active:false,d:999,angle:0,from:prev,to:prev,jerk:0};
 for(let d=stepD;d<=maxD;d+=stepD){
   const h=rawTrackHeadingAt(mod(s+d,L)),delta=angleDiff(h,prev),jump=Math.abs(delta),jerk=Math.abs(angleDiff(delta,prevDelta));
   // v5.210: la telemetría de La telemetría mostró saltos de 10–11° que no activaban el guard
   // antiguo (>20°) y acababan en latigazo lateral. Un giro suave puede acumular ángulo,
   // pero no cambia bruscamente su incremento; por eso exigimos también jerk angular.
   const active=(jump>Math.PI/10)||(jump>Math.PI/25&&jerk>Math.PI/45); // >18° o >7.2° con jerk >4°
   const score=jump+jerk*.65;
   const bestScore=(best.angle||0)+(best.jerk||0)*.65;
   if((active&&!best.active)||(active===best.active&&score>bestScore))best={active,d,angle:jump,from:prev,to:h,jerk};
   prevDelta=delta;prev=h;
 }
 return best;
}

// Renderer de cinta: los bordes se construyen como offsets con unión miter en cada nodo
// del trazado. Esto evita que una curva de ~90° salte de una normal a otra y retuerza la pista.
function ensureRenderMiters(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||trackData.pts.length<3)return null;
 if(trackData.model&&trackData.model.renderMiters&&trackData.model.renderMiters.length===trackData.pts.length)return trackData.model.renderMiters;
 const P=trackData.pts,n=P.length,M=new Array(n);
 const unit=(ax,az,bx,bz)=>{let x=bx-ax,z=bz-az,d=Math.hypot(x,z)||1;return{x:x/d,z:z/d}};
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const pm=P[(i-1+n)%n],pc=P[i],pp=P[(i+1)%n];
   const tin=unit(pm[0],pm[1],pc[0],pc[1]),tout=unit(pc[0],pc[1],pp[0],pp[1]);
   const nin={x:-tin.z,z:tin.x},nout={x:-tout.z,z:tout.x};
   let mx=nin.x+nout.x,mz=nin.z+nout.z,ml=Math.hypot(mx,mz);
   if(ml<.18){mx=nout.x;mz=nout.z;ml=1}
   mx/=ml;mz/=ml;
   let den=mx*nout.x+mz*nout.z;
   if(!Number.isFinite(den)||Math.abs(den)<.28){mx=nout.x;mz=nout.z;den=1}   let scale=1/den;
   // Miter limit: una horquilla extrema se convierte en una unión corta, nunca en una aguja gigante.
   scale=clamp(scale,.62,2.20);
   M[i]={x:mx*scale,z:mz*scale};
 }
 if(trackData.model)trackData.model.renderMiters=M;
 return M;
}
function renderRibbonFrameAt(s){
 const P=trackData&&trackData.pts,n=P?P.length:0,L=trackData?trackData.km*1000:0;
 if(!P||n<2||!Number.isFinite(L)||L<=0){const q=poseAt(s);return{...q,ox:q.nx,oz:q.nz,t:0}}
 const step=L/n,f=mod(Number.isFinite(s)?s:0,L)/step;
 let i=Math.floor(f);if(!Number.isFinite(i))i=0;i=mod(i,n);
 const t=clamp(f-Math.floor(f),0,1),a=P[i],b=P[(i+1)%n],miters=ensureRenderMiters();
 const x=lerp(a[0],b[0],t),z=lerp(a[1],b[1],t);
 let tx=b[0]-a[0],tz=b[1]-a[1],d=Math.hypot(tx,tz)||1;tx/=d;tz/=d;
 const m0=miters?miters[i]:{x:-tz,z:tx},m1=miters?miters[(i+1)%n]:m0;
 const bank=bankAt(s);
 return{x,z,y:elevationAt(s),tx,tz,nx:-tz,nz:tx,ox:lerp(m0.x,m1.x,t),oz:lerp(m0.z,m1.z,t),i,t,bank};
}
function ribbonWorldPoint(frame,width,side=1,yOff=0){
 const bank=Number.isFinite(frame.bank)?frame.bank:0;
 const bankY=Math.tan(bank)*width*side;
 return{x:frame.x+frame.ox*width*side,y:frame.y+bankY+yOff,z:frame.z+frame.oz*width*side};
}

// v5.211 · Pose canónica de la superficie 3D.
// s aporta el desnivel longitudinal y lat la altura transversal REAL debida al peralte.
// Cámara, rivales, ruedas/sombras, espejos y telemetría deben partir de esta misma superficie.
function trackSurfacePoseAt(s,lat=0,yOff=0){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),signedLat=finite(lat,0),side=signedLat>=0?1:-1;
 const p=ribbonWorldPoint(q,Math.abs(signedLat),side,yOff),bank=finite(q.bank,0),tanBank=Math.tan(bank);
 const rd=Math.hypot(q.ox,tanBank,q.oz)||1;
 return {...q,x:p.x,y:p.y,z:p.z,lat:signedLat,centerY:q.y,bank,tanBank,
   surfaceRx:q.ox/rd,surfaceRy:tanBank/rd,surfaceRz:q.oz/rd};
}
function trackSurfaceHeightAt(s,lat=0,yOff=0){return trackSurfacePoseAt(s,lat,yOff).y}
function trackSurfaceCameraRollAt(s,lat=0,trackDirDot=1){
 const surf=trackSurfacePoseAt(s,lat),env=envProfile(s),inside=Math.abs(lat)<=env.half+env.curb+env.runoff+.8;
 if(!inside)return 0;
 const align=clamp((Math.abs(finite(trackDirDot,1))-.12)/.78,0,1);
 // Peralte positivo eleva el lado lateral +. La cámara acompaña al coche inclinando la vista en sentido opuesto.
 return -surf.bank*align;
}

function angleDiff(a,b){
 let d=a-b;
 while(d>Math.PI)d-=Math.PI*2;
 while(d<-Math.PI)d+=Math.PI*2;
 return d;
}
function curvatureAt(s){
 if(trackData&&trackData.model){
   const v=sampleModel(trackData.model.curve,s);
   if(Number.isFinite(v))return v;
 }
 const a=poseFlatAt(s-8),b=poseFlatAt(s+8),aa=Math.atan2(a.tx,a.tz),bb=Math.atan2(b.tx,b.tz);
 return angleDiff(bb,aa)/16;
}
function cornerSafeSpeed(s){
 if(trackData&&trackData.model){
   const v=sampleModel(trackData.model.safe,s);
   if(Number.isFinite(v))return v;
 }
 const k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(s));
 return clamp(96/Math.sqrt(1+k*1750),34,98);
}
function upcomingCorner(){
 const sign=(player.trackDirDot||1)>=0?1:-1;
 let best={d:999,safe:98,k:0,sign};
 for(let d=35;d<=230;d+=10){
   const ss=player.s+sign*d,k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss)),safe=cornerSafeSpeed(ss);
   if(k>.0035&&safe<best.safe)best={d,safe,k,sign};
 }
 return best;
}
function poseAt(s){
 const q=poseFlatAt(s);
 q.y=elevationAt(s);
 return q;
}


function envProfile(s=(player&&Number.isFinite(player.s)?player.s:0)){
 const id=trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:"",j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},je=j.environment||{},js=je.surfaces||((j.track||{}).surfaces)||{},style={runoffSurface:"ASPHALT",offSurface:"GRASS",...js},zone=authoredStructureZoneAtS(s);
 const env={half:trackRoadWidthAt(s)*.5,curb:.75,runoff:3.2,runoffSurface:style.runoffSurface,offSurface:style.offSurface,wall:false,wallH:1.05,scenery:"OPEN",leftWall:false,rightWall:false,containment:false,containmentLeft:false,containmentRight:false,tunnel:false,limitExtra:8.0};
 if(zone&&zone.kind==="UNDER"){env.tunnel=true;env.scenery="SVG_TUNNEL";env.structureHeight=zone.height||0;env.structureTopRiseM=Number.isFinite(zone.roofRiseM)?zone.roofRiseM:0;env.structureWidth=zone.width||0;env.runoff=.65;env.runoffSurface="ASPHALT";env.offSurface="ASPHALT"}
 else if(zone&&zone.kind==="OVER")env.scenery="SVG_BRIDGE";
 env.limit=env.half+env.curb+env.runoff+env.limitExtra;return env;
}
function finalizeEnvForTrack(s,env){return env;}
function curbInfo(s){
 const env=envProfile(s),L=trackData&&trackData.km?trackData.km*1000:0,cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,zones=cw&&Array.isArray(cw.curbZones)?cw.curbZones:[];
 if(L>0&&zones.length){let left=false,right=false;for(const z of zones){if(!z||!apexCurbZoneContains(s,z,L))continue;if(z.side==='left')left=true;else if(z.side==='right')right=true;}return {left,right,source:'SVG_AUTHORITATIVE'};}
 const k=0.66*curvatureAt(s)+0.34*curvatureAt(s+10),a=Math.abs(k);let left=false,right=false;
 if(a>.0018){if(k>0){right=true;if(a>.0065)left=true;}else{left=true;if(a>.0065)right=true;}}
 if(env.scenery==='MAIN_STRAIGHT'||env.scenery==='HIGH_SPEED'||env.tunnel){left=false;right=false;}if(env.scenery==='CHICANE'){left=true;right=true;}if(a<.0016){left=false;right=false;}
 return {left,right,source:'APEX_CURVATURE_FALLBACK'};
}
function surfaceColor(surface,alt=false){
 let c="#64686c";
 if(surface==="ASPHALT")c=alt?"#777b80":"#696d72";
 else if(surface==="GRAVEL")c=alt?"#c2a77e":"#ad9068";
 else if(surface==="SAND")c=alt?"#d3b879":"#bea166";
 else if(surface==="GRASS")c=alt?"#3f7a44":"#2e6737";
 else if(surface==="PARK")c=alt?"#567d51":"#446846";
 else if(surface==="FOREST")c=alt?"#355b38":"#26452a";
 else if(surface==="DIRT")c=alt?"#8d7759":"#755f45";
 else if(surface==="ROAD")c=alt?"#747a80":"#60666d";
 else if(surface==="CITY")c=alt?"#848a90":"#6c7279";
 else if(surface==="WATER")c=alt?"#5c92b0":"#477590";
 else if(surface==="DUNES")c=alt?"#ccb27b":"#b59660";
 else if(surface==="DRYGRASS")c=alt?"#8e915b":"#767949";
 else if(surface==="PLAZA")c=alt?"#8a8f95":"#757a81";
 const dark=apexWeatherDarkness(),wet=apexWeatherWetFactor(),paved=surface==="ASPHALT"||surface==="ROAD"||surface==="CITY"||surface==="PLAZA";
 if(dark>.01)c=shadeColor(c,-Math.round(dark*(paved?14:9)));
 if(paved&&wet>.02)c=shadeColor(c,-Math.round(wet*17));
 return c;
}
function circuitIdentity(){
 const id=trackData?trackData.spec.dir:'',j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},je=j.environment||{},jp=je.palette||null;
 if(jp&&jp.sky1&&jp.sky2&&jp.ground&&jp.road)return{name:String((j.circuit||{}).name||id),sky1:jp.sky1,sky2:jp.sky2,ground:jp.ground,road:jp.road,haze:jp.haze||'rgba(220,232,234,.20)',accent:jp.accent||'#d9404f'};
 return null;
}
function apexColorMix(a,b,t){
 t=clamp(t,0,1);const pa=/^#?([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(String(a||'')),pb=/^#?([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(String(b||''));if(!pa||!pb)return a;
 const A=parseInt(pa[1],16),B=parseInt(pb[1],16),ch=(sh)=>Math.round(lerp((A>>sh)&255,(B>>sh)&255,t));return'#'+[ch(16),ch(8),ch(0)].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}
function apexWeatherTintPalette(base){
 const sm=apexWeatherSample();if(!apexWeatherRuntime)return base;
 const over=clamp(sm.cloud*.72+sm.dark*.55,0,.88),storm=clamp((sm.dark-.28)*2.2,0,.65),wet=clamp(Number.isFinite(apexWeatherWetFactor())?apexWeatherWetFactor():0,0,1);
 let sky1=apexColorMix(base.sky1,storm>.1?'#53606d':'#a8bac7',over*.46+storm*.20),sky2=apexColorMix(base.sky2,'#c7d0d5',over*.50),ground=shadeColor(base.ground,-Math.round(sm.dark*22+sm.cloud*4)),road=shadeColor(base.road,-Math.round(sm.dark*16+wet*13));
 const flash=apexWeatherLightningFlash();if(flash>.01){sky1=apexColorMix(sky1,'#eef5ff',flash*.60);sky2=apexColorMix(sky2,'#ffffff',flash*.52);ground=apexColorMix(ground,'#b9c7d2',flash*.12)}
 return{sky1,sky2,ground,road};
}
function palette(){
 const ci=circuitIdentity();let base;
 if(ci)base={sky1:ci.sky1,sky2:ci.sky2,ground:ci.ground,road:ci.road};
 else{
   const t=trackData?trackData.spec.theme:"park";
   if(t==="night")base={sky1:"#050b18",sky2:"#17264a",ground:"#101720",road:"#34383d"};
   else if(t==="sunset")base={sky1:"#523e79",sky2:"#e48d69",ground:"#45483f",road:"#34383d"};
   else if(t==="dry")base={sky1:"#76c9f1",sky2:"#eeddbc",ground:"#7b8650",road:"#37393d"};
   else if(t==="dunes")base={sky1:"#78cae7",sky2:"#dfebdc",ground:"#9f9768",road:"#37393d"};
   else if(t==="mountain"||t==="forest"||t==="tropical")base={sky1:"#75c4eb",sky2:"#dbeee6",ground:"#2d6b3d",road:"#35383c"};
   else if(t==="city")base={sky1:"#6fc3e9",sky2:"#e4ecee",ground:"#66716d",road:"#34373b"};
   else base={sky1:"#7bc9ef",sky2:"#dceff0",ground:"#39774b",road:"#35383c"};
 }
 return apexWeatherTintPalette(base);
}

function worldToCamera(wx,wy,wz,cam){
 const dx=wx-cam.x,dz=wz-cam.z,x=dx*cam.nx+dz*cam.nz,y=wy-cam.y,z=dx*cam.tx+dz*cam.tz;
 if(cam.inspectionOrbit){const c=Math.cos(cam.pitchRad),sn=Math.sin(cam.pitchRad);return{x,y:y*c-z*sn,z:y*sn+z*c};}
 return{x,y,z};
}
function compactMode(){ return document.body.classList.contains("compact"); }
function mobileLandscapeMode(){ return document.body.classList.contains("mobile-landscape"); }
function shortLandscapeMode(){ return document.body.classList.contains("short-landscape"); }
function updateHudMapLayout(){
 const timeBox=$("timeBox"),miniEl=$("mini"),bars=$("bars");
 if(!timeBox||!miniEl||!bars)return;
 const mobileLand=mobileLandscapeMode(),shortLand=shortLandscapeMode(),compact=compactMode();
 const gap=mobileLand?6:8;
 const right=mobileLand?6:10;
 const rect=timeBox.getBoundingClientRect();
 let w=mobileLand?(shortLand?116:128):(compact?154:176);
 let h=mobileLand?(shortLand?78:88):(compact?116:134);
 const maxW=Math.max(96,Math.floor(innerWidth*(mobileLand?0.24:0.18)));
 if(w>maxW){const sc=maxW/w;w=Math.round(w*sc);h=Math.round(h*sc)}
 const top=Math.round(rect.bottom+gap);
 miniEl.style.right=right+"px";
 miniEl.style.top=top+"px";
 miniEl.style.width=w+"px";
 miniEl.style.height=h+"px";
 miniEl.style.borderRadius=(mobileLand?8:12)+"px";
 if(mobileLand){
   bars.style.right=right+"px";
   bars.style.top=(top+h+gap)+"px";
   bars.style.bottom="auto";
 }else{
   bars.style.right=right+"px";
   bars.style.top="";
   bars.style.bottom="22%";
 }
}
function mobilePerformanceMode(){ return document.body.classList.contains("mobile-performance"); }
// v5.212 · Render adaptativo también en PC.
// La telemetría v5.211 mostró ~5 FPS con >200 ms de CPU construyendo pista/entorno.
// Conservamos el campo cercano preciso y los edificios visibles por frustum, pero reducimos
// distancia trasera, detalle lejano y densidad de segmentos cuando el frame-rate cae.
function desktopAdaptiveRenderPressure(){
 const fps=finite(telemetryPerf&&telemetryPerf.fps,60);
 if(fps<9)return 0;
 if(fps<14)return .18;
 if(fps<20)return .36;
 if(fps<28)return .58;
 if(fps<40)return .78;
 return 1;
}
// v5.214 · En móvil v5.213 sólo obedecía al selector de calidad y NO al FPS real.
// En la prueba del circuito importado eso dejó 470 m de pista con 7.2 FPS. La presión móvil reduce
// trabajo de CPU (segmentos/distancia/detalle), no edificios: los volúmenes lejanos
// siguen existiendo y pasan antes a LOD barato.
function mobileAdaptiveRenderPressure(){
 const fps=finite(telemetryPerf&&telemetryPerf.fps,60);
 if(fps<8)return .12;
 if(fps<10)return .18;
 if(fps<14)return .32;
 if(fps<20)return .50;
 if(fps<28)return .70;
 if(fps<38)return .86;
 return 1;
}
function mobileRenderPressure(){return mobilePerformanceMode()?mobileAdaptiveRenderPressure():desktopAdaptiveRenderPressure();}
// v5.230.62.2 · Política de visibilidad estilo GP4.
// El decorado pequeño se obtiene de los sectores próximos ya compilados. Las masas de skyline
// (edificios, torres, gradas, boxes, puentes y noria) son EXCEPCIÓN: si la cámara mira hacia ellas
// siguen siendo candidatas aunque su sector esté lejos. Así ganamos CPU sin vaciar el horizonte.
function apexIsSkylineLandmark(it){
 if(!it)return false;const t=String(it.type||'').toLowerCase();
 return !!(it.farGeo||it.proposalId||Number(it.maxDs)>1200||['block','building','pit','tower','grandstand','ferris','bridge'].includes(t));
}
// v5.230.62.3 · Visibilidad por EXTENSIÓN + clipping real de near-plane.
// Los boxes y edificios largos podían desaparecer a mitad de recorrerlos porque su anchor/centro
// ya había salido del frustum aunque todavía quedasen decenas o cientos de metros visibles.
// Esta nube de puntos se calcula una sola vez por landmark y cubre huella, bordes largos y altura.
function apexLandmarkExtentWorldSamples(it){
 if(!it)return[];
 if(Array.isArray(it._apexExtentWorldSamples)&&it._apexExtentWorldSamples.length)return it._apexExtentWorldSamples;
 const a=it.anchor||{},h=Math.max(0,Number(it.height)||0),out=[],push=(x,y,z)=>{if(Number.isFinite(x)&&Number.isFinite(y)&&Number.isFinite(z))out.push({x,y,z})};
 const addVertical=(x,y,z)=>{push(x,y,z);if(h>1.2){push(x,y+h*.52,z);push(x,y+h,z)}};
 const fp=Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)?it.worldFootprint:null,wy=Array.isArray(it.worldY)?it.worldY:null;
 if(fp&&fp.length>=2){
   for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){
     const p0=fp[i],p1=fp[(i+1)%fp.length],y0=wy&&Number.isFinite(wy[i])?wy[i]:(Number.isFinite(a.y)?a.y:0),y1=wy&&Number.isFinite(wy[(i+1)%fp.length])?wy[(i+1)%fp.length]:y0;
     const len=Math.hypot(p1[0]-p0[0],p1[1]-p0[1]),steps=Math.max(1,Math.min(8,Math.ceil(len/55)));
     for(let k=0;k<steps;k++){const t=k/steps;addVertical(lerp(p0[0],p1[0],t),lerp(y0,y1,t),lerp(p0[1],p1[1],t));}
   }
 }else{
   const q=a.q||(Number.isFinite(a.s)?poseAt(a.s):null),len=Math.max(0,Number(it.length)||Number(it.width)||0),dep=Math.max(0,Number(it.depth)||Number(it.width)||0),baseY=Number.isFinite(a.y)?a.y:0;
   if(q&&Number.isFinite(a.x)&&Number.isFinite(a.z)&&len>2){
     const steps=Math.max(2,Math.min(10,Math.ceil(len/55)));
     for(let k=0;k<=steps;k++){const along=lerp(-len*.5,len*.5,k/steps);for(const lat of dep>2?[-dep*.5,0,dep*.5]:[0])addVertical(a.x+q.tx*along+q.nx*lat,baseY,a.z+q.tz*along+q.nz*lat);}
   }else if(Number.isFinite(a.x)&&Number.isFinite(a.z))addVertical(a.x,baseY,a.z);
 }
 if(!out.length&&Number.isFinite(a.x)&&Number.isFinite(a.z))addVertical(a.x,Number.isFinite(a.y)?a.y:0,a.z);
 try{Object.defineProperty(it,'_apexExtentWorldSamples',{value:out,writable:true,configurable:true})}catch(e){it._apexExtentWorldSamples=out}
 return out;
}
function apexLandmarkExtentCamSamples(it,cam){return apexLandmarkExtentWorldSamples(it).map(p=>worldToCamera(p.x,p.y,p.z,cam)).filter(c=>c&&Number.isFinite(c.z));}
function apexLandmarkEffectiveCamDepth(it,cam,base=null){
 let z=base&&Number.isFinite(base.z)&&base.z>1?base.z:Infinity;
 for(const c of apexLandmarkExtentCamSamples(it,cam))if(c.z>1&&c.z<z)z=c.z;
 return Number.isFinite(z)?z:(base&&Number.isFinite(base.z)?base.z:99999);
}
// v5.230.62.3 · Clipping geométrico real de masas largas contra el plano cercano.
// No usamos sólo puntos de muestra: las caras completas de la huella 3D se recortan y
// se comprueba si el polígono resultante intersecta realmente el viewport. Así boxes,
// gradas y edificios principales sobreviven mientras quede cualquier fragmento delante.
function apexLandmarkFootprintClipInfo(it,cam,pitch,maxZ=6000,pad=.28){
 if(!it||!cam||!Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)||it.worldFootprint.length<3)return null;
 const fp=it.worldFootprint,a=it.anchor||{},wy=Array.isArray(it.worldY)?it.worldY:null,h=Math.max(.05,Number(it.height)||.05),near=.52,batch=it._apexStaticBatch;
 const bottom=[],top=[];
 if(batch&&Array.isArray(batch.footprintBottom)&&batch.footprintBottom.length===fp.length){
   for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){bottom.push(apexStaticWorldCam(batch.footprintBottom[i],cam));top.push(apexStaticWorldCam(batch.footprintTop[i],cam));}
 }else for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){
   const v=fp[i],y=wy&&Number.isFinite(wy[i])?wy[i]:(Number.isFinite(a.y)?a.y:0);
   bottom.push(worldToCamera(v[0],y,v[1],cam));top.push(worldToCamera(v[0],y+h,v[1],cam));
 }
 const faces=[bottom,top];for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){const k=(i+1)%fp.length;faces.push([bottom[i],bottom[k],top[k],top[i]])}
 let visible=false,depth=Infinity,minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;
 const px=Math.max(60,W*pad),py=Math.max(50,H*pad);
 for(const face of faces){
   const clipped=clipPolyNear(face,near);if(clipped.length<3)continue;
   let faceMin=Infinity;for(const c of clipped)if(Number.isFinite(c.z))faceMin=Math.min(faceMin,c.z);
   if(faceMin>maxZ)continue;
   const pp=projectCameraPoly(clipped,pitch);if(!pp||pp.length<3)continue;
   let x0=Infinity,x1=-Infinity,y0=Infinity,y1=-Infinity;
   for(const p of pp){x0=Math.min(x0,p[0]);x1=Math.max(x1,p[0]);y0=Math.min(y0,p[1]);y1=Math.max(y1,p[1]);}
   if(x1>=-px&&x0<=W+px&&y1>=-py&&y0<=H+py){visible=true;depth=Math.min(depth,faceMin);minX=Math.min(minX,x0);maxX=Math.max(maxX,x1);minY=Math.min(minY,y0);maxY=Math.max(maxY,y1);}
 }
 if(!visible)return{visible:false,depth:Infinity};
 return{visible:true,depth:Number.isFinite(depth)?depth:near,x:(minX+maxX)*.5,y:(minY+maxY)*.5,minX,maxX,minY,maxY};
}
// ---------- v5.230.62.5 · BATCHING CPU DE GEOMETRIA ESTATICA ----------
// WebGL ya enviaba toda la escena principal en UN único drawArrays por frame. Por tanto,
// aquí no inventamos más "draw-call batching": reducimos el trabajo CPU repetido antes
// del submit. Cotas de suelo, quads de carreteras/barreras, footprints y selección de
// sectores se preparan una vez al compilar el circuito y se reutilizan durante la carrera.
const apexStaticBatchPerf={entryCacheHits:0,entryCacheMisses:0,compiledItems:0,compiledPrimitives:0,wallStyles:0,bufferGrows:0};
const apexStaticOcclusionPerf={frames:0,occludersBuilt:0,itemsTested:0,itemsCulled:0,primitiveTests:0,primitiveCulled:0};
function apexStaticWorldCam(p,cam){return worldToCamera(p[0],p[1],p[2],cam)}
function apexStaticWorldBound(points){
 if(!Array.isArray(points)||!points.length)return null;let x=0,y=0,z=0;for(const p of points){x+=p[0];y+=p[1];z+=p[2]}x/=points.length;y/=points.length;z/=points.length;let r=.1;for(const p of points)r=Math.max(r,Math.hypot(p[0]-x,p[1]-y,p[2]-z));return{x,y,z,r};
}
function apexStaticBoundInView(bound,cam,far=1900,margin=1.22){
 if(!bound)return true;apexStaticOcclusionPerf.primitiveTests++;const c=worldToCamera(bound.x,bound.y,bound.z,cam),r=Math.max(.2,bound.r||0);if(c.z+r<.42||c.z-r>far){apexStaticOcclusionPerf.primitiveCulled++;return false}const zz=Math.max(.5,c.z),half=zz*Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5)*margin+r;if(Math.abs(c.x)>half){apexStaticOcclusionPerf.primitiveCulled++;return false}return true;
}
// v5.230.62.8.6.7.6.4.14 · Orden físico de superficies SVG.
// surfaceZone = escapatoria/material del suelo: siempre queda bajo la cinta principal.
// marking = pintura: queda ligeramente por encima para evitar z-fighting.
function apexGroundPatchYOffset(it){
 const t=String(it&&it.type||'').toLowerCase();
 if(t==='surfacezone')return -.035;
 if(t==='water')return -.010;
 if(t==='marking')return .025;
 return .002;
}
function apexGroundPatchDepthBias(it){
 const t=String(it&&it.type||'').toLowerCase();
 if(t==='surfacezone')return .030;
 if(t==='water')return .012;
 if(t==='marking')return -.020;
 return .006;
}
function apexBuildLandmarkStaticBatch(it){
 if(!it)return null;const b={type:String(it.type||''),primitiveCount:0},a=it.anchor||{},fp=Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)?it.worldFootprint:null,wy=Array.isArray(it.worldY)?it.worldY:null;
 if(fp&&fp.length>=3){
   b.footprintBottom=fp.map((v,i)=>[v[0],wy&&Number.isFinite(wy[i])?wy[i]:(Number.isFinite(a.y)?a.y:apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1])),v[1]]);
   const h=Math.max(.05,Number(it.height)||.05);b.footprintTop=b.footprintBottom.map(p=>[p[0],p[1]+h,p[2]]);b.primitiveCount+=fp.length*2;
 }
 if(['surfaceZone','water','marking','forestZone'].includes(it.type)&&fp&&fp.length>=3){
   b.surfacePoly3=fp.map((v,i)=>[v[0],(wy&&Number.isFinite(wy[i])?wy[i]:apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1]))+apexGroundPatchYOffset(it),v[1]]);b.primitiveCount++;
   if(it.type==='forestZone'&&Array.isArray(it.forestPoints)){
     b.forest=it.forestPoints.map(fp0=>{const x=fp0[0],z=fp0[1],y=fp0[2]||0;return{trunk:[[x-.12,y,z],[x+.12,y,z],[x+.12,y+3.1,z],[x-.12,y+3.1,z]],crown:[[x-2,y+1.8,z],[x+2,y+1.8,z],[x,y+8.2,z]]}});b.primitiveCount+=b.forest.length*2;
   }
 }
 if(it.type==='barrier'&&Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){
   const h=Math.max(.55,it.height||1.05),Y=Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)?it.worldPathY:null;b.barrierSegments=[];
   for(let j=0;j<it.worldPath.length-1;j++){const p0=it.worldPath[j],p1=it.worldPath[j+1],g0=Y&&Number.isFinite(Y[j])?Y[j]:apexWorldGroundY(p0[0],p0[1]),g1=Y&&Number.isFinite(Y[j+1])?Y[j+1]:apexWorldGroundY(p1[0],p1[1]),q=[[p0[0],g0,p0[1]],[p1[0],g1,p1[1]],[p1[0],g1+h,p1[1]],[p0[0],g0+h,p0[1]]];b.barrierSegments.push({q,bound:apexStaticWorldBound(q),fenceH:Number(it.fenceH)||1.3});}
   b.primitiveCount+=b.barrierSegments.length;
 }
 if(it.type==='road'&&Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){
   const P=it.worldPath,Y=Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)?it.worldPathY:[],hw=Math.max(1.8,(Number(it.width)||8)*.5),lift=.045;b.roadQuads=[];
   for(let j=0;j<P.length-1;j++){const p0=P[j],p1=P[j+1],dx=p1[0]-p0[0],dz=p1[1]-p0[1],ln=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(ln<.08)continue;const nx=-dz/ln,nz=dx/ln,y0=Number.isFinite(Y[j])?Y[j]:apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(p0[0],p0[1],0),y1=Number.isFinite(Y[j+1])?Y[j+1]:apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(p1[0],p1[1],0),q=[[p0[0]+nx*hw,y0+lift,p0[1]+nz*hw],[p0[0]-nx*hw,y0+lift,p0[1]-nz*hw],[p1[0]-nx*hw,y1+lift,p1[1]-nz*hw],[p1[0]+nx*hw,y1+lift,p1[1]+nz*hw]];try{Object.defineProperty(q,'_apexBound',{value:apexStaticWorldBound(q),configurable:true})}catch(e){q._apexBound=apexStaticWorldBound(q)}b.roadQuads.push(q);}
   b.primitiveCount+=b.roadQuads.length;
 }
 try{Object.defineProperty(it,'_apexStaticBatch',{value:b,writable:true,configurable:true})}catch(e){it._apexStaticBatch=b}return b;
}
function apexBuildCircuitStaticRenderBatches(cw){
 const items=cw&&Array.isArray(cw.landmarks)?cw.landmarks:[],walls=cw&&(cw._wallRenderSegments||cw.wallSegments)||[];let compiled=0,prims=0,wallStyles=0;
 for(const it of items){const b=apexBuildLandmarkStaticBatch(it);if(b){compiled++;prims+=b.primitiveCount||0}}
 for(const sg of walls){if(!sg)continue;const env=envProfile(sg.s||0),style=wallBarrierStyle(env,sg.s||0,sg.side||1);try{Object.defineProperty(sg,'_apexStaticWall',{value:{env,style},writable:true,configurable:true})}catch(e){sg._apexStaticWall={env,style}}wallStyles++;}
 apexStaticBatchPerf.compiledItems=compiled;apexStaticBatchPerf.compiledPrimitives=prims;apexStaticBatchPerf.wallStyles=wallStyles;
 return{items:compiled,primitives:prims,wallStyles};
}
function apexStaticBatchStatsSnapshot(){
 const cc=trackData&&trackData.compiledCircuit||{},b=cc.staticBatchStats||{};return{compiledItems:b.items||0,compiledPrimitives:b.primitives||0,wallStyles:b.wallStyles||0,entryCacheHits:apexStaticBatchPerf.entryCacheHits,entryCacheMisses:apexStaticBatchPerf.entryCacheMisses,bufferGrows:apexStaticBatchPerf.bufferGrows,webglBufferMB:Number((((webglState&&webglState.bufferBytes)||0)/1048576).toFixed(2)),drawCalls:(webglState&&webglState.triangles>0)?1:0};
}

// v5.230.62.6 · OCCLUSION CULLING CONSERVADOR.
// Sólo se omite decorado secundario cuando su caja proyectada queda ENTERAMENTE dentro
// de la silueta de una masa opaca más cercana. Skyline/boxes/gradas/edificios principales nunca se
// eliminan por esta heurística: ellos mismos siguen usando clipping geométrico exacto.
function apexStaticOccluderType(it){const t=String(it&&it.type||'').toLowerCase();return ['block','pit','grandstand'].includes(t)&&Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3&&Number(it.height||0)>=4;}
function apexStaticOccludeeType(it){const t=String(it&&it.type||'').toLowerCase();return ['tree','billboard','advertising','flagpole','parking'].includes(t)&&!apexIsSkylineLandmark(it);}
function apexBuildStaticOcclusionContext(entries,cam,pitch){
 const occluders=[],infoCache=new Map();if(!Array.isArray(entries)||!entries.length||!cam)return{occluders,infoCache};
 for(const ent of entries){const it=ent.it;if(!apexStaticOccluderType(it))continue;const info=apexLandmarkFootprintClipInfo(it,cam,pitch,4200,.10);infoCache.set(it,info);if(!info||!info.visible||info.depth<1)continue;const w=Math.max(0,info.maxX-info.minX),h=Math.max(0,info.maxY-info.minY),area=w*h;if(area<Math.max(900,W*H*.0045))continue;const inset=Math.max(2,Math.min(16,Math.min(w,h)*.055));if(w<=inset*2+4||h<=inset*2+4)continue;occluders.push({depth:info.depth,minX:info.minX+inset,maxX:info.maxX-inset,minY:info.minY+inset,maxY:info.maxY-inset});}
 occluders.sort((a,b)=>a.depth-b.depth);if(occluders.length>18)occluders.length=18;apexStaticOcclusionPerf.frames++;apexStaticOcclusionPerf.occludersBuilt+=occluders.length;return{occluders,infoCache};
}
function apexStaticCandidateScreenBox(it,base,pitch,cam,ctx){
 if(!it||!base||base.z<=.45)return null;let info=ctx&&ctx.infoCache&&ctx.infoCache.get(it);if(!info&&Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){info=apexLandmarkFootprintClipInfo(it,cam,pitch,2500,.08);if(ctx&&ctx.infoCache)ctx.infoCache.set(it,info)}if(info&&info.visible)return{depth:info.depth,minX:info.minX,maxX:info.maxX,minY:info.minY,maxY:info.maxY};
 const p=projectRoadLocal(base.x,base.y,base.z,pitch);if(!p)return null;const r=Math.max(3,Math.min(75,webglCircuitProjectedPx(base.z,webglLandmarkRadius(it))*.72));return{depth:base.z,minX:p.x-r,maxX:p.x+r,minY:p.y-r*1.35,maxY:p.y+r*1.35};
}
function apexStaticLandmarkOccluded(it,base,cam,pitch,ctx){
 if(!ctx||!ctx.occluders.length||!apexStaticOccludeeType(it))return false;const b=apexStaticCandidateScreenBox(it,base,pitch,cam,ctx);if(!b)return false;apexStaticOcclusionPerf.itemsTested++;const bw=b.maxX-b.minX,bh=b.maxY-b.minY;if(bw*bh>W*H*.075)return false;
 for(const o of ctx.occluders){if(b.depth<=o.depth+Math.max(7,o.depth*.018))continue;if(b.minX>=o.minX&&b.maxX<=o.maxX&&b.minY>=o.minY&&b.maxY<=o.maxY){apexStaticOcclusionPerf.itemsCulled++;return true;}}return false;
}
function apexStaticOcclusionStatsSnapshot(){return{frames:apexStaticOcclusionPerf.frames,occludersBuilt:apexStaticOcclusionPerf.occludersBuilt,itemsTested:apexStaticOcclusionPerf.itemsTested,itemsCulled:apexStaticOcclusionPerf.itemsCulled,primitiveTests:apexStaticOcclusionPerf.primitiveTests,primitiveCulled:apexStaticOcclusionPerf.primitiveCulled};}

function apexCompiledLandmarkEntries(allItems){
 if(!Array.isArray(allItems)||!allItems.length)return[];
 const cc=trackData&&trackData.compiledCircuit,L=trackData&&trackData.km?trackData.km*1000:0;
 if(!cc||!Array.isArray(cc.sectors)||!cc.sectors.length||!player||!L)return allItems.map((it,i)=>({it,i}));
 const n=cc.sectors.length,sectorM=Math.max(24,+cc.sectorM||96),pressure=mobileRenderPressure();
 const localM=mobilePerformanceMode()?lerp(330,700,pressure):lerp(520,1120,pressure),span=Math.min(Math.ceil(n*.48),Math.max(2,Math.ceil(localM/sectorM))),cur=Math.floor(mod(player.s,L)/sectorM)%n;
 // v62.5: la lista sólo cambia al cruzar de sector o variar el span adaptativo. En lugar
 // de reconstruir Set + arrays 60 veces/s, reutilizamos el batch de índices ya resuelto.
 const cache=cc._landmarkEntryCache||(cc._landmarkEntryCache=new Map()),key=cur+':'+span+':'+allItems.length;
 if(cache.has(key)){apexStaticBatchPerf.entryCacheHits++;return cache.get(key)}
 apexStaticBatchPerf.entryCacheMisses++;const set=new Set(cc.skylineLandmarks||[]);
 for(let d=-span;d<=span;d++){const sec=cc.sectors[mod(cur+d,n)];if(!sec)continue;for(const i of sec.landmarks||[])set.add(i)}
 const out=[];for(const i of set){const it=allItems[i];if(it)out.push({it,i})}
 if(cache.size>48)cache.clear();cache.set(key,out);return out;
}
function mobileControlProfile(){
 if(!mobilePerformanceMode()){
   const q=desktopAdaptiveRenderPressure();
   return{
     roadForward:Math.round(lerp(285,500,q)),roadRear:Math.round(lerp(78,210,q)),roadStepMul:lerp(3.05,1.18,q),
     terrainFull:Math.round(lerp(105,260,q)),terrainMid:Math.round(lerp(175,390,q)),terrainMax:Math.round(lerp(255,470,q)),
     rivalMax:Math.round(lerp(360,560,q)),mirrorMax:Math.round(lerp(125,280,q)),mirrorCars:Math.round(lerp(3,7,q)),
     rain:Math.round(lerp(38,88,q)),hudGap:Math.round(lerp(72,45,q)),barrierMax:Math.round(lerp(245,460,q)),
     curbMax:Math.round(lerp(225,340,q)),shadeMax:Math.round(lerp(150,230,q)),edgeMax:Math.round(lerp(250,350,q)),
     pitMax:Math.round(lerp(220,330,q)),horizonRatio:.425,dpr:lerp(1.35,2,q)
   };
 }
 const userT=renderQualityT(),pressure=mobileAdaptiveRenderPressure(),t=clamp(userT*pressure,0,1),ease=t*t*(3-2*t);
 return{
   roadForward:Math.round(lerp(122,470,ease)),
   roadRear:Math.round(lerp(18,205,ease)),
   roadStepMul:lerp(6.35,1.55,ease),
   terrainFull:Math.round(lerp(0,135,ease)),
   terrainMid:Math.round(lerp(48,225,ease)),
   terrainMax:Math.round(lerp(82,330,ease)),
   rivalMax:Math.round(lerp(145,430,ease)),
   mirrorMax:Math.round(lerp(44,220,ease)),
   mirrorCars:Math.max(1,Math.round(lerp(1,5,ease))),
   rain:Math.max(8,Math.round(lerp(8,68,ease))),
   hudGap:Math.round(lerp(180,58,ease)),
   barrierMax:Math.round(lerp(108,350,ease)),
   curbMax:Math.round(lerp(126,315,ease)),
   shadeMax:Math.round(lerp(62,205,ease)),
   edgeMax:Math.round(lerp(148,350,ease)),
   pitMax:Math.round(lerp(125,315,ease)),
   horizonRatio:lerp(.382,.422,ease),
   dpr:Math.min(mobileDprCap(),lerp(1.0,mobileDprCap(),ease))
 };
}
let renderFrameId=0;
function sceneHFov(){
 if(typeof apexInspectionActive==='function'&&apexInspectionActive())return clamp(apexInspection.fov||72,38,105)*Math.PI/180;
 // v5.94: ligeramente más apertura lateral para recuperar la sensación de pantalla completa.
 // El canvas ya ocupaba todo el ancho; la sensación estrecha venía de la calibración baja del ojo + FOV conservador.
 const base=mobileLandscapeMode()?84:(compactMode()?85.5:79.5);
 const boost=player?clamp(finite(player.fovBoost,0),0,4.2):0;
 return (base+boost)*Math.PI/180;
}

// v5.50: physical driver-eye reference.
// The projection previously behaved as if the eyes were around 0.82-0.84 m above the asphalt.
// We now calibrate the visual origin to a low seated single-seater driver position.
function driverEyeHeight(){
 if(typeof apexInspectionActive==='function'&&apexInspectionActive())return 0;
 if(mobileLandscapeMode()) return shortLandscapeMode()?.685:.695;
 if(compactMode()) return .690;
 return .700;
}
function sceneHorizon(camPitch=0){ if(apexInspectionActive())return H*.5; const mr=mobilePerformanceMode()?mobileControlProfile().horizonRatio:null;return H*(mr!==null?mr:(mobileLandscapeMode()?.385:(compactMode()?.405:.425)))+camPitch; }
function projectLocal(x,y,z,camPitch=0){
 const horizon=sceneHorizon(camPitch);
 const hfov=sceneHFov();
 const f=(W*.5)/Math.tan(hfov*.5);
 const eyeH=driverEyeHeight();
 if(z<=.45)return null;
 return{x:W*.5+f*x/z,y:horizon+f*(eyeH-y)/z,scale:f/z};
}

function roundedPath(c,x,y,w,h,r){
 r=Math.max(0,Math.min(r,Math.min(w,h)/2));
 if(typeof c.roundRect==="function"){c.roundRect(x,y,w,h,r);return}
 c.moveTo(x+r,y);c.lineTo(x+w-r,y);c.quadraticCurveTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+r);
 c.lineTo(x+w,y+h-r);c.quadraticCurveTo(x+w,y+h,x+w-r,y+h);
 c.lineTo(x+r,y+h);c.quadraticCurveTo(x,y+h,x,y+h-r);
 c.lineTo(x,y+r);c.quadraticCurveTo(x,y,x+r,y);
}
function poly(p,fill){ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p[0][0],p[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<p.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(p[i][0],p[i][1]);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill()}
function mixPt(a,b,t){return{x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t}}
function drawStartFinish(cam,pitch,baseS=null){
 if(!trackData)return;apexDrawSectorSurface({s:Number.isFinite(baseS)?baseS:apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec).finish},cam,pitch);
}
function drawRaceStartLine(cam,pitch,baseS=null){
 if(!trackData)return;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec),sBase=mod(Number.isFinite(baseS)?baseS:sf.start,L),env=envProfile(sBase),half=env.half-.06,th=.18;
 fillTrackQuad([[sBase-th*.5,half],[sBase-th*.5,-half],[sBase+th*.5,-half],[sBase+th*.5,half]],cam,pitch,'#f4f5f6');
}
function drawCurbQuad(o0,i0,i1,o1,seed=0){
 const len=(Math.hypot(i1.x-i0.x,i1.y-i0.y)+Math.hypot(o1.x-o0.x,o1.y-o0.y))*.5;
 poly([[o0.x,o0.y],[i0.x,i0.y],[i1.x,i1.y],[o1.x,o1.y]],"#e7e8ea");
 const steps=Math.max(3,Math.min(14,Math.round(len/14)));
 for(let i=0;i<steps;i++){
   const t0=i/steps,t1=(i+1)/steps,A=mixPt(o0,o1,t0),B=mixPt(i0,i1,t0),C=mixPt(i0,i1,t1),D=mixPt(o0,o1,t1);
   const red=((seed+i)&1)===0,col=red?"#d93b49":"#f7f7f7";
   poly([[A.x,A.y],[B.x,B.y],[C.x,C.y],[D.x,D.y]],col);
 }
 ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(18,20,24,.28)";
 ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,Math.min(3,len*.016));
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(o0.x,o0.y);ctx.lineTo(o1.x,o1.y);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.22)";
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(i0.x,i0.y);ctx.lineTo(i1.x,i1.y);ctx.stroke();
 const m0=mixPt(o0,i0,.52),m1=mixPt(o1,i1,.52);
 ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(0,0,0,.10)";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,Math.min(2,len*.010));ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(m0.x,m0.y);ctx.lineTo(m1.x,m1.y);ctx.stroke();
}

function worldAnchor(s,lat=0,along=0,yOff=0){
 const ss=trackData?mod(s+along,trackData.km*1000):s+along,q=poseAt(ss);
 return {x:q.x+q.nx*lat+q.tx*along,z:q.z+q.nz*lat+q.tz*along,y:q.y+yOff,q,s:ss};
}
function proposalWorldAnchor(s,lat=0,yawDeg=0,maxRenderDistance=1750){
 const a=worldAnchor(s,lat,0,0),g=a.q||poseAt(s),ang=yawDeg*Math.PI/180,c=Math.cos(ang),sn=Math.sin(ang);
 a.groundQ=g;
 a.q={...g,tx:g.tx*c+g.nx*sn,tz:g.tz*c+g.nz*sn,nx:-g.tx*sn+g.nx*c,nz:-g.tz*sn+g.nz*c};
 a.maxRenderDistance=maxRenderDistance;
 return a;
}
function absoluteProposalAnchor(wx,wz,s=0,maxRenderDistance=1750){
 const g=poseAt(s),a={x:wx,z:wz,y:g.y,q:{...g},groundQ:g,s:mod(s,trackData.km*1000),maxRenderDistance};
 return a;
}
function worldDistanceToTrack(wx,wz,step=3){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||!trackData.pts.length)return Infinity;
 let best2=Infinity;
 for(let i=0;i<trackData.pts.length;i+=Math.max(1,step)){
   const p=trackData.pts[i],dx=wx-p[0],dz=wz-p[1],d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;
   if(d2<best2)best2=d2;
 }
 return Math.sqrt(best2);
}
function safeWorldAnchor(s,lat=0,along=0,yOff=0,minClear=90){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.spec)return worldAnchor(s,lat,along,yOff);
 const L=trackData.km*1000,dir=lat>=0?1:-1;
 let testLat=lat||dir*minClear,best=null;
 for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
   const a=worldAnchor(mod(s,L),testLat,along,yOff),d=worldDistanceToTrack(a.x,a.z,2);
   best=a;best._trackClearance=d;
   if(d>=minClear)return a;
   testLat += dir*Math.max(14, minClear-d+12);
 }
 return best||worldAnchor(s,lat,along,yOff);
}

function genericTreeCluster(landmarks,L,points,latBase,height=12,width=5.2,clear=44,foliage="#2f6e41"){
 for(const f of points){landmarks.push({type:"tree",anchor:safeWorldAnchor(L*f,latBase,0,0,clear),height:height+(f%1)*1.8,width:width+((f*10)%1)*.9,foliage});}
}
function buildGenericCircuitWorldProfile(id,L){
 const env=circuitEnvironmentProfile(id),landmarks=[],brakeBoards=[];
 const pitSide=(trackData&&trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.pit&&Number.isFinite(trackData.spec.pit.side))?trackData.spec.pit.side:-1;
 const pitOffset=(trackData&&trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.pit&&Number.isFinite(trackData.spec.pit.offset))?trackData.spec.pit.offset:12;
 const labels=apexPreviewNamedSectionDefs(id);
 const family=env.family||"PARK";
 const cj=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},cc=cj.circuit||{};
 landmarks.push({type:"gantry",anchor:worldAnchor(0,0,0,0),span:18.5,height:5.9,text:cc.hudName||cc.displayName||cc.name||((calendarSpecById(id)||{}).name||"START / FINISH")});
 landmarks.push({type:"pit",anchor:worldAnchor(68,pitSide*(pitOffset+6.5),0,0),length:132,depth:18,height:11.2,side:pitSide,color:family==="URBAN"||family==="NIGHT"?"#7b848b":"#747d84"});
 landmarks.push({type:"grandstand",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.018,26,0,0),length:96,height:12.6,depth:14,side:1,name:"Main Grandstand"});
 landmarks.push({type:"grandstand",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.052,30,0,0),length:72,height:11.2,depth:12,side:1,name:"Start Grandstand"});
 if(env.stands>=3)landmarks.push({type:"grandstand",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.84,-24,0,0),length:58,height:10.4,depth:12,side:-1,name:"Final Sector"});
 if(env.billboards>=1)landmarks.push({type:"billboard",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.21,10,0,0),width:14,height:4.4,side:1,label:"APEX"});
 if(env.billboards>=2)landmarks.push({type:"billboard",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.57,-11,0,0),width:14,height:4.4,side:-1,label:"FORMULA"});
 if(env.billboards>=3)landmarks.push({type:"billboard",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.89,10,0,0),width:13.5,height:4.2,side:1,label:"RACING"});
 if(env.generatedTunnelFallback===true){
   landmarks.push({type:"tunnelPortal",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.118,-2,0,0),span:18,height:8.2});
   landmarks.push({type:"tunnelPortal",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.146,-2,0,0),span:18,height:8.2});
 }
 if(family==="FOREST"||family==="PARK"||family==="TEMPERATE"||family==="MOUNTAIN"||family==="TROPICAL"){
   genericTreeCluster(landmarks,L,[.14,.17,.21],58,12.6,5.3,42,family==="TROPICAL"?"#36784a":"#2f6e41");
   genericTreeCluster(landmarks,L,[.46,.50],-68,13.4,5.8,54,family==="MOUNTAIN"?"#325d39":"#316841");
   genericTreeCluster(landmarks,L,[.64,.68,.72],62,13.0,5.5,48,family==="FOREST"?"#2f6b40":"#3c7a4a");
   if(env.veg>=3)genericTreeCluster(landmarks,L,[.92,.95],74,12.5,5.2,66,family==="TROPICAL"?"#3d8b53":"#376f45");
 }
 if(family==="URBAN"||family==="NIGHT"||family==="SUNSET"){
   landmarks.push({type:"block",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.33,-36,0,0),width:24,depth:14,height:10.5,side:-1,color:"#919aa2"});
   landmarks.push({type:"tower",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.48,-82,0,0),height:family==="NIGHT"?34:24,width:8,color:"#7d8790"});
   landmarks.push({type:"block",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.52,-58,0,0),width:18,depth:12,height:8.2,side:-1,color:"#8d97a0"});
   if(env.urban>=4)landmarks.push({type:"tower",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.78,66,0,0),height:28,width:7,color:"#7b868f"});
 }
 if(family==="DUNES"){
   landmarks.push({type:"grandstand",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.19,24,0,0),length:56,height:10.0,depth:11,side:1,name:"Dunes"});
   landmarks.push({type:"grandstand",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.91,-26,0,0),length:74,height:12.8,depth:14,side:-1,name:"Arena"});
 }
 if(family==="MOUNTAIN"){
   landmarks.push({type:"tower",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.59,76,0,0),height:32,width:8,color:"#7d8892"});
   landmarks.push({type:"block",anchor:worldAnchor(L*.61,52,0,0),width:18,depth:10,height:8,side:1,color:"#8f989f"});
 }
 const authoredBaseLandmarks=Array.isArray((cj.environment||{}).baseLandmarks)?cj.environment.baseLandmarks:[];
 for(const d of authoredBaseLandmarks){const copy={...d},f=clamp(+copy.lapFraction||0,0,1),lat=+copy.lat||0;delete copy.lapFraction;delete copy.lat;landmarks.push({...copy,anchor:worldAnchor(L*f,lat,0,0)})}
 const brakeMarksBase=(family==="MOUNTAIN"||family==="FOREST")?[150,100,50]:[200,150,100,50];
 for(const lab of labels.slice(0,Math.min(5,labels.length))){
   const side=(Number.isFinite(+lab[2])?+lab[2]:12)>=0?1:-1,corner=mod(L*(+lab[1]||0),L);
   for(const mark of brakeMarksBase)brakeBoards.push({s:mod(corner-mark,L),side,mark,width:2.9,height:3.4,latExtra:1.25});
 }
 return {landmarks,brakeBoards};
}

function buildCircuitWorldProfile(){
 if(!trackData)return {landmarks:[],brakeBoards:[]};
 if(trackData.customWorld)return trackData.customWorld;
 return {authoritative:true,landmarks:[],brakeBoards:[],brakeBoardsAuthoritative:false,cornerMarkers:[],pitLaneSamples:[],pitNetwork:null,wallLeftSamples:[],wallRightSamples:[],wallSegments:[],edgeLeftSamples:[],edgeRightSamples:[],structureZones:[],barrierSegments:[],surfaceZones:[],terrainContours:[],terrainContoursAuthoritative:false,drivableArea:null};
}
function circuitWorldProfile(){
 if(!trackData)return{landmarks:[],brakeBoards:[]};
 if(!trackData.world)trackData.world=buildCircuitWorldProfile();
 return trackData.world;
}
function authoredBrakeBoards(){ return circuitWorldProfile().brakeBoards; }
function trackLandmarks(){
 if(!trackData)return [];
 const profile=circuitWorldProfile();
 if(profile.landmarks && profile.landmarks.length) return profile.landmarks;
 return [];
}

// v5.230.32 · Recorte de huellas SVG por bandas laterales. Se usa para crear
// graderíos escalonados reales sin sustituir la planta dibujada por un rectángulo estándar.
function apexClipWorldPolygonScalar(poly,scalar,bound,keepGreater){
 const out=[];if(!Array.isArray(poly)||poly.length<3)return out;
 const inside=p=>keepGreater?scalar(p)>=bound:scalar(p)<=bound;
 for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){
   const A=poly[i],B=poly[(i+1)%poly.length],aIn=inside(A),bIn=inside(B),sa=scalar(A),sb=scalar(B);
   if(aIn)out.push(A);
   if(aIn!==bIn){const den=sb-sa,t=Math.abs(den)<1e-9?0:clamp((bound-sa)/den,0,1);out.push([lerp(A[0],B[0],t),lerp(A[1],B[1],t)]);}
 }
 return out;
}
function apexGrandstandFrontEdge(it){
 const fp=it&&it.worldFootprint;if(!Array.isArray(fp)||fp.length<2)return null;let best=null;
 for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){
  const j=(i+1)%fp.length,a=fp[i],b=fp[j],mx=(a[0]+b[0])*.5,mz=(a[1]+b[1])*.5,len=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);if(len<1)continue;
  const trk=nearestTrackState(mx,mz,null,{radius:360,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:260}),d=trk&&Number.isFinite(trk.dist)?trk.dist:1e6,score=d-len*.010;
  if(!best||score<best.score)best={i,j,a,b,len,mx,mz,score,trk};
 }
 if(!best)return null;let cx=0,cz=0;for(const p of fp){cx+=p[0];cz+=p[1]}cx/=fp.length;cz/=fp.length;
 const dx=best.b[0]-best.a[0],dz=best.b[1]-best.a[1],ln=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,tx=dx/ln,tz=dz/ln;let nx=-tz,nz=tx;
 if((best.mx+nx-cx)**2+(best.mz+nz-cz)**2 > (best.mx-nx-cx)**2+(best.mz-nz-cz)**2){nx=-nx;nz=-nz}
 return {...best,tx,tz,nx,nz,cx,cz};
}
function apexGrandstandBand(fp,anchor,side,t0,t1,it=null){
 if(!Array.isArray(fp)||fp.length<3)return[];
 const edge=it&&(it._grandstandFrontEdge||(it._grandstandFrontEdge=apexGrandstandFrontEdge(it)));
 let scalar;
 if(edge)scalar=p=>(p[0]-edge.mx)*edge.nx + (p[1]-edge.mz)*edge.nz;
 else {const q=anchor.q||poseAt(anchor.s||0),sg=side||1;scalar=p=>sg*((p[0]-anchor.x)*q.nx+(p[1]-anchor.z)*q.nz);}
 const vals=fp.map(scalar),lo=Math.min(...vals),hi=Math.max(...vals),den=Math.max(.001,hi-lo),b0=lo+den*clamp(t0,0,1),b1=lo+den*clamp(t1,0,1);let out=apexClipWorldPolygonScalar(fp,scalar,b0,true);out=apexClipWorldPolygonScalar(out,scalar,b1,false);return out;
}

function apexGrandstandSeatVisual(prof,ri,rows,ci=0){
 const empty=ri%3===0?(prof.seatAlt||prof.seat):ri%3===1?(prof.seatAlt2||prof.seatAlt||prof.seat):(prof.seat||prof.base),att=apexGrandstandAttendanceProfile();
 if(!att.crowd||att.fill<=.01)return{top:empty,alt:shadeColor(empty,8),occupied:false,empty};

}

}

function apexSceneDetailBudget(){
 if(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())return {mobile:false,q:1,fm:16.7,renderMs:0,pressure:0,density:1,detailScale:1,farScale:1};
 const mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),q=clamp(Number(renderQualityT())||0,0,1),fm=Math.max(0,Number(telemetryPerf&&telemetryPerf.frameMs)||16.7),rm=Math.max(0,Number(telemetryPerf&&telemetryPerf.renderMs)||0);
 let pressure=0;
 if(fm>46||rm>34)pressure=3;else if(fm>34||rm>25)pressure=2;else if(fm>25||rm>18)pressure=1;
 let density=mobile?.78:1,detailScale=1,farScale=1;
 if(q<.68){density*=.86;detailScale*=.88;farScale*=.92}if(q<.42){density*=.78;detailScale*=.80;farScale*=.84}if(q<.24){density*=.70;detailScale*=.72;farScale*=.76}
 if(pressure===1){density*=.82;detailScale*=.84;farScale*=.90}
 else if(pressure===2){density*=.62;detailScale*=.68;farScale*=.78}
 else if(pressure>=3){density*=.44;detailScale*=.52;farScale*=.66}
 return {mobile,q,fm,renderMs:rm,pressure,density:clamp(density,.28,1),detailScale:clamp(detailScale,.42,1),farScale:clamp(farScale,.58,1)};
}
function apexSceneryDensity(type='',ds=999){
 if(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())return 1;
 const b=apexSceneDetailBudget(),t=String(type||'').toLowerCase();let d=b.density;
 if(ds<120)return 1;
 if(['tree','flagpole','billboard','advertising','parking','ferris','forestzone'].includes(t)){
   if(ds>900*b.farScale)d*=.58;else if(ds>520*b.farScale)d*=.76;
 }
 return clamp(d,.20,1);
}
function apexObjectLodBudget(type,lod,ds=999){

 if(['road','tunnel','tunnelportal','barrier','bridge','pit'].includes(t))return out;
 if(b.pressure>=3&&ds>170)out=Math.max(out,2);else if(b.pressure>=2&&ds>240)out=Math.max(out,2);else if(b.pressure>=1&&ds>420)out=Math.max(out,2);
 if((t==='grandstand'||t==='block'||t==='building'||t==='tower')&&b.detailScale<.72&&ds>260)out=Math.max(out,1);
 return clamp(out,0,3);
}
function apexSceneryShouldRender(it,index=0,ds=999,z=999){

 const d=apexSceneryDensity(t,ds),seed=apexStringHash(String(it.sourceId||it.name||t)+'|'+index),r=((seed>>>0)&65535)/65535;

}

 if(tier===2)return{tier,crowdRows:6,crowdCols:9,stairs:true,braces:true,vip:true,flags:true,roofSupports:true,crowdHeight:1.10};
 if(tier===1)return{tier,crowdRows:3,crowdCols:6,stairs:false,braces:true,vip:false,flags:false,roofSupports:true,crowdHeight:1.05};

}

function apexPitFrontEdge(it){
 const fp=it&&it.worldFootprint;if(!Array.isArray(fp)||fp.length<2)return null;let best=null;
 for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){
  const j=(i+1)%fp.length,a=fp[i],b=fp[j],mx=(a[0]+b[0])*.5,mz=(a[1]+b[1])*.5,len=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);if(len<1)continue;

 }

}
