function apexWebglRenderPitExact(it,cam,pitch,lod){
 const fp=it.worldFootprint;if(!Array.isArray(fp)||fp.length<3)return false;const prof=apexPitStyleProfile(it),h=Math.min(Math.max(5,+it.height||18),48),clear=Math.max(0,+it.clearance||0),bp=[],tp=[];
 for(const v of fp){const y=apexPitGroundAt(it,v[0],v[1])+clear;bp.push(worldToCamera(v[0],y,v[1],cam));tp.push(worldToCamera(v[0],y+h,v[1],cam))}
 webglPushCamPoly(tp,pitch,prof.roof,.60,-.008);for(let j=0;j<bp.length;j++){const k=(j+1)%bp.length;webglPushCamQuad(bp[j],bp[k],tp[k],tp[j],pitch,j%2?prof.side:prof.body,.60)}
 if(lod>=2)return true;const edge=apexPitFrontEdge(it);if(!edge)return true;const bays=Math.max(2,Math.min(48,Math.round(+it.garageBays||edge.len/9))),doorTop=h*.29,glass0=h*.40,glass1=h*.68;
 for(let k=0;k<bays;k++){
  const t0=(k+.08)/bays,t1=(k+.92)/bays,p0=apexPitEdgeWorld(edge,t0,.06),p1=apexPitEdgeWorld(edge,t1,.06),y0=apexPitGroundAt(it,p0[0],p0[1])+clear+.05,y1=apexPitGroundAt(it,p1[0],p1[1])+clear+.05;
  const A=worldToCamera(p0[0],y0,p0[1],cam),B=worldToCamera(p1[0],y1,p1[1],cam),C=worldToCamera(p1[0],y1+doorTop,p1[1],cam),D=worldToCamera(p0[0],y0+doorTop,p0[1],cam);webglPushCamQuad(A,B,C,D,pitch,(k&1)?prof.garageAlt:prof.garage,.60,-.014);
  if(it.hasGlass!==false){const G0=worldToCamera(p0[0],y0+glass0,p0[1],cam),G1=worldToCamera(p1[0],y1+glass0,p1[1],cam),G2=worldToCamera(p1[0],y1+glass1,p1[1],cam),G3=worldToCamera(p0[0],y0+glass1,p0[1],cam);webglPushCamQuad(G0,G1,G2,G3,pitch,(k&1)?prof.glassAlt:prof.glass,.60,-.016)}
 }
 // Banda estructural y balcón continuo sobre los garajes.
 const e0=apexPitEdgeWorld(edge,.01,.10),e1=apexPitEdgeWorld(edge,.99,.10),yy0=apexPitGroundAt(it,e0[0],e0[1])+clear,yy1=apexPitGroundAt(it,e1[0],e1[1])+clear;
 webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(e0[0],yy0+h*.31,e0[1],cam),worldToCamera(e1[0],yy1+h*.31,e1[1],cam),worldToCamera(e1[0],yy1+h*.36,e1[1],cam),worldToCamera(e0[0],yy0+h*.36,e0[1],cam),pitch,prof.frame,.60,-.018);
 if(it.hasBalcony!==false){const bo=1.35,q0=apexPitEdgeWorld(edge,.02,bo),q1=apexPitEdgeWorld(edge,.98,bo);webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(e0[0],yy0+h*.70,e0[1],cam),worldToCamera(e1[0],yy1+h*.70,e1[1],cam),worldToCamera(q1[0],apexPitGroundAt(it,q1[0],q1[1])+clear+h*.70,q1[1],cam),worldToCamera(q0[0],apexPitGroundAt(it,q0[0],q0[1])+clear+h*.70,q0[1],cam),pitch,prof.balcony,.60,-.020)}
 // Parapeto superior.
 webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(e0[0],yy0+h*.91,e0[1],cam),worldToCamera(e1[0],yy1+h*.91,e1[1],cam),worldToCamera(e1[0],yy1+h,e1[1],cam),worldToCamera(e0[0],yy0+h,e0[1],cam),pitch,prof.parapet,.60,-.018);
 // Pit wall independiente, paralelo al edificio y gobernado por SVG.
 if(it.hasPitwall!==false&&lod===0){const off=4.6,w0=apexPitEdgeWorld(edge,.03,off),w1=apexPitEdgeWorld(edge,.97,off),wy0=apexPitGroundAt(it,w0[0],w0[1]),wy1=apexPitGroundAt(it,w1[0],w1[1]),wh=1.05;webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(w0[0],wy0,w0[1],cam),worldToCamera(w1[0],wy1,w1[1],cam),worldToCamera(w1[0],wy1+wh,w1[1],cam),worldToCamera(w0[0],wy0+wh,w0[1],cam),pitch,prof.pitwall,.60,-.022)}
 return true;
}


function apexBuildingStyleProfile(it){
 const s=String(it&&(it.buildingStyle||it.apexStyle||it.style)||'building_concrete').toLowerCase();
 const base={style:s,body:'#aab2b9',side:'#959da4',roof:'#d9dee2',roofDark:'#c0c6cb',glass:'#7fa9bc',glassAlt:'#95bfd1',window:'#97a9b3',windowAlt:'#b0c0c9',accent:'#5c6e7c',balcony:'#b7bdc2',door:'#575f66',hvac:'#99a1a8',floors:4,windowMode:'grid',hasBalcony:false,hvacCount:2,roofStyle:(it&&it.roofStyle)||'flat',baseBand:'#6c747a',entry:'#4e5962',parapet:'#c4c9cd',nightWarm:'#d8b96b',nightCool:'#8fb6c9'};
 if(s==='building_glass_office')return {...base,body:'#8f989f',side:'#7b858d',roof:'#dce3e8',roofDark:'#bcc6cd',glass:'#6d9fba',glassAlt:'#8ab8cf',window:'#78abc6',windowAlt:'#93c2d8',accent:'#3e5b6f',floors:Math.max(5,Math.round((it&&it.height||22)/4.2)),windowMode:'glass',hvacCount:2};
 if(s==='building_industrial')return {...base,body:'#8d9398',side:'#788086',roof:'#c4c9cd',roofDark:'#adb4b9',glass:'#6f8793',glassAlt:'#819aa6',window:'#97a4ab',windowAlt:'#a7b2b8',accent:'#6f7780',door:'#454b51',floors:Math.max(2,Math.round((it&&it.height||14)/5.5)),windowMode:'industrial',hvacCount:4};
 if(s==='building_residential')return {...base,body:'#c8c0b8',side:'#b1aaa3',roof:'#d5d7d9',roofDark:'#bdbfc2',glass:'#9eb7c8',glassAlt:'#b2cad8',window:'#ebdda3',windowAlt:'#f3e7bd',accent:'#8f7466',balcony:'#b9bbb8',floors:Math.max(4,Math.round((it&&it.height||18)/3.4)),windowMode:'residential',hasBalcony:true,hvacCount:1};
 if(s==='building_hospitality')return {...base,body:'#d2d6d9',side:'#bcc1c5',roof:'#e4e8ea',roofDark:'#c7ccd0',glass:'#7fa8bd',glassAlt:'#9bc0d1',window:'#87b1c7',windowAlt:'#9bc0d1',accent:'#7d8d97',balcony:'#d7dde0',floors:Math.max(5,Math.round((it&&it.height||24)/3.8)),windowMode:'hospitality',hasBalcony:true,hvacCount:2};
 if(s==='building_tower')return {...base,body:'#7e8790',side:'#69737b',roof:'#c5ccd1',roofDark:'#aeb6bc',glass:'#679ab6',glassAlt:'#80aec6',window:'#6fa7c2',windowAlt:'#8ab7cd',accent:'#516775',floors:Math.max(8,Math.round((it&&it.height||38)/3.5)),windowMode:'tower',hvacCount:3};
 return {...base,body:'#b0b5b8',side:'#9aa1a6',roof:'#d7dcdf',roofDark:'#bcc3c8',glass:'#8298a5',glassAlt:'#93a7b3',window:'#8f9ea7',windowAlt:'#a9b6bd',accent:'#6f787f',floors:Math.max(3,Math.round((it&&it.height||16)/4.5)),windowMode:'concrete',hvacCount:2};
}
function apexBuildingRenderBudget(lod,detail=1){
 const scene=apexSceneDetailBudget(),mobile=scene.mobile,q=scene.q,fm=scene.fm,d=clamp(Number(detail)||0,0,1);
 let budget=lod<=0?2:(lod===1?1:0);
 if(d<.16)budget=Math.min(budget,0);else if(d<.42)budget=Math.min(budget,1);
 if(mobile&&q<.62)budget--;if(q<.34)budget--;
 if(scene.pressure>=3)budget-=2;else if(scene.pressure>=1)budget--;
 return clamp(Math.round(budget),0,2);
}
function apexBuildingFrontEdge(it){
 const fp=it&&it.worldFootprint;if(!Array.isArray(fp)||fp.length<2)return null;let best=null;
 for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){
  const j=(i+1)%fp.length,a=fp[i],b=fp[j],mx=(a[0]+b[0])*.5,mz=(a[1]+b[1])*.5,len=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);if(len<1)continue;
  const pit=nearestPitState(mx,mz,null),trk=nearestTrackState(mx,mz,null,{radius:260,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:220}),d=pit&&Number.isFinite(pit.dist)?pit.dist:(trk&&Number.isFinite(trk.dist)?trk.dist:1e6),score=d-len*.015;
  if(!best||score<best.score)best={i,j,a,b,len,mx,mz,score};
 }
 if(!best)return null;let cx=0,cz=0;for(const p of fp){cx+=p[0];cz+=p[1]}cx/=fp.length;cz/=fp.length;
 const dx=best.b[0]-best.a[0],dz=best.b[1]-best.a[1],ln=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,tx=dx/ln,tz=dz/ln;let nx=-tz,nz=tx;
 const emx=best.mx,emz=best.mz;if((emx+nx-cx)**2+(emz+nz-cz)**2 < (emx-nx-cx)**2+(emz-nz-cz)**2){nx=-nx;nz=-nz}
 return {...best,tx,tz,nx,nz,cx,cz};
}
function apexBuildingFrameStats(it){
 const fp=it&&it.worldFootprint,edge=it&&(it._buildingFrontEdge||(it._buildingFrontEdge=apexBuildingFrontEdge(it)));
 if(!edge||!Array.isArray(fp)||fp.length<3)return null;let depth=0;
 for(const p of fp)depth=Math.max(depth,(p[0]-edge.a[0])*edge.nx + (p[1]-edge.a[1])*edge.nz);
 return {edge,span:edge.len||Math.hypot(edge.b[0]-edge.a[0],edge.b[1]-edge.a[1])||10,depth:Math.max(4,depth)};
}
function apexBuildingFrameXZ(frame,u,depth){const e=frame.edge||frame;return [lerp(e.a[0],e.b[0],u)+e.nx*depth,lerp(e.a[1],e.b[1],u)+e.nz*depth]}
function apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u,depth,y,cam){const p=apexBuildingFrameXZ(frame,u,depth),yy=apexWorldGroundY(p[0],p[1])+y;return worldToCamera(p[0],yy,p[1],cam)}
function apexRenderBuildingExact2D(it,cam,detailAlpha,s,polyCamFn){
 const fp=it&&it.worldFootprint;if(!Array.isArray(fp)||fp.length<3)return false;
 const prof=apexBuildingStyleProfile(it),h=Math.min(Math.max(6,+it.height||14),90),clear=Math.max(0,+it.clearance||0),frame=apexBuildingFrameStats(it),budget=apexBuildingRenderBudget(detailAlpha>.58?0:(detailAlpha>.18?1:2),detailAlpha),baseY=[];
 const bp=[],tp=[];for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){const v=fp[i],gy=(Array.isArray(it.worldY)&&Number.isFinite(it.worldY[i])?it.worldY[i]:apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1]))+clear;baseY.push(gy);bp.push(worldToCamera(v[0],gy,v[1],cam));tp.push(worldToCamera(v[0],gy+h,v[1],cam));}
 polyCamFn(bp,shadeColor(prof.body,-20),null,1,1);for(let j=0;j<fp.length;j++){const k=(j+1)%fp.length;polyCamFn([bp[j],bp[k],tp[k],tp[j]],j&1?prof.side:prof.body,null,1,1)}polyCamFn(tp,prof.roof,'rgba(255,255,255,.14)',1,1);
 if(budget<0)return true;
 const rows=Math.max(2,Math.min(prof.floors,budget===2?prof.floors:Math.max(2,Math.round(prof.floors*.68))));
 for(let j=0;j<fp.length;j++){
   const k=(j+1)%fp.length,v0=fp[j],v1=fp[k],g0=baseY[j],g1=baseY[k],len=Math.hypot(v1[0]-v0[0],v1[1]-v0[1]);if(len<5)continue;
   const cols=Math.max(1,Math.min(budget===2?10:6,Math.round(len/(prof.windowMode==='tower'?4.0:(prof.windowMode==='industrial'?5.4:4.6)))));
   for(let r=0;r<rows;r++){
     const yy0=h*(.14 + (r/rows)*.70),yy1=h*(.14 + ((r+.64)/rows)*.70);
     const rowCol=(r&1)?prof.windowAlt:prof.window;
     if(prof.windowMode==='glass' || prof.windowMode==='tower' || prof.windowMode==='hospitality'){
       const band0=.04, band1=.96, x0=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],band0), z0=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],band0), x1=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],band1), z1=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],band1), gy0=lerp(g0,g1,band0), gy1=lerp(g0,g1,band1);
       polyCamFn([worldToCamera(x0,gy0+yy0,z0,cam),worldToCamera(x1,gy1+yy0,z1,cam),worldToCamera(x1,gy1+yy1,z1,cam),worldToCamera(x0,gy0+yy1,z0,cam)],(r&1)?prof.glassAlt:prof.glass,null,1,.98);
       if(budget===2&&cols>2){for(let c=1;c<cols;c++){const tt=c/cols,x=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],tt),z=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],tt),gy=lerp(g0,g1,tt);polyCamFn([worldToCamera(x-.01,gy+yy0,z-.01,cam),worldToCamera(x+.01,gy+yy0,z+.01,cam),worldToCamera(x+.01,gy+yy1,z+.01,cam),worldToCamera(x-.01,gy+yy1,z-.01,cam)],prof.accent,null,1,.99)}}
     }else{
       for(let c=0;c<cols;c++){const t0=(c+.12)/cols,t1=(c+.88)/cols,x0=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],t0),z0=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],t0),x1=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],t1),z1=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],t1),gy0=lerp(g0,g1,t0),gy1=lerp(g0,g1,t1);polyCamFn([worldToCamera(x0,gy0+yy0,z0,cam),worldToCamera(x1,gy1+yy0,z1,cam),worldToCamera(x1,gy1+yy1,z1,cam),worldToCamera(x0,gy0+yy1,z0,cam)],(c&1&&budget===2)?prof.windowAlt:rowCol,null,1,.98);}
       if(prof.hasBalcony&&budget>=1){const bb0=h*(.14 + (r/rows)*.70)-h*.012,bb1=bb0+h*.028;polyCamFn([worldToCamera(v0[0],g0+bb0,v0[1],cam),worldToCamera(v1[0],g1+bb0,v1[1],cam),worldToCamera(v1[0],g1+bb1,v1[1],cam),worldToCamera(v0[0],g0+bb1,v0[1],cam)],prof.balcony,null,1,.97)}
     }
   }
 }
 if(frame){
   const dep=frame.depth,span=frame.span;const e=frame.edge;
   if(prof.windowMode==='industrial'&&budget>=1){const u0=.08,u1=.92,d=.04,y0=.05*h,y1=.34*h,p0=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u0,d,y0,cam),p1=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u1,d,y0,cam),p2=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u1,d,y1,cam),p3=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u0,d,y1,cam);polyCamFn([p0,p1,p2,p3],prof.door,null,1,.99)}
   if(budget>=1){const hv=Math.max(1,Math.min(prof.hvacCount,budget===2?4:2));for(let i=0;i<hv;i++){const u=(i+.5)/hv,w=.05*span,d0=dep*.56,d1=dep*.74,y0=h+.05,y1=h+.55;const q0=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u-.04,d0,y0,cam),q1=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u+.04,d0,y0,cam),q2=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u+.04,d1,y0,cam),q3=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u-.04,d1,y0,cam),q4=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u-.04,d0,y1,cam),q5=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u+.04,d0,y1,cam),q6=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u+.04,d1,y1,cam),q7=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u-.04,d1,y1,cam);polyCamFn([q0,q1,q5,q4],prof.hvac,null,1,.96);polyCamFn([q1,q2,q6,q5],shadeColor(prof.hvac,-8),null,1,.95);polyCamFn([q0,q3,q7,q4],shadeColor(prof.hvac,-15),null,1,.95);polyCamFn([q4,q5,q6,q7],shadeColor(prof.hvac,8),null,1,.98);}}
   const rf0=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,-.02,dep*.08,h,cam),rf1=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,1.02,dep*.08,h,cam),rb1=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,1.02,dep*1.02,h+.32,cam),rb0=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,-.02,dep*1.02,h+.32,cam);polyCamFn([rf0,rf1,rb1,rb0],prof.roofDark,null,1,.96);
  }
 return true;
}
function apexWebglRenderBuildingExact(it,cam,pitch,lod){
 const fp=it&&it.worldFootprint;if(!Array.isArray(fp)||fp.length<3)return false;
 const prof=apexBuildingStyleProfile(it),h=Math.min(Math.max(6,+it.height||14),90),clear=Math.max(0,+it.clearance||0),frame=apexBuildingFrameStats(it),budget=apexBuildingRenderBudget(lod,lod===0?1:(lod===1?.45:.12)),baseY=[];
 const bp=[],tp=[];for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){const v=fp[i],gy=(Array.isArray(it.worldY)&&Number.isFinite(it.worldY[i])?it.worldY[i]:apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1]))+clear;baseY.push(gy);bp.push(worldToCamera(v[0],gy,v[1],cam));tp.push(worldToCamera(v[0],gy+h,v[1],cam));}
 webglPushCamPoly(tp,pitch,prof.roof,.60,-.008);for(let j=0;j<fp.length;j++){const k=(j+1)%fp.length;webglPushCamQuad(bp[j],bp[k],tp[k],tp[j],pitch,j&1?prof.side:prof.body,.60,-.006)}if(budget>=1){for(let j=0;j<fp.length;j++){const k=(j+1)%fp.length;webglPushCamQuad(bp[j],bp[k],camMix(bp[k],tp[k],.10),camMix(bp[j],tp[j],.10),pitch,prof.baseBand,.60,-.009);}}
 if(budget<=0){if(frame){const dep=frame.depth;webglPushCamPoly([apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,-.02,dep*.08,h,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,1.02,dep*.08,h,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,1.02,dep*1.02,h+.28,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,-.02,dep*1.02,h+.28,cam)],pitch,prof.roofDark,.60,-.010)}return true;}
 const rows=Math.max(2,Math.min(prof.floors,budget===2?prof.floors:Math.max(2,Math.round(prof.floors*.62))));
 for(let j=0;j<fp.length;j++){
   const k=(j+1)%fp.length,v0=fp[j],v1=fp[k],g0=baseY[j],g1=baseY[k],len=Math.hypot(v1[0]-v0[0],v1[1]-v0[1]);if(len<5)continue;
   const cols=Math.max(1,Math.min(budget===2?10:5,Math.round(len/(prof.windowMode==='tower'?4.0:(prof.windowMode==='industrial'?5.4:4.6)))));
   for(let r=0;r<rows;r++){
     const yy0=h*(.14 + (r/rows)*.70),yy1=h*(.14 + ((r+.64)/rows)*.70),rowCol=(r&1)?prof.windowAlt:prof.window;
     if(prof.windowMode==='glass' || prof.windowMode==='tower' || prof.windowMode==='hospitality'){
       const band0=.04,band1=.96,x0=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],band0),z0=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],band0),x1=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],band1),z1=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],band1),gy0=lerp(g0,g1,band0),gy1=lerp(g0,g1,band1);
       webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(x0,gy0+yy0,z0,cam),worldToCamera(x1,gy1+yy0,z1,cam),worldToCamera(x1,gy1+yy1,z1,cam),worldToCamera(x0,gy0+yy1,z0,cam),pitch,(r&1)?prof.glassAlt:prof.glass,.60,-.012);
     }else{
       for(let c=0;c<cols;c++){const t0=(c+.12)/cols,t1=(c+.88)/cols,x0=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],t0),z0=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],t0),x1=lerp(v0[0],v1[0],t1),z1=lerp(v0[1],v1[1],t1),gy0=lerp(g0,g1,t0),gy1=lerp(g0,g1,t1);webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(x0,gy0+yy0,z0,cam),worldToCamera(x1,gy1+yy0,z1,cam),worldToCamera(x1,gy1+yy1,z1,cam),worldToCamera(x0,gy0+yy1,z0,cam),pitch,(c&1&&budget===2)?prof.windowAlt:rowCol,.60,-.012);}
       if(prof.hasBalcony&&budget>=1){const bb0=h*(.14 + (r/rows)*.70)-h*.012,bb1=bb0+h*.028;webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(v0[0],g0+bb0,v0[1],cam),worldToCamera(v1[0],g1+bb0,v1[1],cam),worldToCamera(v1[0],g1+bb1,v1[1],cam),worldToCamera(v0[0],g0+bb1,v0[1],cam),pitch,prof.balcony,.60,-.013)}
     }
   }
 }
 if(frame){
   const dep=frame.depth,span=frame.span;
   if(prof.windowMode==='industrial'){webglPushCamQuad(apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,.08,.04,h*.05,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,.92,.04,h*.05,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,.92,.04,h*.34,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,.08,.04,h*.34,cam),pitch,prof.door,.60,-.014)}else if(budget>=1){webglPushCamQuad(apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,.40,.035,h*.03,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,.60,.035,h*.03,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,.60,.035,h*.20,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,.40,.035,h*.20,cam),pitch,prof.entry,.60,-.014)}
   if(budget===2){const hv=Math.max(1,Math.min(prof.hvacCount,4));for(let i=0;i<hv;i++){const u=(i+.5)/hv,d0=dep*.56,d1=dep*.74,y0=h+.05,y1=h+.55;const q0=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u-.04,d0,y0,cam),q1=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u+.04,d0,y0,cam),q2=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u+.04,d1,y0,cam),q3=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u-.04,d1,y0,cam),q4=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u-.04,d0,y1,cam),q5=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u+.04,d0,y1,cam),q6=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u+.04,d1,y1,cam),q7=apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,u-.04,d1,y1,cam);webglPushCamQuad(q0,q1,q5,q4,pitch,prof.hvac,.60,-.015);webglPushCamQuad(q1,q2,q6,q5,pitch,shadeColor(prof.hvac,-8),.60,-.015);webglPushCamQuad(q0,q3,q7,q4,pitch,shadeColor(prof.hvac,-15),.60,-.015);webglPushCamPoly([q4,q5,q6,q7],pitch,shadeColor(prof.hvac,8),.60,-.016);}}
   webglPushCamPoly([apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,-.02,dep*.08,h,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,1.02,dep*.08,h,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,1.02,dep*1.02,h+.32,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,-.02,dep*1.02,h+.32,cam)],pitch,prof.roofDark,.60,-.010);if(budget>=1){webglPushCamQuad(apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,-.02,dep*.08,h,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,1.02,dep*.08,h,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,1.02,dep*.08,h+.28,cam),apexBuildingWorldCam(frame,-.02,dep*.08,h+.28,cam),pitch,prof.parapet,.60,-.011);}
 }
 return true;
}

function drawWorldLandmarks(cam,pitch){
 const allItems=trackLandmarks(); if(!allItems||!allItems.length)return;const entries=apexCompiledLandmarkEntries(allItems),items=entries.map(e=>e.it),staticOcclusion=apexBuildStaticOcclusionContext(entries,cam,pitch);
 const compactSceneryProfile=!(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())&&!!(trackData&&trackData.spec&&apexCircuitVisualFeature('worldBackdrop',null)==='hills');
 const cfgJson=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:"")||{},perfCfg=cfgJson.performance||{},mobileSvgScene=!(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())&&mobilePerformanceMode()&&trackData&&trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.authoritative,sceneScale=Number.isFinite(+perfCfg.mobileSceneryDistanceScale)?clamp(+perfCfg.mobileSceneryDistanceScale,.35,1):.72;
 const project=(x,y,z,maxZ=1750)=>{if(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,apexInspectionFarDistance());const c=worldToCamera(x,y,z,cam);if(c.z<2||c.z>maxZ)return null;const p=projectRoadLocal(c.x,c.y,c.z,pitch);return {...p,z:c.z,c};};
 const inferredBaseLat=(anchor)=>{
   const q=anchor.groundQ||anchor.q||poseAt(anchor.s||0),dx=anchor.x-q.x,dz=anchor.z-q.z;
   return dx*q.nx+dz*q.nz;
 };
 const groundedY=(anchor,along=0,localLat=0)=>{
   if(!Number.isFinite(anchor.s))return anchor.y;
   // El terreno es estático durante la sesión. Los elementos SVG (gradas, boxes,
   // edificios...) preguntaban decenas de veces por frame por la misma cota.
   const key=`${Math.round(along*10)}|${Math.round(localLat*10)}`;
   const cache=anchor._apexGroundCache||(anchor._apexGroundCache=new Map());
   if(cache.has(key))return cache.get(key);
   const L=trackData.km*1000,ss=mod(anchor.s+along,L),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(ss),baseLat=inferredBaseLat(anchor),absLat=baseLat+localLat;
   const st={s:ss,q,env:envProfile(ss)},edge=terrainOuterWidth(st),spread=terrainSpread(st),baseGround=terrainGroundY(st),wx=q.x+q.nx*absLat,wz=q.z+q.nz*absLat,ground=apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(wx,wz,baseGround);
   const bankY=Math.tan(Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0)*absLat;
   const t=terrainEase(clamp((Math.abs(absLat)-edge)/Math.max(8,spread.far*.72),0,1));
   const y=lerp(q.y+bankY,ground,t);cache.set(key,y);return y;
 };
 const anchorPoint=(anchor,along=0,lat=0,y=0)=>{
   const q=anchor.q||poseAt(0),wx=anchor.x+q.tx*along+q.nx*lat,wz=anchor.z+q.tz*along+q.nz*lat;
   return project(wx,groundedY(anchor,along,lat)+y,wz,anchor.maxRenderDistance||1750);
 };
 const poly=(pts,fill,stroke=null,w=1,alpha=1)=>{
   const ok=pts.filter(Boolean);if(ok.length<3)return;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=alpha;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(ok[0].x,ok[0].y);for(let i=1;i<ok.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(ok[i].x,ok[i].y);ctx.closePath();if(fill){ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fill();}if(stroke){ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=w;ctx.stroke();}ctx.restore();
 };
 const line=(a,b,stroke,w=1,alpha=1)=>{if(!a||!b)return;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=alpha;ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=w;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a.x,a.y);ctx.lineTo(b.x,b.y);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();};
 const polyCam=(poly3,fill,stroke=null,w=1,alpha=1,near=.52)=>{
   const clipped=clipPolyNear(poly3,near);if(clipped.length<3)return false;const pp=projectCameraPoly(clipped,pitch);if(!pp||pp.length<3)return false;
   ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=alpha;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pp[0][0],pp[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<pp.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(pp[i][0],pp[i][1]);ctx.closePath();if(fill){ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fill();}if(stroke){ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=w;ctx.stroke();}ctx.restore();return true;
 };
 const lineCam=(A,B,stroke,w=1,alpha=1,near=.52)=>{
   if(!A||!B)return false;let a=A,b=B;if(a.z<near&&b.z<near)return false;if(a.z<near||b.z<near){const t=(near-a.z)/(b.z-a.z);const I={x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t,z:near};if(a.z<near)a=I;else b=I;}
   const pa=projectRoadLocal(a.x,a.y,a.z,pitch),pb=projectRoadLocal(b.x,b.y,b.z,pitch);if(!pa||!pb)return false;line(pa,pb,stroke,w,alpha);return true;
 };
 const labelQueue=[],visible=[];
 let sceneItemIndex=0;
 for(const it of items){
   const itemIndex=sceneItemIndex++;
   const skyline=apexIsSkylineLandmark(it),gy=groundedY(it.anchor);let maxZ=skyline?Math.max(6000,it.maxRenderDistance||it.anchor.maxRenderDistance||0):(it.maxRenderDistance||it.anchor.maxRenderDistance||1750);if(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,apexInspectionFarDistance());
   let p=project(it.anchor.x,gy,it.anchor.z,maxZ);
   if(skyline){
     // v62.2.2: si hay huella real, la visibilidad se decide por caras 3D recortadas
     // contra near-plane. Esto evita que edificios/boxes/gradas desaparezcan al rebasar su centro.
     const exact=Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3?apexLandmarkFootprintClipInfo(it,cam,pitch,maxZ,.30):null;
     if(exact){if(!exact.visible)continue;p={x:exact.x,y:exact.y,z:exact.depth,scale:1};}
     else{
       const ep=apexLandmarkExtentWorldSamples(it).map(v=>project(v.x,v.y,v.z,maxZ)).filter(Boolean);if(!ep.length)continue;
       let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity;for(const q of ep){minX=Math.min(minX,q.x);maxX=Math.max(maxX,q.x)}if(maxX<-W*.72||minX>W*1.72)continue;p=ep.reduce((best,q)=>!best||q.z<best.z?q:best,null);
     }
   }else{
     if(!p)continue;
     if(p.x<-W*.32||p.x>W*1.32)continue;
   }
   let dsLm=1e9;
   if(player&&Number.isFinite(it.anchor.s)){
     const L=trackData.km*1000,ds=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(it.anchor.s,player.s,L));dsLm=ds;
     if(mobileSvgScene&&!skyline){
       const baseMax=(it.type==="tree"?520:(it.type==="grandstand"?1050:(it.type==="pit"||it.type==="block"?900:760)))*sceneScale;
       if(ds>baseMax&&p.z>baseMax*.72)continue;
     }
     if(compactSceneryProfile&&!skyline){
       if(it.type==="gantry"&&(ds>430||p.z>520))continue;
       if((it.type==="grandstand"||it.type==="pit"||it.type==="block")&&(ds>4200||p.z>4200))continue;
       if(it.type==="ferris"&&(ds>3100||p.z>2400))continue;
     }
   }
   if(!apexSceneryShouldRender(it,itemIndex,dsLm,p.z))continue;
   const baseCam=p&&p.c?p.c:worldToCamera(it.anchor.x,gy,it.anchor.z,cam);if(!(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())&&apexStaticLandmarkOccluded(it,baseCam,cam,pitch,staticOcclusion))continue;
   visible.push({it,p});
 }
 visible.sort((A,B)=>B.p.z-A.p.z);
 ctx.save();ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.lineCap="round";
 for(const entry of visible){
   const it=entry.it,a=it.anchor,base=entry.p,s=clamp(420/Math.max(140,base.z),.35,1.15),detailAlpha=clamp((720-base.z)/310,0,1);
   // Las masas principales son siempre opacas. Sólo los detalles se simplifican a distancia.
   if(it.type==="gantry"){
     const span=it.span||19,h=it.height||5.9,postW=Math.max(.22,span*.012),baseFoot=Math.max(.8,span*.040),beamT=Math.max(.28,h*.045);
     const bl0=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5,0),br0=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5,0),tl0=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5,h),tr0=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5,h);
     const bl1=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5-postW,0),br1=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5+postW,0),tl1=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5-postW,h),tr1=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5+postW,h);
     const beamB1=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5-postW,h-beamT),beamB2=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5+postW,h-beamT),beamT1=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5-postW,h+beamT*.18),beamT2=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5+postW,h+beamT*.18);
     // posts
     poly([bl0,bl1,tl1,tl0],"#8e979f",null,1,1); poly([br1,br0,tr0,tr1],"#8e979f",null,1,1);
     line(bl0,tl0,"#c3ccd3",Math.max(1,2.3*s),1); line(br0,tr0,"#c3ccd3",Math.max(1,2.3*s),1);
     // feet
     const lf1=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5-baseFoot,0),lf2=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5+baseFoot*.15,0),lf3=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5+baseFoot*.30,h*.10),lf4=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5-baseFoot*.75,h*.10);
     const rf1=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5-baseFoot*.15,0),rf2=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5+baseFoot,0),rf3=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5+baseFoot*.75,h*.10),rf4=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5-baseFoot*.30,h*.10);
     poly([lf1,lf2,lf3,lf4],"#6b737a",null,1,1); poly([rf1,rf2,rf3,rf4],"#6b737a",null,1,1);
     // main beam and truss
     poly([beamB1,beamB2,beamT2,beamT1],"#d7dde2","rgba(255,255,255,.20)",1,1);
     for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const t0=i/6,t1=(i+.5)/6,t2=(i+1)/6;line(anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.5,span*.5,t0),h-beamT*.85),anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.5,span*.5,t1),h+beamT*.02),"rgba(146,153,160,.95)",Math.max(1,1.25*s),1);line(anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.5,span*.5,t2),h-beamT*.85),anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.5,span*.5,t1),h+beamT*.02),"rgba(146,153,160,.95)",Math.max(1,1.25*s),1);}
     // diagonal braces
     line(anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.5+postW*.5,h*.18),anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.18,h*.72),"#8e979f",Math.max(1,1.8*s),1); line(anchorPoint(a,0,span*.5-postW*.5,h*.18),anchorPoint(a,0,span*.18,h*.72),"#8e979f",Math.max(1,1.8*s),1);
     // timing / branding panels
     const topL=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.28,h*.53),topR=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.28,h*.53),topLT=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.28,h*.82),topRT=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.28,h*.82);
     poly([topL,topR,topRT,topLT],"#151a1f","rgba(255,255,255,.14)",1,1);
     const botL=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.18,h*.16),botR=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.18,h*.16),botLT=anchorPoint(a,0,-span*.18,h*.34),botRT=anchorPoint(a,0,span*.18,h*.34);
     poly([botL,botR,botRT,botLT],"#0f1419","rgba(255,255,255,.12)",1,1);
     // checker strip
     const csY0=h*.84,csY1=h*.96,tiles=10;
     for(let i=0;i<tiles;i++){const c0=anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.36,span*.36,i/tiles),csY0),c1=anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.36,span*.36,(i+1)/tiles),csY0),c2=anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.36,span*.36,(i+1)/tiles),csY1),c3=anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.36,span*.36,i/tiles),csY1);poly([c0,c1,c2,c3],i%2?"#d8dde0":"#21262b",null,1,1);}
     // lamps
     for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const lamp=anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.16,span*.16,(i+.5)/5),h*.44),lamp2=anchorPoint(a,0,lerp(-span*.16,span*.16,(i+.5)/5),h*.50); if(lamp&&lamp2){const rr=Math.max(2,Math.abs(lamp2.y-lamp.y)*.85); ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle=i<4?"#d62834":"#1d3c22"; ctx.shadowColor=i<4?"rgba(255,50,60,.30)":"rgba(110,255,120,.18)"; ctx.shadowBlur=rr*1.8; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(lamp.x,lamp.y,rr,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();}}
     const mid=anchorPoint(a,0,0,h*.68),ad=circuitAdvertisingStyle(a.s,{},it.side||1); if(mid&&mid.scale>.80&&base.z<360)labelQueue.push({x:mid.x,y:mid.y,text:(it.startGantry?"START · " :(it.finishGantry?"FINISH · ":""))+(ad?ad.brand:(it.text||"APEX")),scale:mid.scale,z:base.z,color:ad?ad.fg:'#ffffff'});
   }else if(it.type==="surfaceZone"||it.type==="terrainZone"||it.type==="water"||it.type==="marking"||it.type==="forestZone"){
     // terrain-zone delimita el terreno en planta. No se dibuja como una lámina plana:
     // eso atravesaba el asfalto en zonas de desnivel. El terreno 3D lo genera APEX
     // mediante la malla longitudinal y sus taludes.
     if(it.type==='terrainZone')continue;
     if(it.worldFootprint&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){const bp=it.worldFootprint.map((v,j)=>project(v[0],(Array.isArray(it.worldY)&&Number.isFinite(it.worldY[j])?it.worldY[j]:apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1]))+apexGroundPatchYOffset(it),v[1],it.maxDs||1700)),col=it.color||(it.type==='water'?'#347ca5':it.type==='forestZone'?'#4b7b50':'#777');poly(bp,col,it.type==='marking'?'rgba(255,255,255,.58)':null,1,.94);if(it.type==='forestZone'&&Array.isArray(it.forestPoints)){for(const fp of it.forestPoints){const b=project(fp[0],fp[2]||0,fp[1],it.maxDs||1500),t=project(fp[0],(fp[2]||0)+8.5,fp[1],it.maxDs||1500),l=project(fp[0]-1.9,(fp[2]||0)+2.0,fp[1],it.maxDs||1500),r=project(fp[0]+1.9,(fp[2]||0)+2.0,fp[1],it.maxDs||1500);if(b&&t&&l&&r){line(b,t,'#57452f',1,1);poly([l,r,t],'#285f38',null,1,.94)}}}if(it.type==='marking'&&it.markingKind==='CROSSWALK'&&bp.filter(Boolean).length>=4){const B=bp.filter(Boolean),minX=Math.min(...B.map(p=>p.x)),maxX=Math.max(...B.map(p=>p.x)),minY=Math.min(...B.map(p=>p.y)),maxY=Math.max(...B.map(p=>p.y));ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.85;for(let k=0;k<7;k++){ctx.fillStyle=k%2?'#202428':'#f4f5f5';const x=minX+(maxX-minX)*k/7;ctx.fillRect(x,minY,(maxX-minX)/9,Math.max(2,maxY-minY));}ctx.restore();}continue;}
   }else if(it.type==="barrier"){
     if(Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){const h=Math.max(.55,it.height||1.05),kind=it.barrierKind||'CONCRETE',baseCol=apexBarrierColor(kind),ad=circuitAdvertisingStyle(a.s,{},it.side||1);for(let j=0;j<it.worldPath.length-1;j++){const p0=it.worldPath[j],p1=it.worldPath[j+1],g0=Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)&&Number.isFinite(it.worldPathY[j])?it.worldPathY[j]:apexWorldGroundY(p0[0],p0[1]),g1=Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)&&Number.isFinite(it.worldPathY[j+1])?it.worldPathY[j+1]:apexWorldGroundY(p1[0],p1[1]),b0=project(p0[0],g0,p0[1],it.maxDs||1700),b1=project(p1[0],g1,p1[1],it.maxDs||1700),t0=project(p0[0],g0+h,p0[1],it.maxDs||1700),t1=project(p1[0],g1+h,p1[1],it.maxDs||1700);poly([b0,b1,t1,t0],(ad&&kind!=='TYRE')?ad.bg:baseCol,null,1,1);}continue;}
   }else if(it.type==="tunnel"){
     // El path tunnel define un corredor hueco. La geometría visible la construye
     // drawTunnelSegment; nunca se convierte en un prisma sólido que cierre la boca.
     continue;
   }else if(it.type==="bridge"&&it.pedestrianBridge){
     // v5.230.27 · Pasarela peatonal: cuerpo horizontal fino y oscuro,
     // apoyos esbeltos fuera de la calzada y barandilla/estructura ligera superior.
     // data-apex-height se interpreta como altura total aproximada, no como un bloque macizo desde el suelo.
     const totalH=Math.max(6.1,Math.min(8.6,Number(it.height)||7)),deckT=clamp(totalH*.16,.88,1.24),deckBase=totalH-deckT,gy=groundedY(a),col=it.color||"#343b42";
     if(it.worldFootprint&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){
       const bp3=it.worldFootprint.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],gy+deckBase,v[1],cam)),tp3=it.worldFootprint.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],gy+totalH,v[1],cam));
       polyCam(bp3,"#1c2227",null,1,1);
       for(let j=0;j<bp3.length;j++){const k=(j+1)%bp3.length;polyCam([bp3[j],bp3[k],tp3[k],tp3[j]],j%2?shadeColor(col,-8):col,"rgba(255,255,255,.10)",1,1);}
       polyCam(tp3,shadeColor(col,16),"rgba(255,255,255,.18)",1,1);
       if(Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){
         const p0=it.worldPath[0],p1=it.worldPath[it.worldPath.length-1],dx=p1[0]-p0[0],dz=p1[1]-p0[1],ln=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,nx=-dz/ln,nz=dx/ln,colHalf=.30;
         for(const ep of [p0,p1]){
           const ey=apexWorldGroundY(ep[0],ep[1]),b0=project(ep[0]-nx*colHalf,ey,ep[1]-nz*colHalf,it.maxDs||1700),b1=project(ep[0]+nx*colHalf,ey,ep[1]+nz*colHalf,it.maxDs||1700),t0=project(ep[0]-nx*colHalf,gy+deckBase,ep[1]-nz*colHalf,it.maxDs||1700),t1=project(ep[0]+nx*colHalf,gy+deckBase,ep[1]+nz*colHalf,it.maxDs||1700);
           poly([b0,b1,t1,t0],"#aeb7be","rgba(255,255,255,.16)",1,1);
         }
         if(detailAlpha>.05){
           const railY=gy+totalH+.42,rail=it.worldPath.map(v=>project(v[0],railY,v[1],it.maxDs||1700));
           for(let j=1;j<rail.length;j++)line(rail[j-1],rail[j],"rgba(205,215,222,.82)",Math.max(1,1.15*s),1);
           const posts=Math.min(8,Math.max(3,Math.round(it.worldPath.length/3)));
           for(let j=0;j<posts;j++){const ix=Math.round(j*(it.worldPath.length-1)/Math.max(1,posts-1)),v=it.worldPath[ix],rb=project(v[0],gy+totalH,v[1],it.maxDs||1700),rt=project(v[0],railY,v[1],it.maxDs||1700);line(rb,rt,"rgba(205,215,222,.62)",Math.max(1,.9*s),1);}
         }
       }
       continue;     }
   }else if(it.type==="road"&&Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){
     // v5.230.56 · Vial como cinta 3D segmentada. El código anterior convertía toda
     // la polilínea en un único polígono plano a la altura del anclaje: una carretera
     // larga podía aparecer como una enorme losa flotante sobre un túnel.
     const P=it.worldPath,Y=Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)?it.worldPathY:[],hw=Math.max(1.8,(Number(it.width)||8)*.5),col=it.color||"#747c83";
     for(let j=0;j<P.length-1;j++){
       const p0=P[j],p1=P[j+1],dx=p1[0]-p0[0],dz=p1[1]-p0[1],ln=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(ln<.08)continue;

     poly([ufL,ufR,usR,usL],"#434b52",null,1,1); poly([ufL,usL,tl,sl],"#4b535a",null,1,.95); poly([sr,tr,usR,ufR],"#505860",null,1,.95);
     if(it.grounded){const gbL=anchorPoint(a,-len*.50,frontLat,0),gbR=anchorPoint(a,len*.50,frontLat,0),gtR=anchorPoint(a,len*.50,frontLat+side*1.6,h*.10),gtL=anchorPoint(a,-len*.50,frontLat+side*1.6,h*.10);poly([gbL,gbR,gtR,gtL],"#496341",null,1,.82);}
     if(sl&&sr&&tr&&tl&&detailAlpha>.05){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.88*detailAlpha;ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(239,243,245,.82)";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,1.1*s);for(let i=1;i<6;i++){const k=i/6;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sl.x+(tl.x-sl.x)*k,sl.y+(tl.y-sl.y)*k);ctx.lineTo(sr.x+(tr.x-sr.x)*k,sr.y+(tr.y-sr.y)*k);ctx.stroke();}ctx.restore();}
     // Cubierta con voladizo e inclinación suave.

       ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.92*detailAlpha;ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(224,232,236,.78)";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,1.5*s);

       for(let i=0;i<cols;i++){
         const k=(i+.5)/cols-0.5,x=len*k*.92;

         line(pb,pt,"rgba(217,224,228,.76)",Math.max(1,1.3*s),1);
         line(sf,st,"rgba(233,238,241,.56)",Math.max(1,1.1*s),1);
       }

     }
     line(rfl2,rfr2,"rgba(255,255,255,.84)",Math.max(1,1.4*s),1);
   }else if(it.type==="billboard"||it.type==="advertising"){
     if(compactSceneryProfile&&it.type==="billboard"&&apexCircuitVisualFeature('suppressTracksideBillboards',false)===true)continue;

     line(p1,p2,"#bfc8cd",Math.max(1,2.0*s),1); line(anchorPoint(a,0,lat+w*.26,0),anchorPoint(a,0,lat+w*.26,h*1.12),"#bfc8cd",Math.max(1,2.0*s),1); const ad=circuitAdvertisingStyle(a.s,{},side);poly([l,r,rt,lt],ad?ad.bg:'#1e2429',ad?ad.fg:'rgba(255,255,255,.18)',1,1);

   }else if(it.type==="tunnelPortal"){

     const span=Math.max(8,it.span||18),h=Math.max(3.6,it.height||6),frameW=clamp(span*.075,.72,1.35),lintelH=clamp(h*.13,.55,.95),depth=.72;

       }
       line(oLT,oRT,"rgba(238,242,244,.72)",Math.max(1,1.1*s),1);
     }
   }else if(it.type==="flagPole"){
     const h=it.height||18;
     const b=anchorPoint(a,0,0,0),t=anchorPoint(a,0,0,h);
     const fA=anchorPoint(a,0,.5,h-4.8),fB=anchorPoint(a,0,5.0,h-4.2),fC=anchorPoint(a,0,5.8,h-2.7),fD=anchorPoint(a,0,4.8,h-1.0),fE=anchorPoint(a,0,.4,h-1.4);

         poly([fA,fB,fC,fD,fE],"#f4c724",null,1,1);
         const r1a=anchorPoint(a,0,.45,h-4.9),r1b=anchorPoint(a,0,4.7,h-4.35),r1c=anchorPoint(a,0,5.5,h-3.55),r1d=anchorPoint(a,0,.42,h-3.75);
         const r2a=anchorPoint(a,0,.40,h-2.05),r2b=anchorPoint(a,0,5.2,h-1.55),r2c=anchorPoint(a,0,4.6,h-.90),r2d=anchorPoint(a,0,.36,h-1.35);
         poly([r1a,r1b,r1c,r1d],"#c61f28",null,1,1);

         const crest=anchorPoint(a,0,2.05,h-3.02); if(crest) ctx.save(),ctx.fillStyle='#9a3a25',ctx.beginPath(),ctx.arc(crest.x,crest.y,Math.max(1.2,1.8*s),0,Math.PI*2),ctx.fill(),ctx.restore();
       }
     }
   }else if(it.type==="ferris"){
     const center=anchorPoint(a,0,0,it.radius*1.25),rim=anchorPoint(a,0,0,it.radius*2.25),baseY=anchorPoint(a,0,0,0);
     if(center&&rim&&baseY){const rr=Math.max(5,Math.abs(rim.y-center.y));ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.strokeStyle=it.color||"#dfe7ec";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,2.0*s);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(center.x,center.y,rr,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const ang=(Math.PI*2*i)/8;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(center.x,center.y);ctx.lineTo(center.x+Math.cos(ang)*rr,center.y+Math.sin(ang)*rr);ctx.stroke();}ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(center.x-rr*.6,baseY.y);ctx.lineTo(center.x-rr*.18,center.y+rr*.82);ctx.moveTo(center.x+rr*.6,baseY.y);ctx.lineTo(center.x+rr*.18,center.y+rr*.82);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
   }else if(it.type==="tree"){
     const v=it.treeVariant||it.variant||'deciduous',seed=apexStringHash((it.sourceId||it.name||'tree')+'|'+v),r0=((seed>>>0)&1023)/1023,r1=((seed>>>10)&1023)/1023,r2=((seed>>>20)&1023)/1023,wMul=.88+r0*.34,hMul=.92+r1*.22,crossTilt=(r2-.5)*.22,treeW=it.width*wMul,treeH=it.height*hMul,trunkFrac=v==='palm'?(0.48+r0*.07):(v==='fir'?(0.24+r0*.05):(0.37+r0*.06)),trunkB=anchorPoint(a,0,0,0),trunkT=anchorPoint(a,0,0,treeH*trunkFrac),crown=anchorPoint(a,0,0,treeH),crownLow=anchorPoint(a,0,0,treeH*(trunkFrac+.06));
     if(trunkB&&trunkT&&crown){ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle=v==='palm'?'#6b5737':'#5a432d';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,v==='palm'?2.3*s:2.15*s);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(trunkB.x,trunkB.y);ctx.lineTo(trunkT.x,trunkT.y);ctx.stroke();
       if(v==='fir'){
         const fc=it.foliage||'#285f38',tier0=treeH*(.24+.03*r1),tier1=treeH*(.46+.05*r2),tier2=treeH*(.67+.03*r0),l0=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.62,tier0),r0p=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.62,tier0),l1=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.50,tier1),r1p=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.50,tier1),l2=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.36,tier2),r2p=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.36,tier2);
         poly([l0,r0p,crown],fc,null,1,1);poly([l1,r1p,anchorPoint(a,0,0,treeH*.92)],shadeColor(fc,6),null,1,.97);poly([l2,r2p,anchorPoint(a,0,0,treeH*1.01)],shadeColor(fc,12),null,1,.94);
         const crossL=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.20,treeH*.56),crossR=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.20,treeH*.56),crossTop=anchorPoint(a,0,crossTilt*treeW*.25,treeH*.76);poly([crossL,crossR,crossTop],shadeColor(fc,18),null,1,.86);
       }
       else if(v==='palm'){
         const fc=it.foliage||'#4e8b45';
         for(let k=0;k<8;k++){
           const aa=-1.26+k*(2.52/7),lat=Math.sin(aa)*treeW*(.88-.08*(k%2)),tipY=treeH*(.80-.06*(Math.abs(k-3.5)/3.5)),leaf=anchorPoint(a,0,lat,tipY),col=shadeColor(fc,k%2?8:-5);
           line(crownLow,leaf,col,Math.max(1,1.9*s),1);
         }
         const frontL=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.38,treeH*.73),frontR=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.42,treeH*.75),frontTop=anchorPoint(a,0,crossTilt*treeW*.18,treeH*.86),frontL2=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.18,treeH*.66),frontR2=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.16,treeH*.68);
         poly([frontL,frontR,frontTop],shadeColor(fc,12),null,1,.92);poly([frontL2,frontR2,anchorPoint(a,0,crossTilt*treeW*.12,treeH*.80)],shadeColor(fc,5),null,1,.86);
       }
       else{
         const fc=it.foliage||'#4b8447',l=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*(.62+.04*r0),treeH*.34),r=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*(.64-.02*r1),treeH*.35),ll=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.56,treeH*.72),rr=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.52,treeH*.74),top=anchorPoint(a,0,crossTilt*treeW*.14,treeH*1.01);
         poly([l,r,rr,top,ll],fc,null,1,1);
         const l2=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.48,treeH*.46),r2=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.46,treeH*.48),ll2=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.34,treeH*.84),rr2=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.32,treeH*.86),top2=anchorPoint(a,0,crossTilt*treeW*.10,treeH*.96);poly([l2,r2,rr2,top2,ll2],shadeColor(fc,8),null,1,.90);
         const crossL=anchorPoint(a,0,-treeW*.24,treeH*.54),crossR=anchorPoint(a,0,treeW*.22,treeH*.56),crossTop=anchorPoint(a,0,crossTilt*treeW*.18,treeH*.74);poly([crossL,crossR,crossTop],shadeColor(fc,16),null,1,.86);
       }
       ctx.restore();}
   }else if(it.type==="tower"){
     const b=anchorPoint(a,0,0,0),t=anchorPoint(a,0,0,it.height||20); if(b&&t){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.strokeStyle=it.color||"#7c8792";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,6*s);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(b.x,b.y);ctx.lineTo(t.x,t.y);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
   }
 }
 ctx.restore();
 if(labelQueue.length){
   labelQueue.sort((A,B)=>B.z-A.z);ctx.save();ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="middle";
   for(const lb of labelQueue){const fs=clamp(8+lb.scale*6,10,18),padX=Math.max(5,fs*.55),padY=Math.max(3,fs*.28);ctx.font=`700 ${fs}px Arial`;const tw=Math.ceil(ctx.measureText(lb.text).width),x=Math.round(lb.x),y=Math.round(lb.y),w=tw+padX*2,h=fs+padY*2;ctx.globalAlpha=.88;ctx.fillStyle="rgba(6,10,14,.92)";ctx.fillRect(x-w/2,y-h/2,w,h);ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.18)";ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeRect(x-w/2+.5,y-h/2+.5,w-1,h-1);ctx.fillStyle="#eef4f7";ctx.fillText(lb.text,x,y+1);}
   ctx.restore();
 }
}


function terrainPoly(p,surface,alt=false,seed=0){
 poly(p,surfaceColor(surface,alt));
 if(surface==="ASPHALT")return;
 let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;
 for(const q of p){minX=Math.min(minX,q[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,q[0]);minY=Math.min(minY,q[1]);maxY=Math.max(maxY,q[1])}
 if(maxX-minX<5||maxY-minY<3)return;
 ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p[0][0],p[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<p.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(p[i][0],p[i][1]);ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();ctx.globalAlpha=.34;
 if(surface==="GRAVEL"){
   for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
     const x=minX+mod(seed*17+i*31,101)/101*(maxX-minX),y=minY+mod(seed*29+i*43,97)/97*(maxY-minY);
     ctx.fillStyle=i%2?"#6d5942":"#ddc097";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,1.0+(i%2)*.7,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   }
 }else if(surface==="SAND"){
   ctx.strokeStyle="#806b45";ctx.lineWidth=1;
   for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const y=minY+(i+1)*(maxY-minY)/3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(minX,y);ctx.quadraticCurveTo((minX+maxX)/2,y-2,maxX,y);ctx.stroke()}
 }else if(surface==="WATER"){
   ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.22)";ctx.lineWidth=1;
   for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
     const y=minY+(i+1)*(maxY-minY)/4;
     ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(minX,y);ctx.quadraticCurveTo((minX+maxX)/2,y-2+(i%2)*3,maxX,y+(i%2?1:-1));ctx.stroke();
   }
 }else if(surface==="GRASS"||surface==="PARK"||surface==="FOREST"||surface==="DRYGRASS"){
   ctx.strokeStyle=surface==="FOREST"?"#11311a":surface==="DRYGRASS"?"#55582f":"#174725";ctx.lineWidth=1;
   for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
     const x=minX+mod(seed*13+i*23,89)/89*(maxX-minX),y=minY+mod(seed*19+i*37,83)/83*(maxY-minY);
     ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(x+(i%3)-1,y-3-(i%3));ctx.stroke();
   }
 }
 ctx.restore();
}

// v5.230.62.7.3 · CAMPO DE NUBES EN COORDENADAS DE MUNDO.
// Las nubes ya no están pegadas a la pantalla: cada nube tiene X/Z/altura propias,
// deriva con el viento y se proyecta desde la cámara activa. Al girar o desplazar
// la cámara cambia su posición aparente de forma natural.
let apexWeatherCloudFieldCache=null;
function apexWeatherCloudHash(str){let h=2166136261>>>0;for(let i=0;i<str.length;i++){h^=str.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)>>>0}return h>>>0}
function apexWeatherCloudField(){
 if(!trackData||!Array.isArray(trackData.pts)||!trackData.pts.length)return null;
 const id=String(trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir||'track')+':'+trackData.pts.length;
 if(apexWeatherCloudFieldCache&&apexWeatherCloudFieldCache.id===id)return apexWeatherCloudFieldCache;
 let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;
 for(const p of trackData.pts){if(!p)continue;minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1])}
 if(!Number.isFinite(minX)){minX=minZ=-1500;maxX=maxZ=1500}
 const cx=(minX+maxX)*.5,cz=(minZ+maxZ)*.5,spanX=Math.max(5200,(maxX-minX)+4200),spanZ=Math.max(5200,(maxZ-minZ)+4200);
 let seed=apexWeatherCloudHash(id)^0x9e3779b9;const rnd=()=>{seed=(Math.imul(seed,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return seed/4294967296};
 const mk=(count,layer)=>Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>{const high=layer==='high';return{layer,i,x:cx-spanX*.5+rnd()*spanX,z:cz-spanZ*.5+rnd()*spanZ,y:high?(560+rnd()*430):(180+rnd()*260),w:high?(460+rnd()*700):(190+rnd()*390),h:high?(44+rnd()*68):(50+rnd()*92),phase:rnd()*Math.PI*2};});
 const field={id,cx,cz,spanX,spanZ,windAngle:(.35+rnd()*1.65),high:mk(72,'high'),low:mk(96,'low')};apexWeatherCloudFieldCache=field;return field;
}
function apexWeatherCloudWorldNode(node,sm,t){
 const f=apexWeatherCloudField();if(!f)return null;const high=node.layer==='high',speed=high?(3.8+sm.wind*5.6):(8.0+sm.wind*13.0),ang=f.windAngle,dx=Math.cos(ang)*speed*t,dz=Math.sin(ang)*speed*t;
 const wrap=(v,c,span,d)=>c-span*.5+mod((v-c+span*.5)+d,span);
 return{x:wrap(node.x,f.cx,f.spanX,dx),z:wrap(node.z,f.cz,f.spanZ,dz),y:node.y+Math.sin(t*(high?.004:.008)+node.phase)*(high?18:11),w:node.w,h:node.h,layer:node.layer,i:node.i};
}
function apexWeatherCloudScreenNode(node,cam,pitch,sm,t){
 if(!cam)return null;
 const w=apexWeatherCloudWorldNode(node,sm,t);
 if(!w)return null;
 const c=worldToCamera(w.x,w.y,w.z,cam);
 const high=w.layer==='high',nearZ=high?120:80,farZ=high?22000:10000,fadeFrom=high?15000:7000;
 if(!c||!Number.isFinite(c.x)||!Number.isFinite(c.y)||!Number.isFinite(c.z)||c.z<nearZ||c.z>farZ)return null;
 const p=projectLocal(c.x,c.y,c.z,pitch);
 if(!p||!Number.isFinite(p.x)||!Number.isFinite(p.y)||!Number.isFinite(p.scale))return null;
 const ww=clamp(w.w*p.scale,18,W*.55),hh=clamp(w.h*p.scale,5,H*.18),hy=clamp(sceneHorizon(pitch),H*.18,H*.70);
 if(p.x+ww< -W*.12||p.x-ww>W*1.12||p.y-hh>hy+H*.055||p.y+hh< -H*.18)return null;
 const fade=(c.z<=fadeFrom)?1:clamp(1-(c.z-fadeFrom)/(farZ-fadeFrom),0,1);
 if(fade<=.01)return null;
 return{x:p.x,y:p.y,w:ww,h:hh,z:c.z,layer:w.layer,i:w.i,fade};
}
// Selecciona NUBES VISIBLES recorriendo TODO el campo mundial. A diferencia de
// v62.7.3, aquí NO capamos agresivamente el número final; con ~168 nodos totales
// es más barato y mucho más estable dibujar todas las visibles y evitar popping.
function apexWeatherVisibleClouds(nodes,cam,pitch,sm,t,target){
 if(!Array.isArray(nodes)||!nodes.length)return[];
 const visible=[];
 for(const node of nodes){const p=apexWeatherCloudScreenNode(node,cam,pitch,sm,t);if(p)visible.push(p)}
 if(!visible.length)return visible;
 visible.sort((a,b)=>b.z-a.z);
 return visible;
}
// Nubes de dos capas. Las altas derivan despacio y son muy anchas; las bajas
// tienen más parallax, volumen y oscurecimiento. Son vectoriales y baratas.
function apexDrawWeatherCloudsCanvas(horizon,cam){
 if(!apexWeatherRuntime||!cam)return;const sm=apexWeatherSample(),cover=sm.cloud;if(cover<.025)return;
 const mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),t=Number.isFinite(raceT)?raceT:0,hy=Math.max(30,horizon||H*.46),field=apexWeatherCloudField();if(!field)return;ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(0,0,W,hy+18);ctx.clip();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
 const hiTarget=sm.high<.08?0:Math.max(1,Math.round((mobile?8:12)*sm.high+sm.cloud*2)),loTarget=sm.low<.06?0:Math.max(1,Math.round((mobile?9:14)*sm.low+sm.cloud*2));
 const hi=apexWeatherVisibleClouds(field.high,cam,cam.pitch||0,sm,t,hiTarget),lo=apexWeatherVisibleClouds(field.low,cam,cam.pitch||0,sm,t,loTarget);
 for(const p of hi){
   ctx.globalAlpha=(.075+.14*sm.high)*(p.fade||1);ctx.fillStyle=sm.dark>.30?'#c1c9d0':'#ffffff';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(p.x,p.y,p.w*.42,p.h*.62,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.ellipse(p.x+p.w*.25,p.y-p.h*.16,p.w*.34,p.h*.50,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.ellipse(p.x-p.w*.26,p.y+p.h*.04,p.w*.30,p.h*.46,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 }
 for(const p of lo){
   const a=(.11+.19*sm.low)*(p.fade||1);ctx.globalAlpha=a;ctx.fillStyle=sm.state==='STORM'?'#6e7882':sm.dark>.20?'#98a4ad':'#e8edf0';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(p.x-p.w*.24,p.y+p.h*.03,p.w*.27,p.h*.48,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.ellipse(p.x,p.y-p.h*.18,p.w*.31,p.h*.62,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.ellipse(p.x+p.w*.28,p.y,p.w*.33,p.h*.50,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();if(sm.low>.62){ctx.globalAlpha=a*.40;ctx.fillStyle='#515b64';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(p.x,p.y+p.h*.28,p.w*.43,p.h*.22,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
 }
 const flash=apexWeatherLightningFlash();if(flash>.01){ctx.globalAlpha=.14*flash;ctx.fillStyle='#f4f8ff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,hy)}ctx.restore();
}

function drawBackground(cam){
 if(apexInspectionActive()){ctx.fillStyle='#24313d';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);return;}
 const p=palette(),ci=circuitIdentity();
 const envNow=player?envProfile(player.s):null;
 const visualNow=envNow?terrainVisualProfile({s:player.s,env:envNow}):null;
 ctx.save();
 ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.globalCompositeOperation="source-over";

 // Respaldo opaco absoluto: nunca debe quedar un píxel del canvas sin suelo/cielo.
 const sky=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H*.62);
 sky.addColorStop(0,p.sky1);sky.addColorStop(1,p.sky2);
 ctx.fillStyle=sky;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 apexDrawWeatherCloudsCanvas(H*(mobileLandscapeMode()?.43:(compactMode()?.45:.465)),cam);

 // Terreno lejano de respaldo. Se adapta al entorno dominante del sector actual.
 const groundBase=visualNow?surfaceColor(visualNow.far,false):((ci&&ci.ground)||p.ground||surfaceColor("GRASS",false));
 const gy=H*(mobileLandscapeMode()?.43:(compactMode()?.45:.465));
 const gg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,gy,0,H);
 gg.addColorStop(0,shadeColor(groundBase,10));
 gg.addColorStop(.35,groundBase);
 gg.addColorStop(1,shadeColor(groundBase,-16));
 ctx.fillStyle=gg;ctx.fillRect(0,gy,W,H-gy);

 // Textura de fondo extremadamente discreta: solo evita una masa totalmente plana.
 ctx.globalAlpha=.10;ctx.strokeStyle=shadeColor(groundBase,-28);ctx.lineWidth=1;
 for(let i=0;i<24;i++){
   const x=(i*149+37)%Math.max(1,W),y=gy+((i*83)%997)/997*Math.max(1,H-gy);
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(x+((i%3)-1)*2,y-2-(i%3));ctx.stroke();
 }

 // Insinuación suave del contexto real: parques con viales, zonas urbanas o explanadas.
 const coastNow=(envNow&&player)?terrainWaterSideProfile({s:player.s,env:envNow}):null;
 if(visualNow&&(visualNow.far==="WATER"||(coastNow&&(coastNow.leftFar==="WATER"||coastNow.rightFar==="WATER")))){
   ctx.globalAlpha=.30;
   const wave=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,gy,W,H);
   wave.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,.18)');wave.addColorStop(.45,'rgba(255,255,255,.03)');wave.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,.08)');
   ctx.strokeStyle=wave;ctx.lineWidth=1.3;
   for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
     const yy=gy+(H-gy)*(.12+i*.12);
     ctx.beginPath();
     ctx.moveTo(-W*.02,yy);
     ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.14,yy-6,W*.37,yy+7,W*.58,yy-1);
     ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.78,yy-8,W*.92,yy+5,W*1.02,yy-2);
     ctx.stroke();
   }
 }

 if(visualNow&&visualNow.detail!=="NONE"){
   ctx.globalAlpha=visualNow.detail==="CITY_STREET"?.12:.10;
   ctx.lineWidth=1.4;
   ctx.strokeStyle=visualNow.detail==="CITY_STREET"?shadeColor(surfaceColor("ROAD",false),-2):shadeColor(surfaceColor("ROAD",false),-6);
   for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
     const yy=gy+(H-gy)*(.18+i*.18);
     ctx.beginPath();
     ctx.moveTo(-W*.05,yy+(i%2?-6:4));
     ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.18,yy-12,W*.42,yy+8,W*.68,yy-4);
     ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.83,yy-10,W*.94,yy+6,W*1.05,yy-3);
     ctx.stroke();
   }
 }

 if(["night","sunset"].includes(trackData.spec.theme)){
   ctx.globalAlpha=.65;ctx.fillStyle="#fff";
   for(let i=0;i<35;i++){const x=(i*197%997)/997*W,y=(i*83%331)/331*H*.36;ctx.fillRect(x,y,1.2,1.2)}
 }
 ctx.restore();
}

function projectRoadLocal(x,y,z,camPitch=0){
 const horizon=sceneHorizon(camPitch);
 const hfov=sceneHFov();
 const f=(W*.5)/Math.tan(hfov*.5);
 const eyeH=driverEyeHeight();
 const zz=Math.max(.38,z);
 return{x:W*.5+f*x/zz,y:horizon+f*(eyeH-y)/zz,scale:f/zz,behind:z<.38};
}

// ---------- v5.152 · RENDER PERFORMANCE PROFILER ----------
// Perfilador de coste por subsistema. No cambia la simulación: únicamente mide y
// suaviza tiempos para DEV/telemetría. Las medidas se reinician al inicio de frame.
const renderProfileRaw={trackMs:0,terrainMs:0,tunnelMs:0,environmentMs:0,carsMs:0,mirrorsMs:0,cockpitGrossMs:0,effectsMs:0,miniMapMs:0,webglSubmitMs:0};
function renderProfileReset(){for(const k in renderProfileRaw)renderProfileRaw[k]=0;}
function renderProfileAdd(key,ms){if(Number.isFinite(ms)&&ms>=0)renderProfileRaw[key]=(renderProfileRaw[key]||0)+ms;}
function renderProfileMeasure(key,fn){const t0=performance.now();const out=fn();renderProfileAdd(key,performance.now()-t0);return out;}
function renderProfileCommit(){
 const a=.14,mirror=Math.max(0,renderProfileRaw.mirrorsMs||0),cockpitGross=Math.max(0,renderProfileRaw.cockpitGrossMs||0);
 telemetryPerf.trackRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.trackRenderMs||0,renderProfileRaw.trackMs||0,a);
 telemetryPerf.terrainRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.terrainRenderMs||0,renderProfileRaw.terrainMs||0,a);
 telemetryPerf.tunnelRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.tunnelRenderMs||0,renderProfileRaw.tunnelMs||0,a);
 telemetryPerf.environmentRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.environmentRenderMs||0,renderProfileRaw.environmentMs||0,a);
 telemetryPerf.carsRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.carsRenderMs||0,renderProfileRaw.carsMs||0,a);
 telemetryPerf.mirrorsRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.mirrorsRenderMs||0,mirror,a);
 telemetryPerf.cockpitRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.cockpitRenderMs||0,Math.max(0,cockpitGross-mirror),a);
 telemetryPerf.effectsRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.effectsRenderMs||0,renderProfileRaw.effectsMs||0,a);
 telemetryPerf.miniMapRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.miniMapRenderMs||0,renderProfileRaw.miniMapMs||0,a);
 telemetryPerf.webglSubmitMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.webglSubmitMs||0,renderProfileRaw.webglSubmitMs||0,a);
}
function renderProfilerSnapshot(){return{
 backend:rendererBackendId?rendererBackendId():'canvas2d',fps:telemetryPerf.fps||0,frameMs:telemetryPerf.frameMs||0,renderMs:telemetryPerf.renderMs||0,
 physicsMs:telemetryPerf.physicsMs||0,aiMs:telemetryPerf.aiMs||0,collisionMs:telemetryPerf.collisionMs||0,telemetryMs:telemetryPerf.telemetryMs||0,hudMs:telemetryPerf.hudMs||0,audioMs:telemetryPerf.audioMs||0,
 trackMs:telemetryPerf.trackRenderMs||0,terrainMs:telemetryPerf.terrainRenderMs||0,tunnelMs:telemetryPerf.tunnelRenderMs||0,environmentMs:telemetryPerf.environmentRenderMs||0,carsMs:telemetryPerf.carsRenderMs||0,mirrorsMs:telemetryPerf.mirrorsRenderMs||0,cockpitMs:telemetryPerf.cockpitRenderMs||0,effectsMs:telemetryPerf.effectsRenderMs||0,miniMapMs:telemetryPerf.miniMapRenderMs||0,webglSubmitMs:telemetryPerf.webglSubmitMs||0,webglTriangles:telemetryPerf.webglTriangles||0,staticBatch:typeof apexStaticBatchStatsSnapshot==='function'?apexStaticBatchStatsSnapshot():null,staticOcclusion:typeof apexStaticOcclusionStatsSnapshot==='function'?apexStaticOcclusionStatsSnapshot():null,weather:apexWeatherRuntimeSnapshot(),rivalLod:typeof apexRivalLodStatsSnapshot==='function'?apexRivalLodStatsSnapshot():null,mirrorAdaptive:typeof apexMirrorPerfStatsSnapshot==='function'?apexMirrorPerfStatsSnapshot():null
};}
// v5.230.62 · PROFILER DE CONDUCCIÓN. Acumula tiempos RAW, no sólo medias suavizadas,
// para detectar tirones aunque la telemetría detallada esté desactivada.
let apexRuntimePerf=null;
function apexRuntimePerfReset(){
 apexRuntimePerf={frames:0,totalFrameMs:0,totalPhysicsMs:0,totalRenderMs:0,totalTerrainMs:0,totalTunnelMs:0,totalTrackMs:0,totalEnvironmentMs:0,totalCarsMs:0,totalMirrorsMs:0,totalCockpitMs:0,totalEffectsMs:0,totalHudMs:0,totalAudioMs:0,worstFrameMs:0,worstRenderMs:0,worstPhysicsMs:0,over16_7:0,over33:0,over50:0,over100:0,hitches:[]};
}
function apexRuntimePerfRecord(frameMs,physicsMs,renderMs){
 const p=apexRuntimePerf;if(!p||!Number.isFinite(frameMs)||frameMs<=0)return;
 p.frames++;p.totalFrameMs+=frameMs;p.totalPhysicsMs+=physicsMs||0;p.totalRenderMs+=renderMs||0;
 p.totalTerrainMs+=renderProfileRaw.terrainMs||0;p.totalTunnelMs+=renderProfileRaw.tunnelMs||0;p.totalTrackMs+=renderProfileRaw.trackMs||0;p.totalEnvironmentMs+=renderProfileRaw.environmentMs||0;p.totalCarsMs+=renderProfileRaw.carsMs||0;p.totalMirrorsMs+=renderProfileRaw.mirrorsMs||0;p.totalCockpitMs+=Math.max(0,(renderProfileRaw.cockpitGrossMs||0)-(renderProfileRaw.mirrorsMs||0));p.totalEffectsMs+=renderProfileRaw.effectsMs||0;p.totalHudMs+=telemetryPerf.hudMs||0;p.totalAudioMs+=telemetryPerf.audioMs||0;
 p.worstFrameMs=Math.max(p.worstFrameMs,frameMs);p.worstRenderMs=Math.max(p.worstRenderMs,renderMs||0);p.worstPhysicsMs=Math.max(p.worstPhysicsMs,physicsMs||0);
 if(frameMs>16.7)p.over16_7++;if(frameMs>33.34)p.over33++;if(frameMs>50)p.over50++;if(frameMs>100)p.over100++;
 if(frameMs>50&&p.hitches.length<80)p.hitches.push({t:Number((raceT||0).toFixed(3)),frameMs:Number(frameMs.toFixed(2)),renderMs:Number((renderMs||0).toFixed(2)),physicsMs:Number((physicsMs||0).toFixed(2)),terrainMs:Number((renderProfileRaw.terrainMs||0).toFixed(2)),tunnelMs:Number((renderProfileRaw.tunnelMs||0).toFixed(2)),mirrorsMs:Number((renderProfileRaw.mirrorsMs||0).toFixed(2)),carsMs:Number((renderProfileRaw.carsMs||0).toFixed(2)),s:player&&Number.isFinite(player.s)?Number(player.s.toFixed(1)):null,speedKph:player?Number((player.speed*3.6).toFixed(1)):null});
}
function apexRuntimePerfSummary(){
 const p=apexRuntimePerf;if(!p||!p.frames)return null;const n=p.frames,avg=v=>Number((v/n).toFixed(3));
 return{frames:n,avgFps:Number((1000/Math.max(.001,p.totalFrameMs/n)).toFixed(1)),avgFrameMs:avg(p.totalFrameMs),worstFrameMs:Number(p.worstFrameMs.toFixed(2)),framesOver16_7:p.over16_7,framesOver33:p.over33,framesOver50:p.over50,framesOver100:p.over100,avgPhysicsMs:avg(p.totalPhysicsMs),worstPhysicsMs:Number(p.worstPhysicsMs.toFixed(2)),avgRenderMs:avg(p.totalRenderMs),worstRenderMs:Number(p.worstRenderMs.toFixed(2)),avgTerrainMs:avg(p.totalTerrainMs),avgTunnelMs:avg(p.totalTunnelMs),avgTrackMs:avg(p.totalTrackMs),avgEnvironmentMs:avg(p.totalEnvironmentMs),avgCarsMs:avg(p.totalCarsMs),avgMirrorsMs:avg(p.totalMirrorsMs),avgCockpitMs:avg(p.totalCockpitMs),avgEffectsMs:avg(p.totalEffectsMs),avgHudMs:avg(p.totalHudMs),avgAudioMs:avg(p.totalAudioMs),hitches:p.hitches.slice()};
}
window.apexPerformanceSummary=()=>apexRuntimePerfSummary();
const CAR_HALF_L=2.78,CAR_HALF_W=.98,CAR_MASS=800,CAR_IZ=1080;

function resetImpactFX(){
 impactFX={side:0,forward:0,pitch:0,roll:0,vSide:0,vForward:0,vPitch:0,vRoll:0};
 impactParticles.length=0;
}
function updateImpactFX(dt){
 const spring=(x,v,k,c)=>[x+v*dt,v+(-k*x-c*v)*dt];

 impactFX.side=0;impactFX.forward=0;impactFX.vSide=0;impactFX.vForward=0;
 [impactFX.pitch,impactFX.vPitch]=spring(impactFX.pitch,impactFX.vPitch,58,14);
 [impactFX.roll,impactFX.vRoll]=spring(impactFX.roll,impactFX.vRoll,54,13);

 for(let i=impactParticles.length-1;i>=0;i--){
   const p=impactParticles[i];p.life-=dt;
   if(p.life<=0){impactParticles.splice(i,1);continue}
   p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.z+=p.vz*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.vy-=9.81*dt;
   p.vx*=Math.exp(-1.7*dt);p.vz*=Math.exp(-1.7*dt);
 }
}
function impactCamera(nx,nz,severity){
 if(!player)return;
 const a=carAxes(),side=nx*a.rx+nz*a.rz,front=nx*a.fx+nz*a.fz,s=clamp(severity,0,1.8);

 // El impacto ya NO desplaza físicamente el punto de vista.
 // Solo provoca cabeceo/balanceo del conjunto visual.
 impactFX.vPitch+=Math.abs(front)*s*11.5+(1-Math.abs(front))*s*3.4;
 impactFX.vRoll+=-side*s*.050;
}
function spawnImpactParticles(x,y,z,nx,nz,severity){
 const n=Math.round(clamp(4+severity*8,4,18));
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const side=(Math.random()-.5)*2,along=(Math.random()-.5)*2;
   impactParticles.push({
     x,z,y:y+.10,
     vx:-nx*(2+Math.random()*5)*severity + (-nz)*side*2.6 + along,
     vz:-nz*(2+Math.random()*5)*severity + ( nx)*side*2.6 + along,
     vy:1.6+Math.random()*4.8*severity,
     life:.18+Math.random()*.42,
     size:.7+Math.random()*1.6,
     kind:Math.random()<.78?"spark":"debris"
   });
 }
 if(impactParticles.length>120)impactParticles.splice(0,impactParticles.length-120);
}
function drawImpactParticles(cam,pitch){
 for(const p of impactParticles){
   const v=worldToCamera(p.x,p.y,p.z,cam);if(v.z<1.2||v.z>220)continue;
   const q=projectLocal(v.x,v.y,v.z,pitch);if(!q)continue;
   const alpha=clamp(p.life/.45,0,1),sz=clamp(q.scale*.025*p.size,1,5);
   ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;
   ctx.fillStyle=p.kind==="spark"?"#ffd66b":"#34383d";
   ctx.fillRect(q.x-sz*.5,q.y-sz*.5,sz,sz);
 }
 ctx.globalAlpha=1;
}

function freshDamage(){
 return {frontWing:100,frontSuspL:100,frontSuspR:100,sideL:100,sideR:100,floor:100,rearSuspL:100,rearSuspR:100,rearWing:100,engine:100};
}
function damageFactors(){
 const d=player&&player.damage?player.damage:freshDamage();
 const frontSusp=Math.min(d.frontSuspL,d.frontSuspR)/100,rearSusp=Math.min(d.rearSuspL,d.rearSuspR)/100;
 const frontAero=.58+.42*(d.frontWing/100),rearAero=.66+.34*(d.rearWing/100),floor=.58+.42*(d.floor/100);
 return {
   frontGrip:.56+.44*frontSusp,
   rearGrip:.58+.42*rearSusp,
   aero:floor,
   frontAero,rearAero,
   engine:.56+.44*(d.engine/100),
   steerBias:clamp((d.frontSuspL-d.frontSuspR)/100*.065,-.055,.055)
 };
}
function recalcPlayerHealth(){
 if(!player||!player.damage)return;
 const d=player.damage;
 const weighted=
   d.frontWing*.10+d.frontSuspL*.12+d.frontSuspR*.12+
   d.sideL*.08+d.sideR*.08+d.floor*.13+
   d.rearSuspL*.10+d.rearSuspR*.10+d.rearWing*.07+d.engine*.10;
 player.hp=clamp(weighted,0,100);
}
function damageZoneMultiplier(zone){
 if(zone==="frontWing")return 1.25;
 if(zone==="frontSuspL"||zone==="frontSuspR")return 1.18;
 if(zone==="rearSuspL"||zone==="rearSuspR")return 1.12;
 if(zone==="rearWing")return 1.05;
 if(zone==="sideL"||zone==="sideR")return .88;
 return 1;
}
function applyPlayerDamage(zone,amount,severity=0){
 if(!player.damage)player.damage=freshDamage();
 if(!(zone in player.damage))zone="floor";
 const mul=damageZoneMultiplier(zone);
 player.damage[zone]=clamp(player.damage[zone]-amount*mul,0,100);
 if(severity>1.05){
   player.damage.floor=clamp(player.damage.floor-amount*.22,0,100);
   if(zone==="rearWing"||zone.startsWith("rearSusp"))player.damage.engine=clamp(player.damage.engine-amount*.18,0,100);
 }
 recalcPlayerHealth();
 player.lastDamageZone=zone;
}
function playerZoneFromLocal(long,lat){
 if(long>2.15)return "frontWing";
 if(long>1.00&&Math.abs(lat)>.58)return lat<0?"frontSuspL":"frontSuspR";
 if(long<-1.05&&Math.abs(lat)>.58)return lat<0?"rearSuspL":"rearSuspR";
 if(long<-2.05)return "rearWing";
 if(Math.abs(lat)>.68)return lat<0?"sideL":"sideR";
 return "floor";
}
function carAxesAt(heading){
 const fx=Math.sin(heading),fz=Math.cos(heading),rx=fz,rz=-fx;
 return {fx,fz,rx,rz};
}
function makeCarBody(x,z,heading,halfL=CAR_HALF_L,halfW=CAR_HALF_W){
 const a=carAxesAt(heading);
 return {x,z,heading,halfL,halfW,...a};
}
function supportPoint(body,dx,dz){
 const sf=(body.fx*dx+body.fz*dz)>=0?1:-1,sr=(body.rx*dx+body.rz*dz)>=0?1:-1;
 return {x:body.x+body.fx*body.halfL*sf+body.rx*body.halfW*sr,
         z:body.z+body.fz*body.halfL*sf+body.rz*body.halfW*sr};
}
function satCarCollision(A,B){
 const axes=[[A.fx,A.fz],[A.rx,A.rz],[B.fx,B.fz],[B.rx,B.rz]];
 let bestPen=Infinity,bnx=0,bnz=0;
 const dcx=A.x-B.x,dcz=A.z-B.z;
 for(const [ax,az] of axes){
   const ra=A.halfL*Math.abs(A.fx*ax+A.fz*az)+A.halfW*Math.abs(A.rx*ax+A.rz*az);
   const rb=B.halfL*Math.abs(B.fx*ax+B.fz*az)+B.halfW*Math.abs(B.rx*ax+B.rz*az);
   const dist=Math.abs(dcx*ax+dcz*az),pen=ra+rb-dist;
   if(pen<=0)return null;
   if(pen<bestPen){
     bestPen=pen;
     const sign=(dcx*ax+dcz*az)>=0?1:-1;
     bnx=ax*sign;bnz=az*sign;
   }
 }
 const pa=supportPoint(A,-bnx,-bnz),pb=supportPoint(B,bnx,bnz);
 return {penetration:bestPen,nx:bnx,nz:bnz,x:(pa.x+pb.x)*.5,z:(pa.z+pb.z)*.5,pa,pb};
}
function rivalBodyState(rv){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(rv.s),heading=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz)+(rv.yawKick||0),body=makeCarBody(q.x+q.ox*rv.lat,q.z+q.oz*rv.lat,heading);
 const vx=q.tx*rv.speed+q.nx*(rv.latVel||0),vz=q.tz*rv.speed+q.nz*(rv.latVel||0);
 return {q,body,vx,vz};
}

// ---------- v5.144 · IMPACTOS RECÍPROCOS DE RIVALES ----------
// Hasta v5.143 el jugador recibía un momento de guiñada físico, pero el rival sólo una
// corrección visual muy pequeña que se borraba en unas décimas. Además, los rivales no
// chocaban físicamente entre sí. El resultado era una asimetría: tocar al jugador tenía
// consecuencias, tocar a otro rival casi ninguna. Estos estados mantienen la IA barata
// (s/lat) pero dejan que un impacto tenga inercia, pérdida de velocidad, excursión y trompo.
function rivalDriverStability(rv){
 return clamp((rv.skill||.93)*.27+(rv.consistency||.86)*.34+(rv.awareness||.88)*.25+(rv.cornerSkill||.94)*.14,.76,.995);
}
function rivalRecklessness(rv){
 return clamp((rv.aggression||.5)*.58+(rv.bravery||.9)*.27+(1-(rv.awareness||.9))*.15,.20,.98);
}
function rivalAvoidanceScale(rv){
 const mem=rv.contactRiskMemory||0;
 return clamp(1+(rv.awareness-.88)*.92+(rv.consistency-.86)*.36-(rv.aggression-.55)*.46+mem*.38,.80,1.30);
}
function rivalImpactRank(mode){return mode==='SPIN'?3:mode==='WOBBLE'?2:mode==='RECOVER'?1:0;}
function rivalIncidentNearPlayer(rv,range=260){return !!(player&&Math.abs((rv.total||0)-(player.total||0))<range);}
function triggerRivalIncident(rv,opt={}){
 if(!rv||rv.finished)return;
 const severity=clamp(Number(opt.severity)||0,0,1.8),closing=Math.max(0,Number(opt.closing)||0),pen=Math.max(0,Number(opt.penetration)||0),yawImpulse=Number(opt.yawImpulse)||0;
 const zone=opt.zone||'body',stability=rivalDriverStability(rv),reckless=rivalRecklessness(rv),sideHeavy=/Susp|side/i.test(zone),wing=/Wing/i.test(zone);
 const memoryGain=(severity*.42+Math.min(.75,pen)*.085+Math.min(.18,closing/55))*(.88+.30*(rv.awareness||.88))*(1-.34*(rv.aggression||.5));
 rv.contactRiskMemory=clamp((rv.contactRiskMemory||0)+memoryGain,0,1);
 const cautionBase=.52+1.55*((rv.awareness||.88)*.63+(rv.consistency||.86)*.37)*(1-.53*(rv.aggression||.5));
 rv.cautionTimer=Math.max(rv.cautionTimer||0,cautionBase+severity*.72+Math.min(.35,pen*.22));
 const avoid=Number(opt.avoidSide)||0;if(avoid)rv.contactAvoidSide=Math.sign(avoid);
 rv.impactSource=opt.source||rv.impactSource||'CONTACT';rv.impactOtherId=opt.otherId??rv.impactOtherId??null;
 rv.impactStress=clamp((rv.impactStress||0)+severity*.60+Math.min(.80,pen)*.10+Math.min(1.8,Math.abs(yawImpulse))*.10,0,1.55);
 let score=(severity*.90+Math.min(2.0,Math.abs(yawImpulse))*.18+Math.min(.80,pen)*.12+(rv.impactStress||0)*.24)*(sideHeavy?1.18:wing?.82:1);
 // Un piloto consistente puede salvar un latigazo que otro no, pero nadie es inmune a la física.
 const spinThreshold=.56+stability*.16-reckless*.035;
 let next='NONE';
 if(score>spinThreshold||(severity>.72&&sideHeavy))next='SPIN';
 else if(score>.145||severity>.10||Math.abs(yawImpulse)>.30)next='WOBBLE';
 const old=rv.impactMode||'NONE',oldRank=rivalImpactRank(old),newRank=rivalImpactRank(next);
 if(newRank>0){
   const sgn=Math.sign(yawImpulse)||Math.sign(rv.contactAvoidSide||0)||(stableTrait(rv.uid||rv.id,73)>.5?1:-1);
   if(next==='SPIN'){
     rv.impactTimer=Math.max(rv.impactTimer||0,1.18+score*.92+(1-stability)*.55);
     rv.impactYawRate=clamp((rv.impactYawRate||0)+yawImpulse*.78+sgn*(.55+score*.82),-2.8,2.8);
     if(Math.abs(rv.latVel||0)<1.25)rv.latVel=(rv.latVel||0)+(rv.contactAvoidSide||sgn)*(1.15+score*2.7);
   }else{
     rv.impactTimer=Math.max(rv.impactTimer||0,.48+score*.78+(1-stability)*.28);
     rv.impactYawRate=clamp((rv.impactYawRate||0)+yawImpulse*.62+sgn*score*.22,-1.65,1.65);
     if(Math.abs(rv.latVel||0)<.55)rv.latVel=(rv.latVel||0)+(rv.contactAvoidSide||sgn)*(.28+score*1.25);
   }
   if(newRank>=oldRank)rv.impactMode=next;
   rv.yawKick=clamp((rv.yawKick||0)+(rv.impactYawRate||0)*.035,-2.7,2.7);
   if(newRank>oldRank&&sessionTelemetry&&rivalIncidentNearPlayer(rv,320))pushTelemetryEvent('RIVAL_INCIDENT',{rivalId:rv.id,rivalUid:raceIdentity(rv.id),rivalName:raceName(rv.id),team:rv.team,mode:next,source:opt.source||'CONTACT',otherId:opt.otherId??null,zone,severity:telemetryRound(severity,3),closingKph:telemetryRound(closing*3.6,1),penetrationM:telemetryRound(pen,3),score:telemetryRound(score,3),stability:telemetryRound(stability,3),aggression:telemetryRound(rv.aggression,3)});
 }
}
function updateRivalIncidentTimers(rv,dt){
 rv.cautionTimer=Math.max(0,(rv.cautionTimer||0)-dt);
 rv.contactRiskMemory=Math.max(0,(rv.contactRiskMemory||0)*Math.exp(-dt/(9+(rv.awareness||.88)*7)));
 rv.impactStress=Math.max(0,(rv.impactStress||0)*Math.exp(-.31*dt));
 rv.impactContactCooldown=Math.max(0,(rv.impactContactCooldown||0)-dt);
 rv.rivalContactCooldown=Math.max(0,(rv.rivalContactCooldown||0)-dt);
 const mode=rv.impactMode||'NONE';
 if(mode!=='NONE')rv.impactTimer=Math.max(0,(rv.impactTimer||0)-dt);
 if((mode==='WOBBLE'||mode==='SPIN')&&(rv.impactTimer||0)<=0){
   rv.impactMode='RECOVER';rv.impactTimer=.78+(1-rivalDriverStability(rv))*1.25;
 }else if(mode==='RECOVER'&&(rv.impactTimer||0)<=0){
   rv.impactMode='NONE';rv.impactSource=null;rv.impactOtherId=null;rv.impactYawRate*=.25;
   if(sessionTelemetry&&rivalIncidentNearPlayer(rv,260))pushTelemetryEvent('RIVAL_RECOVER',{rivalId:rv.id,rivalUid:raceIdentity(rv.id),rivalName:raceName(rv.id),team:rv.team,damage:telemetryRound(rv.damage??100,1)});
 }
}
function applyRivalIncidentTargets(rv,target,line){
 const mode=rv.impactMode||'NONE',stability=rivalDriverStability(rv);
 if(rv.cautionTimer>0){
   const caution=clamp(((rv.awareness||.88)*.68+(rv.consistency||.86)*.32)-rv.aggression*.32+(rv.contactRiskMemory||0)*.45,.20,1.10);
   line+=(rv.contactAvoidSide||0)*(.18+.64*caution)*clamp(rv.cautionTimer/1.5,.25,1);
   rv.racecraftNote='CAUTION';
 }
 if(mode==='WOBBLE'){
   target*=.84+.10*stability;line=lerp(rv.lat,line,.24);rv.racecraftNote='WOBBLE';
 }else if(mode==='SPIN'){
   target=Math.min(target,6.5+10*stability);line=rv.lat;rv.racecraftNote='SPIN';
 }else if(mode==='RECOVER'){
   target*=.88+.07*stability;line=lerp(rv.lat,line,.46);rv.racecraftNote='RECOVER';
 }
 return {target,line};
}
function resolveRivalRivalCollision(a,b){
 if(!a||!b||a===b||a.finished||b.finished)return false;
 // Broad phase barato: 210 pares máximos; SAT sólo si realmente comparten unos pocos metros.
 if(Math.abs((a.total||0)-(b.total||0))>7.2||Math.abs((a.lat||0)-(b.lat||0))>3.0)return false;
 const A=rivalBodyState(a),B=rivalBodyState(b);
 if(Math.hypot(A.body.x-B.body.x,A.body.z-B.body.z)>7.0)return false;
 const c=satCarCollision(A.body,B.body);if(!c)return false;
 const relVx=A.vx-B.vx,relVz=A.vz-B.vz,vn=relVx*c.nx+relVz*c.nz,closing=Math.max(0,-vn),invM=1/CAR_MASS;
 // v5.147: en la compresión de la salida, un solape profundo a casi la misma velocidad
 // no puede generar una expulsión lateral de casi un metro en un solo substep. En un circuito importado
 // esa corrección geométrica era suficiente para mandar coches a la barrera antes de T1.
 const launchPack=currentSession==='race'&&raceT<14.5;
 const packContainment=launchPack&&closing<4.4&&c.penetration<.90;
 const restitution=packContainment?.02:(closing>18?.12:.06);let j=closing>0?-(1+restitution)*vn/(invM+invM):0;j=clamp(j,0,52000);
 if(packContainment)j*=.38;
 let aVx=A.vx+j*invM*c.nx,aVz=A.vz+j*invM*c.nz,bVx=B.vx-j*invM*c.nx,bVz=B.vz-j*invM*c.nz;
 const tx=-c.nz,tz=c.nx,vrt=(aVx-bVx)*tx+(aVz-bVz)*tz,jt=clamp(-vrt/(invM+invM),-(packContainment?.14:.30)*j,(packContainment?.14:.30)*j);
 aVx+=jt*invM*tx;aVz+=jt*invM*tz;bVx-=jt*invM*tx;bVz-=jt*invM*tz;
 const corr=Math.min(c.penetration+.025,packContainment?.30:1.25),aDx=c.nx*corr*.5,aDz=c.nz*corr*.5,bDx=-c.nx*corr*.5,bDz=-c.nz*corr*.5;
 a.total+=aDx*A.q.tx+aDz*A.q.tz;a.lat+=aDx*A.q.nx+aDz*A.q.nz;b.total+=bDx*B.q.tx+bDz*B.q.tz;b.lat+=bDx*B.q.nx+bDz*B.q.nz;
 const L=trackData.km*1000;a.s=mod(a.total,L);b.s=mod(b.total,L);
 const aq=renderRibbonFrameAt(a.s),bq=renderRibbonFrameAt(b.s);
 a.speed=clamp(aVx*aq.tx+aVz*aq.tz,0,112);a.latVel=clamp(aVx*aq.nx+aVz*aq.nz,-12,12);
 b.speed=clamp(bVx*bq.tx+bVz*bq.tz,0,112);b.latVel=clamp(bVx*bq.nx+bVz*bq.nz,-12,12);
 const arx=c.x-A.body.x,arz=c.z-A.body.z,brx=c.x-B.body.x,brz=c.z-B.body.z;
 const dYawA=clamp((arx*(j*c.nz)-arz*(j*c.nx))/CAR_IZ*.34,-2.3,2.3),dYawB=clamp((brx*(-j*c.nz)-brz*(-j*c.nx))/CAR_IZ*.34,-2.3,2.3);
 const aLong=arx*A.body.fx+arz*A.body.fz,aLat=arx*A.body.rx+arz*A.body.rz,bLong=brx*B.body.fx+brz*B.body.fz,bLat=brx*B.body.rx+brz*B.body.rz;
 const zoneA=playerZoneFromLocal(aLong,aLat),zoneB=playerZoneFromLocal(bLong,bLat),severity=clamp((closing-1.2)/25.5,0,1.60);
 // Contacto de compresión ≠ impacto. En parrilla y pelotón dos coches pueden rozarse
 // con casi la misma velocidad; ese roce debe separarlos y volverlos prudentes, no
 // acumular estrés hasta provocar un trompo artificial.
 const softRub=packContainment||(closing<(launchPack?3.4:2.2)&&c.penetration<(launchPack?.34:.24));
 if(softRub){
   const sideA=-Math.sign(aLat||b.lat-a.lat||1),sideB=-Math.sign(bLat||a.lat-b.lat||-1);
   a.contactAvoidSide=sideA;b.contactAvoidSide=sideB;
   a.contactRiskMemory=clamp((a.contactRiskMemory||0)+.018,0,1);b.contactRiskMemory=clamp((b.contactRiskMemory||0)+.018,0,1);
   a.cautionTimer=Math.max(a.cautionTimer||0,.34+(a.awareness||.88)*.24);b.cautionTimer=Math.max(b.cautionTimer||0,.34+(b.awareness||.88)*.24);
 }else if(closing>.25||c.penetration>.035){
   if((a.rivalContactCooldown||0)<=0){triggerRivalIncident(a,{severity,closing,penetration:c.penetration,yawImpulse:dYawA,avoidSide:-Math.sign(aLat||b.lat-a.lat),source:'RIVAL_RIVAL',otherId:b.id,zone:zoneA});a.rivalContactCooldown=.28;}
   if((b.rivalContactCooldown||0)<=0){triggerRivalIncident(b,{severity,closing,penetration:c.penetration,yawImpulse:dYawB,avoidSide:-Math.sign(bLat||a.lat-b.lat),source:'RIVAL_RIVAL',otherId:a.id,zone:zoneB});b.rivalContactCooldown=.28;}
 }
 if(!softRub&&severity>.03){const dmg=clamp((closing-1)*.42,1,23);a.damage=clamp((a.damage??100)-dmg*.80,0,100);b.damage=clamp((b.damage??100)-dmg*.80,0,100);}
 if(sessionTelemetry&&!softRub&&(severity>.015||c.penetration>.075)&&rivalIncidentNearPlayer(a,320)){
   if(!a.pairContactAt)a.pairContactAt={};const last=a.pairContactAt[b.id]??-99;
   if(raceT-last>.55){a.pairContactAt[b.id]=raceT;pushTelemetryEvent('RIVAL_RIVAL_CONTACT',{rivalA:a.id,rivalAUid:raceIdentity(a.id),rivalAName:raceName(a.id),teamA:a.team,rivalB:b.id,rivalBUid:raceIdentity(b.id),rivalBName:raceName(b.id),teamB:b.team,zoneA,zoneB,closingKph:telemetryRound(closing*3.6,1),penetrationM:telemetryRound(c.penetration,3),severity:telemetryRound(severity,3)});}
 }
 return true;
}
function resolveRivalRivalCollisions(){
 let hits=0;for(let i=0;i<rivals.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<rivals.length;j++)if(resolveRivalRivalCollision(rivals[i],rivals[j]))hits++;return hits;
}

function resolveRivalWallCollision(rv){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(rv.s),heading=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz)+(rv.yawKick||0);
 const x=q.x+q.ox*rv.lat,z=q.z+q.oz*rv.lat;
 const c=wallContactAtPose(x,z,heading,q.i);
 if(!c)return false;

 // Misma barrera, misma huella y misma respuesta normal/tangencial que el jugador.
 const kind=c.wall&&c.wall.barrier?c.wall.barrier.kind:"CONCRETE";
 const profile=barrierImpactProfile(kind);
 let rvVx=q.tx*rv.speed+q.nx*(rv.latVel||0),rvVz=q.tz*rv.speed+q.nz*(rv.latVel||0);
 const vn=rvVx*c.nx+rvVz*c.nz;
 if(vn>0){
   const restitution=vn>18?profile.restitution:Math.max(.02,profile.restitution*.65);
   rvVx-=(1+restitution)*vn*c.nx;
   rvVz-=(1+restitution)*vn*c.nz;
   const tx=-c.nz,tz=c.nx,vt=rvVx*tx+rvVz*tz;
   const tangentKeep=clamp(profile.tangentKeep-Math.min(.16,vn*.0028),.46,.92);
   const newVt=vt*tangentKeep,newVn=rvVx*c.nx+rvVz*c.nz;
   rvVx=tx*newVt+c.nx*newVn;
   rvVz=tz*newVt+c.nz*newVn;
 }

 // Separación geométrica en mundo, igual que el coche del jugador, y vuelta a s/lat.
 const corr=Math.min(c.penetration+.045,1.35);
 const cx=x-c.nx*corr,cz=z-c.nz*corr;
 const rt=localTrackStateFromCenter(cx,cz,{s:rv.s});
 const L=trackData.km*1000,ds=clamp(wrapDeltaS(rt.s,rv.s,L),-4.0,4.0);
 rv.total+=ds;rv.s=mod(rv.total,L);rv.lat=rt.lat;
 const rq=renderRibbonFrameAt(rv.s);
 rv.speed=clamp(rvVx*rq.tx+rvVz*rq.tz,0,112);
 rv.latVel=clamp(rvVx*rq.nx+rvVz*rq.nz,-11,11);
 rv.yawKick=clamp((rv.yawKick||0)-c.side*.030,-2.7,2.7);

 const severity=clamp(Math.max(0,vn-1)/25,0,1.4);
 if(severity>.02&&(rv.wallCooldown||0)<=0){
   const dmg=clamp((Math.max(0,vn)-1)*.50*profile.damage,.7,24);
   rv.damage=clamp((rv.damage??100)-dmg,0,100);
   const wallYaw=-c.side*(.16+severity*.72);
   triggerRivalIncident(rv,{severity,closing:Math.max(0,vn),penetration:c.penetration,yawImpulse:wallYaw,avoidSide:-Math.sign(c.side||1),source:'WALL',zone:c.side<0?'sideL':'sideR'});
   rv.wallCooldown=.16;
   if(player){
     const dx=x-player.x,dz=z-player.z;
     if(dx*dx+dz*dz<95*95)spawnImpactParticles(c.x,c.t.y+.10,c.z,c.nx,c.nz,severity*.72*profile.scrape);
   }
 }
 return true;
}

function resolvePlayerRivalCollision(rv){
 const rb=rivalBodyState(rv),pb=makeCarBody(player.x,player.z,player.heading);
 if(Math.hypot(pb.x-rb.body.x,pb.z-rb.body.z)>7.0)return false;
 const c=satCarCollision(pb,rb.body);if(!c)return false;

 const relVx=player.vx-rb.vx,relVz=player.vz-rb.vz;
 const vn=relVx*c.nx+relVz*c.nz;
 const invM=1/CAR_MASS,closing=Math.max(0,-vn);
 const autoRaceContact=(demoMode||assistMode)&&currentSession==='race';
 // v5.147: contención de contacto en el primer complejo. Si dos coches ya están
 // solapados pero casi igualados de velocidad, se separan progresivamente sin una
 // corrección lateral de 0.7-1.0 m ni un latigazo de guiñada. La telemetría mostró exactamente
 // ese patrón a 12.05 s, con cierre 0 km/h y 0.732 m de penetración.
 const packContainment=autoRaceContact&&raceT<16.5&&(player.autoLaunchCorridorHold||player.autoRaceStartProtect)&&closing<4.4&&c.penetration<.95;
 const restitution=packContainment?.02:(closing>18?.12:.06);
 let j=closing>0?-(1+restitution)*vn/(invM+invM):0;
 j=clamp(j,0,52000);
 if(packContainment)j*=.34;

 // Impulso normal recíproco.
 player.vx+=j*invM*c.nx;player.vz+=j*invM*c.nz;
 let rvVx=rb.vx-j*invM*c.nx,rvVz=rb.vz-j*invM*c.nz;

 // Fricción tangencial limitada: contacto rueda/carrocería no pega los coches entre sí.
 const tx=-c.nz,tz=c.nx,vrt=(player.vx-rvVx)*tx+(player.vz-rvVz)*tz;
 let jt=clamp(-vrt/(invM+invM),-(packContainment?.12:.30)*j,(packContainment?.12:.30)*j);
 player.vx+=jt*invM*tx;player.vz+=jt*invM*tz;
 rvVx-=jt*invM*tx;rvVz-=jt*invM*tz;

 // Separación geométrica: ambos cuerpos participan.
 const corr=Math.min(c.penetration+.025,packContainment?.28:1.25);
 player.x+=c.nx*corr*(packContainment?.48:.56);player.z+=c.nz*corr*(packContainment?.48:.56);
 const rvShare=packContainment?.52:.44,rvDx=-c.nx*corr*rvShare,rvDz=-c.nz*corr*rvShare;
 rv.total+=rvDx*rb.q.tx+rvDz*rb.q.tz;
 rv.lat+=rvDx*rb.q.nx+rvDz*rb.q.nz;
 rv.s=mod(rv.total,trackData.km*1000);

 const rq=poseAt(rv.s);
 rv.speed=clamp(rvVx*rq.tx+rvVz*rq.tz,-4,112);
 rv.latVel=clamp(rvVx*rq.nx+rvVz*rq.nz,-11,11);

 // Momento de guiñada según el punto donde golpean, no solo el centro.
 const rpx=c.x-player.x,rpz=c.z-player.z;
 const torqueP=rpx*(j*c.nz)-rpz*(j*c.nx);
 const playerSoftRub=autoRaceContact&&(packContainment||(closing<2.65&&c.penetration<.22));
 const torqueScale=autoRaceContact?(packContainment?.035:(playerSoftRub?.11:closing<12?.24:closing<22?.34:.44)):.52;
 player.yawRate=clamp(player.yawRate+torqueP/CAR_IZ*torqueScale,-2.7,2.7);

 const rrx=c.x-rb.body.x,rrz=c.z-rb.body.z;
 const torqueR=rrx*(-j*c.nz)-rrz*(-j*c.nx);
 // El rival recibe el mismo orden de magnitud de momento físico; la personalidad decide
 // cuánto tarda en recuperarlo, no si las leyes de la física le afectan o no.
 const rivalYawImpulse=clamp(torqueR/CAR_IZ*.34,-2.3,2.3);
 rv.impactYawRate=clamp((rv.impactYawRate||0)+rivalYawImpulse*.45,-2.8,2.8);

 const localLong=rpx*pb.fx+rpz*pb.fz,localLat=rpx*pb.rx+rpz*pb.rz;
 const zone=playerZoneFromLocal(localLong,localLat);
 const rivalLong=rrx*rb.body.fx+rrz*rb.body.fz,rivalLat=rrx*rb.body.rx+rrz*rb.body.rz;
 const rivalZone=playerZoneFromLocal(rivalLong,rivalLat);
 const severity=clamp((closing-1.2)/24,0,1.65);
 if(packContainment&&sessionTelemetry&&raceT-(player.telemetryLastContainedContact||-99)>.24){
   player.telemetryLastContainedContact=raceT;
   pushTelemetryEvent('RIVAL_CONTACT_CONTAINED',{rivalId:rv.id,rivalUid:raceIdentity(rv.id),rivalName:raceName(rv.id),team:rv.team,zone,closingKph:telemetryRound(closing*3.6,1),penetrationM:telemetryRound(c.penetration,3),playerLat:telemetryRound(player.lat,2),launchLaneLock:!!player.autoLaunchCorridorHold});
 }
