 if((demoMode||assistMode)&&currentSession==='race'&&!playerSoftRub&&(closing>.25||c.penetration>.035)){
   const guardSec=clamp(.55+severity*1.15+Math.min(.35,closing/28),.55,1.95);
   player.autoContactGuardUntil=Math.max(player.autoContactGuardUntil||-99,raceT+guardSec);
   player.autoContactHoldLat=clamp(player.lat,-autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s),autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s));
 }
 if(!playerSoftRub&&(closing>.25||c.penetration>.035)&&(rv.impactContactCooldown||0)<=0){
   triggerRivalIncident(rv,{severity,closing,penetration:c.penetration,yawImpulse:rivalYawImpulse,avoidSide:-Math.sign(rivalLat||(rv.lat-player.lat)||1),source:'PLAYER',otherId:0,zone:rivalZone});
   rv.impactContactCooldown=.13;
 }
 if(!playerSoftRub){player.autoLastRivalContact=raceT;player.autoLastRivalContactId=rv.id;player.autoLastRivalContactClosing=closing;}
 if(!playerSoftRub&&(closing>.25||c.penetration>.04)&&raceT-(player.telemetryLastRivalContact||-99)>.14){
   player.telemetryLastRivalContact=raceT;
   pushTelemetryEvent('RIVAL_CONTACT',{rivalId:rv.id,rivalUid:raceIdentity(rv.id),rivalName:raceName(rv.id),team:rv.team,zone,closingKph:telemetryRound(closing*3.6,1),playerSpeedKph:telemetryRound(player.speed*3.6,1),rivalSpeedKph:telemetryRound(rv.speed*3.6,1),longitudinalM:telemetryRound(localLong,2),lateralM:telemetryRound(localLat,2),penetrationM:telemetryRound(c.penetration,3),severity:telemetryRound(severity,3)});
 }
 if(!playerSoftRub&&severity>.03&&(rv.contactCooldown||0)<=0){
   const dmg=clamp((closing-1.0)*.48,1.0,27);
   applyPlayerDamage(zone,dmg,severity);
   rv.damage=clamp((rv.damage??100)-dmg*.85,0,100);
   rv.contactCooldown=.18;
   soundImpact(.42+severity*.42);
   impactCamera(c.nx,c.nz,severity);
   const y=trackSurfaceHeightAt(player.s,player.lat,.12);
   if(severity>.20)spawnImpactParticles(c.x,y,c.z,c.nx,c.nz,severity);
 }
 return true;
}

function wallFootprintSamples(x,z,heading){
 const a=carAxesAt(heading),L=CAR_HALF_L,W=CAR_HALF_W;
 // Huella única para jugador y rivales. Todos prueban exactamente el mismo perímetro.
 const defs=[
   [ L,-W,"frontWing"],[ L,W,"frontWing"],
   [ L*.52,-W,"frontSuspL"],[ L*.52,W,"frontSuspR"],
   [ 0,-W,"sideL"],[0,W,"sideR"],
   [-L*.52,-W,"rearSuspL"],[-L*.52,W,"rearSuspR"],
   [-L,-W*.80,"rearWing"],[-L,W*.80,"rearWing"]
 ];
 return defs.map(([lo,la,zone])=>({
   x:x+a.fx*lo+a.rx*la,z:z+a.fz*lo+a.rz*la,lo,la,zone
 }));
}
function localTrackStateFromCenter(px,pz,center){
 // Proyección sobre la MISMA geometría que dibuja el asfalto y los muros.
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 let s=Number.isFinite(center.s)?center.s:0;
 let q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s);
 let dx=px-q.x,dz=pz-q.z;
 let along=finite(dx*q.tx+dz*q.tz,0);
 along=clamp(along,-7.5,7.5);s=mod(s+along,L);
 q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s);dx=px-q.x;dz=pz-q.z;
 along=finite(dx*q.tx+dz*q.tz,0);
 along=clamp(along,-4.0,4.0);s=mod(s+along,L);
 q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s);dx=px-q.x;dz=pz-q.z;
 const od=q.ox*q.ox+q.oz*q.oz||1;
 const lat=finite((dx*q.ox+dz*q.oz)/od,0);
 return {...q,s,index:q.i,lat,dist:Math.abs(lat),px:q.x,pz:q.z};
}

function wallBarrierStyle(env,s,side){
 // v62.8.0 · WALL es una familia distinta de BARRIER.
 // wall-left / wall-right sólo definen la geometría continua. Por defecto se presentan
 // como un cerramiento F1 moderno: zócalo de hormigón publicitario + debris fence.
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:'')||{},cfg=j.walls||{},
       h=Number.isFinite(+cfg.heightM)&&+cfg.heightM>0?+cfg.heightM:((env&&env.wallH)||1.08),
       rawFh=Number.isFinite(+cfg.fenceHeightM)&&+cfg.fenceHeightM>0?+cfg.fenceHeightM:2.35,
       // Los JSON antiguos de APEX guardaban 1.30 m: era suficiente como dato técnico,
       // pero visualmente la debris-fence quedaba casi invisible desde cockpit.
       // wall_f1 usa una altura visual moderna salvo que el propio SVG la sobrescriba.
       fh=Math.max(2.05,rawFh),
       lean=Number.isFinite(+cfg.fenceLeanM)?clamp(+cfg.fenceLeanM,0,.95):.55,
       post=Number.isFinite(+cfg.postSpacingM)?clamp(+cfg.postSpacingM,1.8,6):2.8;
 return {family:'WALL',style:String(cfg.style||'wall_f1').toLowerCase(),kind:'WALL_F1',base:cfg.color||'#c7ccd0',accent:'#6f777d',stripe:'#eff2f4',fence:cfg.catchFence!==false,fenceH:fh,fenceLeanM:lean,postSpacingM:post,height:h};
}
function apexWallFenceTopCameraFromWG(wg,cam){
 const b=wg&&wg.barrier||{},fh=Math.max(.15,+b.fenceH||1.3),lean=Math.max(0,+b.fenceLeanM||0),q=wg.q,on=Math.hypot(q.ox,q.oz)||1,
       ix=-(wg.side||1)*q.ox/on,iz=-(wg.side||1)*q.oz/on,p=wg.top;
 return worldToCamera(p.x+ix*lean,p.y+fh,p.z+iz*lean,cam);
}
function apexExactWallFencePoly3(sg,style,cam,A,T0,T1,B){
 if(!style||!style.fence)return null;const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(sg.s||0),on=Math.hypot(q.ox,q.oz)||1,side=sg.side||1,
       lean=Math.max(0,Number.isFinite(+sg.fenceLeanM)?+sg.fenceLeanM:(+style.fenceLeanM||0)),fh=Math.max(.15,Number.isFinite(+sg.fenceH)?+sg.fenceH:(+style.fenceH||1.3)),ix=-side*q.ox/on,iz=-side*q.oz/on,
       F0=worldToCamera(sg.x1+ix*lean,sg.y1+(sg.height||style.height||1.08)+fh,sg.z1+iz*lean,cam),F1=worldToCamera(sg.x2+ix*lean,sg.y2+(sg.height||style.height||1.08)+fh,sg.z2+iz*lean,cam);
 const p=clipPolyNear([T0,F0,F1,T1],.62);return p.length>=3?p:null;
}
function apexExactWallFenceGeom(sg,style,cam,T0,T1){
 if(!style||!style.fence)return null;
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(sg.s||0),on=Math.hypot(q.ox,q.oz)||1,side=sg.side||1,
       lean=Math.max(0,Number.isFinite(+sg.fenceLeanM)?+sg.fenceLeanM:(+style.fenceLeanM||0)),
       fh=Math.max(.15,Number.isFinite(+sg.fenceH)?+sg.fenceH:(+style.fenceH||1.3)),ix=-side*q.ox/on,iz=-side*q.oz/on,
       h=sg.height||style.height||1.08,
       top0=worldToCamera(sg.x1+ix*lean,sg.y1+h+fh,sg.z1+iz*lean,cam),
       top1=worldToCamera(sg.x2+ix*lean,sg.y2+h+fh,sg.z2+iz*lean,cam);
 // Base semántica de la valla = coronación del muro. No se deduce de un polígono clippeado.
 return {base0:T0,top0,top1,base1:T1};
}
function apexWallFenceProfileWorld(sg,style,cam){
 if(!sg||!style||style.fence===false)return null;
 const side=sg.side||1,q=renderRibbonFrameAt(sg.s||0),on=Math.hypot(q.ox,q.oz)||1,
       h=(Number.isFinite(+sg.height)&&+sg.height>0)?+sg.height:(+style.height||1.04),
       fh=Math.max(2.05,(Number.isFinite(+sg.fenceH)&&+sg.fenceH>0)?+sg.fenceH:(+style.fenceH||2.35)),
       lean=Number.isFinite(+sg.fenceLeanM)?Math.max(0,+sg.fenceLeanM):Math.max(0,+style.fenceLeanM||.55),
       verticalH=Math.max(1.45,fh*.74),cantH=Math.max(.34,fh-verticalH),ix=-side*q.ox/on,iz=-side*q.oz/on;
 const B0=worldToCamera(sg.x1,sg.y1+h,sg.z1,cam),B1=worldToCamera(sg.x2,sg.y2+h,sg.z2,cam),
       J0=worldToCamera(sg.x1,sg.y1+h+verticalH,sg.z1,cam),J1=worldToCamera(sg.x2,sg.y2+h+verticalH,sg.z2,cam),
       T0=worldToCamera(sg.x1+ix*lean,sg.y1+h+verticalH+cantH,sg.z1+iz*lean,cam),
       T1=worldToCamera(sg.x2+ix*lean,sg.y2+h+verticalH+cantH,sg.z2+iz*lean,cam);
 return {B0,B1,J0,J1,T0,T1,fh,verticalH,cantH,lean};
}
function apexWallFenceScreenLine(lineFn,a,b,w,col,bias){return lineFn(a,b,w,col,bias)!==false}
function barrierImpactProfile(kind){
 if(kind==="TYRE")return {restitution:.02,tangentKeep:.52,damage:.38,scrape:.80,shake:.85};
 if(kind==="TECPRO"||kind==="TECPRO_FENCE")return {restitution:.05,tangentKeep:.68,damage:.62,scrape:.95,shake:1.00};
 if(kind==="SAFER"||kind==="SAFER_FENCE")return {restitution:.07,tangentKeep:.74,damage:.78,scrape:1.05,shake:1.08};
 if(kind==="GUARDRAIL")return {restitution:.08,tangentKeep:.76,damage:.92,scrape:1.12,shake:1.10};
 return {restitution:.10,tangentKeep:.82,damage:1.05,scrape:1.12,shake:1.16};
}

function wallGeometryAtS(s,side){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),env=envProfile(s),cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld?trackData.customWorld:null,L=trackData.km*1000;
 const samples=cw?(side>0?cw.wallLeftSamples:cw.wallRightSamples):null,wall=Array.isArray(samples)?guideContinuousSample(samples,s,L,18,24):null;
 const enabled=!!wall,edge=wall?Math.max(env.half+.25,Math.abs(wall.lat)):(env.half+env.curb+env.runoff);
 const x=wall&&Number.isFinite(wall.x)?wall.x:q.x+q.ox*edge*side,z=wall&&Number.isFinite(wall.z)?wall.z:q.z+q.oz*edge*side,barrier=wallBarrierStyle(env,s,side),baseY=apexRoadSideHeight(q,env,side);
 return{s:mod(s,L),side,q,env,enabled,edge,barrier,bottom:{x,y:baseY,z},top:{x,y:baseY+Math.max(env.wallH,barrier.height||env.wallH),z}};
}
function tracePolyPath(p){
 if(!p||p.length<3)return false;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p[0][0],p[0][1]);
 for(let i=1;i<p.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(p[i][0],p[i][1]);
 ctx.closePath();return true;
}
function mixPt(a,b,t){return {x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t}}
function mixPt2(a,b,t){return [a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t]}

function wallContactAtPose(x,z,heading,hint){
 const nearest=nearestTrackState(x,z,hint),center=localTrackStateFromCenter(x,z,{s:nearest.s}),svgContact=apexImportedBarrierContactAtPose(x,z,heading,center),cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;
 // En un circuito SVG autoritativo la colisión de wall-left/wall-right y barrier-* es
 // literalmente contra sus segmentos X/Z. Nunca reconstruimos una pared por s/lat.
 if(cw&&cw.authoritative)return svgContact;
 const left=wallGeometryAtS(center.s,1),right=wallGeometryAtS(center.s,-1);
 if(!left.enabled&&!right.enabled)return svgContact;

 const nearestEdge=Math.min(left.enabled?left.edge:Infinity,right.enabled?right.edge:Infinity);
 if(Math.abs(center.lat)<nearestEdge-3.45)return svgContact;

 let best=null;
 for(const sp of wallFootprintSamples(x,z,heading)){
   const t=localTrackStateFromCenter(sp.x,sp.z,center);
   for(const side of [1,-1]){
     const wg=wallGeometryAtS(t.s,side);if(!wg.enabled)continue;
     const crossed=side>0?t.lat>wg.edge:t.lat<-wg.edge;
     if(!crossed)continue;
     const pen=Math.abs(t.lat)-wg.edge;
     const on=Math.hypot(wg.q.ox,wg.q.oz)||1,nx=wg.q.ox/on*side,nz=wg.q.oz/on*side;
     if(!best||pen>best.penetration){
       best={penetration:pen,nx,nz,x:sp.x,z:sp.z,zone:sp.zone,lo:sp.lo,la:sp.la,t:{...t,y:wg.q.y},side,wall:wg};
     }
   }
 }
 if(svgContact&&(!best||svgContact.penetration>best.penetration))best=svgContact;
 return best;
}


function clampCameraToWalls(cx,cz,tr){
 // Compatibilidad: desde v4.7B.2 la cámara no se traslada durante impactos.
 return{x:cx,z:cz};
}

function apexRoadGradeCameraPitchTarget(s,dirSign=1,lat=0){
 if(!trackData||!Number.isFinite(s))return 0;
 const sign=dirSign>=0?1:-1,L=trackData.km*1000,baseS=mod(s-sign*4,L),base=trackSurfacePoseAt(baseS,lat),samples=[[12,.50],[30,.32],[58,.18]];
 let grade=0,w=0; for(const it of samples){const d=it[0],ww=it[1],q=trackSurfacePoseAt(mod(s+sign*d,L),lat),run=d+4;grade+=((q.y-base.y)/Math.max(1,run))*ww;w+=ww;}
 grade/=Math.max(.001,w);
 // Convertimos pendiente geométrica en desplazamiento real del horizonte. Antes se
 // multiplicaban metros por un número fijo, de modo que un 10 % apenas movía la vista.
 const f=(W*.5)/Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5),angle=Math.atan(clamp(grade,-.24,.24));
 return clamp(Math.tan(angle)*f*.72,-H*.095,H*.095);
}
function driverCamera(){
 if(typeof apexInspectionActive==='function'&&apexInspectionActive())return apexInspectionDriverCamera();
 const tr=player.track||syncPlayerTrack(),a=carAxes(),surface=playerDriveSurfacePose();
 // Anticipación horizontal contenida; el pitch longitudinal se calcula por separado
 // usando la pendiente real de varios puntos de la pista.
 const look=clamp(finite(player.cameraLook,0),-.045,.045),c=Math.cos(look),sn=Math.sin(look);
 const tx=a.fx*c+a.rx*sn,tz=a.fz*c+a.rz*sn,nx=a.rx*c-a.fx*sn,nz=a.rz*c-a.fz*sn;
 let pitch=impactFX.pitch+finite(player.cameraGradePitch,0);
 if(Math.abs(player.trackDirDot)>.30){
   // Corrección local muy pequeña para irregularidades inmediatas; la pendiente principal
   // ya está resuelta por cameraGradePitch y no debe duplicarse aquí.
   const sign=player.trackDirDot>=0?1:-1,aim=(player.driveNetwork==='PIT'&&player.pitState)?{y:player.pitState.y}:trackSurfacePoseAt(player.s+sign*16,player.lat);
   pitch+=clamp((aim.y-surface.y)*.48,-H*.008,H*.008);
 }
 pitch+=clamp(-finite(player.longG,0)*H*.00135,-H*.008,H*.008);
 const roadRoll=player.driveNetwork==='PIT'?0:trackSurfaceCameraRollAt(player.s,player.lat,player.trackDirDot);
 // Datum vertical = superficie REAL bajo el coche; la altura de ojos se añade una sola vez en proyección.
 return {...tr,x:player.x,z:player.z,y:surface.y,tx,tz,nx,nz,pitch,bank:surface.bank,roadRoll,surfaceY:surface.y,centerY:surface.centerY};
}


window.apexCameraGradeDiagnostics=()=>{
 if(!trackData||!player)return null;const sign=(player.trackDirDot||1)>=0?1:-1,L=trackData.km*1000,out=[];
 for(const d of [0,12,30,58,90]){const s=mod(player.s+sign*d,L),q=trackSurfacePoseAt(s,player.lat);out.push({aheadM:d,s:Number(s.toFixed(2)),elevationM:Number(q.y.toFixed(3))});}
 return{circuit:trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir,s:Number(player.s.toFixed(2)),cameraGradePitchPx:Number(finite(player.cameraGradePitch,0).toFixed(2)),targetPx:Number(apexRoadGradeCameraPitchTarget(player.s,sign,player.lat).toFixed(2)),samples:out};
};

function apexFlatRunoffPoint(q,e,width,side,yOff=0){
 const p=ribbonWorldPoint(q,width,side,yOff);
 p.y=apexRoadSideHeight(q,e,side)+yOff;
 return p;
}
function roadStep(s,sd,cam,pitch){
 const env=envProfile(s),curb=curbInfo(s),half=env.half,outer=half+env.curb+env.runoff,q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s);
 const WP=(width,side,yOff=0)=>ribbonWorldPoint(q,width,side,yOff),
       wc=P=>worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
 const C=worldToCamera(q.x,q.y,q.z,cam),
       L=wc(WP(half,1)),R=wc(WP(half,-1)),
       LO=wc(apexFlatRunoffPoint(q,env,half+env.curb,1,.02)),RO=wc(apexFlatRunoffPoint(q,env,half+env.curb,-1,.02)),
       LX=wc(apexFlatRunoffPoint(q,env,apexRoadFlatLimit(s,env,1),1)),RX=wc(apexFlatRunoffPoint(q,env,apexRoadFlatLimit(s,env,-1),-1));
 // Los muros físicos siguen usando la geometría original; en la base actual no se renderizan.
 const wl=wallGeometryAtS(s,1),wr=wallGeometryAtS(s,-1);
 const LXT=worldToCamera(wl.top.x,wl.top.y,wl.top.z,cam),RXT=worldToCamera(wr.top.x,wr.top.y,wr.top.z,cam);
 return {
   s,env,curb,sd,d:Math.abs(sd),z:C.z,i:q.i,q,
   v:{c:C,l:L,r:R,lo:LO,ro:RO,lx:LX,rx:RX,lxt:LXT,rxt:RXT},
   c:projectRoadLocal(C.x,C.y,C.z,pitch),l:projectRoadLocal(L.x,L.y,L.z,pitch),r:projectRoadLocal(R.x,R.y,R.z,pitch),
   lo:projectRoadLocal(LO.x,LO.y,LO.z,pitch),ro:projectRoadLocal(RO.x,RO.y,RO.z,pitch),
   lx:projectRoadLocal(LX.x,LX.y,LX.z,pitch),rx:projectRoadLocal(RX.x,RX.y,RX.z,pitch),
   lxt:projectRoadLocal(LXT.x,LXT.y,LXT.z,pitch),rxt:projectRoadLocal(RXT.x,RXT.y,RXT.z,pitch)
 };
}


function clipPolyNear(poly,near=.58){
 const out=[];
 if(!poly||poly.length<3)return out;
 for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){
   const A=poly[i],B=poly[(i+1)%poly.length];
   const ain=A.z>=near,bin=B.z>=near;
   if(ain)out.push(A);
   if(ain!==bin){
     const den=B.z-A.z;
     const t=Math.abs(den)<1e-9?0:(near-A.z)/den;
     out.push({
       x:A.x+(B.x-A.x)*t,
       y:A.y+(B.y-A.y)*t,
       z:near
     });
   }
 }
 return out;
}
function projectCameraPoly(poly,pitch){
 const pts=[];
 for(const v of poly){
   const p=projectLocal(v.x,v.y,v.z,pitch);
   if(!p)return null;
   pts.push([p.x,p.y]);
 }
 return pts;
}
function wallPointCamera(wg,top,cam){
 const p=top?wg.top:wg.bottom;
 return worldToCamera(p.x,p.y,p.z,cam);
}
function buildWallRenderItem(seg,side,cam){
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 const ds=wrapDeltaS(seg.b.s,seg.a.s,L);
 const smid=mod(seg.a.s+ds*.5,L);
 const mid=wallGeometryAtS(smid,side);
 if(!mid.enabled)return null;

 // Mismo borde físico para los extremos. El estado del punto medio decide
 // si existe barrera en este pequeño segmento de 2.7 m.
 const A=wallGeometryAtS(seg.a.s,side);
 const B=wallGeometryAtS(seg.b.s,side);
 if(!A.enabled||!B.enabled)return null;

 const poly3=[
   wallPointCamera(A,false,cam),
   wallPointCamera(A,true,cam),
   wallPointCamera(B,true,cam),
   wallPointCamera(B,false,cam)
 ];
 const clipped=clipPolyNear(poly3,.62);
 if(clipped.length<3)return null;

 const z=clipped.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.z,0)/clipped.length;
 if(!Number.isFinite(z)||z>((typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())?apexInspectionFarDistance():760))return null;

 const fencePoly3=mid.barrier&&mid.barrier.fence?clipPolyNear([wallPointCamera(A,true,cam),apexWallFenceTopCameraFromWG(A,cam),apexWallFenceTopCameraFromWG(B,cam),wallPointCamera(B,true,cam)],.62):null;
 return {family:'WALL',side,s:smid,z:z-.015,poly3:clipped,fencePoly3:fencePoly3&&fencePoly3.length>=3?fencePoly3:null,env:mid.env,barrier:mid.barrier};
}


function collectExactWallRenderItems(cam){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,segs=cw&&Array.isArray(cw.wallSegments)?cw.wallSegments:null;if(!cw||!cw.authoritative||!segs||!segs.length)return[];
 const out=[];
 for(const sg of (cw._wallRenderSegments||segs)){
  const A=worldToCamera(sg.x1,sg.y1,sg.z1,cam),B=worldToCamera(sg.x2,sg.y2,sg.z2,cam);const far=(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())?apexInspectionFarDistance():780;if((A.z<.45&&B.z<.45)||(A.z>far&&B.z>far))continue;
  const sb=sg._apexStaticWall,env=sb&&sb.env||envProfile(sg.s||0),baseStyle=sb&&sb.style||wallBarrierStyle(env,sg.s||0,sg.side||1),h=sg.height||baseStyle.height||1.08,
        barrier={...baseStyle,family:'WALL',style:String(sg.wallStyle||baseStyle.style||'wall_f1').toLowerCase(),kind:'WALL_F1',height:h};
  if(sg.color)barrier.base=sg.color;if(sg.fence!==null&&sg.fence!==undefined)barrier.fence=sg.fence;if(Number.isFinite(sg.fenceH)&&sg.fenceH>0)barrier.fenceH=Math.max(1.90,sg.fenceH);if(Number.isFinite(sg.fenceLeanM))barrier.fenceLeanM=sg.fenceLeanM;if(Number.isFinite(sg.postSpacingM))barrier.postSpacingM=sg.postSpacingM;
  const T0=worldToCamera(sg.x1,sg.y1+h,sg.z1,cam),T1=worldToCamera(sg.x2,sg.y2+h,sg.z2,cam),poly3=clipPolyNear([A,T0,T1,B],.62);if(poly3.length<3)continue;
  const z=poly3.reduce((q,p)=>q+p.z,0)/poly3.length;if(!Number.isFinite(z)||z>((typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())?apexInspectionFarDistance():760))continue;const fencePoly3=apexExactWallFencePoly3(sg,barrier,cam,A,T0,T1,B),fenceGeom=apexExactWallFenceGeom(sg,barrier,cam,T0,T1);
  out.push({kind:'wall',z:z-.015,data:{family:'WALL',side:sg.side||0,s:sg.s||0,z:z-.015,poly3,fencePoly3,fenceGeom,env,barrier,advertising:sg.advertising,exact:true}})
 }
 return out;
}

const APEX_AD_BRANDS=[
 {brand:'PIRELLI',bg:'#f2cb25',fg:'#b51f28'},{brand:'ARAMCO',bg:'#2f9da6',fg:'#ffffff'},{brand:'QATAR AIRWAYS',bg:'#6b3f75',fg:'#ffffff'},
 {brand:'SANTANDER',bg:'#cf2028',fg:'#ffffff'},{brand:'TAG HEUER',bg:'#15191d',fg:'#ffffff'},{brand:'ROLEX',bg:'#17633a',fg:'#f2d866'},
 {brand:'AWS',bg:'#232f3e',fg:'#ffffff'},{brand:'DHL',bg:'#f3cf2f',fg:'#c51d29'}
];
function circuitAdvertisingStyle(s,env,side){
 if(!trackData)return null;
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir)||{},cfg=j.walls&&j.walls.advertising||{},L=trackData.km*1000;
 if(cfg.enabled===false)return null;
 const pos=mod(s,L),panels=Array.isArray(cfg.panels)?cfg.panels:[];
 for(const p of panels){if(!p)continue;const fs=Number(p.fromM),ts=Number(p.toM);
   if(Number.isFinite(fs)&&Number.isFinite(ts)&&(p.side===undefined||Number(p.side)===side)&&wrapRangeContains(pos,fs,ts,L)){
     if(p.blank)return null;
     return {brand:String(p.brand||''),bg:p.bg||'#26323c',fg:p.fg||'#ffffff'};
   }
 }
 const step=Math.max(32,Math.min(200,Number(cfg.panelLengthM)||72)),slot=Math.floor(pos/step),salt=(trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.r)||0,defs=Array.isArray(cfg.brands)&&cfg.brands.length?cfg.brands:APEX_AD_BRANDS,idx=mod(slot*3+(side>0?1:4)+salt,defs.length);
 if(mod(slot+salt+(side>0?0:2),5)===4)return null;return defs[idx];
}
function drawGeneratedWallAd(item,topL,topR,botL,botR,kind){
 if(kind==='TYRE'||item.advertising===false)return;const ad=circuitAdvertisingStyle(item.s,item.env,item.side);if(!ad)return;const a0=mixPt(botL,topL,.42),a1=mixPt(botR,topR,.42),b1=mixPt(botR,topR,.78),b0=mixPt(botL,topL,.78),P=[a0,a1,b1,b0];
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.96;ctx.fillStyle=ad.bg;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(P[0].x,P[0].y);for(let i=1;i<P.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(P[i].x,P[i].y);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();const cx=(a0.x+a1.x+b1.x+b0.x)*.25,cy=(a0.y+a1.y+b1.y+b0.y)*.25,w=Math.hypot(a1.x-a0.x,a1.y-a0.y);if(w>46){ctx.fillStyle=ad.fg;ctx.font=`700 ${Math.max(7,Math.min(15,w*.12))}px system-ui,sans-serif`;ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillText(ad.brand,cx,cy);}ctx.restore();
}

function drawWallRenderItem(item,pitch){
 const pp=projectCameraPoly(item.poly3,pitch);if(!pp||pp.length<3)return;
 const barrier=item.barrier||wallBarrierStyle(item.env||{},item.s||0,item.side||1),kind=barrier.kind||"WALL_F1",style=String(barrier.style||'wall_f1').toLowerCase();
 const pts=pp.map(p=>({x:p[0],y:p[1]})),minX=Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p.x)),maxX=Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p.x)),minY=Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p.y)),maxY=Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p.y));
 const topPts=pts.slice().sort((A,B)=>A.y-B.y).slice(0,2).sort((A,B)=>A.x-B.x),botPts=pts.slice().sort((A,B)=>B.y-A.y).slice(0,2).sort((A,B)=>A.x-B.x),topL=topPts[0]||{x:minX,y:minY},topR=topPts[1]||{x:maxX,y:minY},botL=botPts[0]||{x:minX,y:maxY},botR=botPts[1]||{x:maxX,y:maxY};
 const wallH=Math.max(4,((botL.y-topL.y)+(botR.y-topR.y))*.5),scaleHint=Math.max(1.2,Math.min(4.2,wallH*.06)),f1=(barrier.family==='WALL'||kind==='WALL_F1'||style==='wall_f1');
 poly(pp,barrier.base||'#bfc4c8');
 ctx.save();tracePolyPath(pp);ctx.clip();
 // Hormigón F1: juntas horizontales suaves, coronación clara y zócalo más oscuro.
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(75,82,88,.34)';ctx.lineWidth=1;for(let y=minY+4;y<maxY;y+=Math.max(5,wallH*.34)){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(minX-2,y);ctx.lineTo(maxX+2,y);ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.16)';ctx.fillRect(minX,minY,Math.max(2,maxX-minX),Math.max(2,wallH*.13));ctx.fillStyle='rgba(20,24,28,.13)';ctx.fillRect(minX,maxY-wallH*.14,Math.max(2,maxX-minX),Math.max(2,wallH*.14));
 ctx.restore();
 drawGeneratedWallAd(item,topL,topR,botL,botR,kind);
 ctx.strokeStyle=barrier.accent||'#737b82';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,Math.min(3,scaleHint));ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(topL.x,topL.y);ctx.lineTo(topR.x,topR.y);ctx.stroke();
 if(!barrier.fence)return;
 const fp=item.fencePoly3?projectCameraPoly(item.fencePoly3,pitch):null;if(!fp||fp.length<3)return;
 const fpts=fp.map(p=>({x:p[0],y:p[1]})),fTop=fpts.slice().sort((A,B)=>A.y-B.y).slice(0,2).sort((A,B)=>A.x-B.x),fBot=fpts.slice().sort((A,B)=>B.y-A.y).slice(0,2).sort((A,B)=>A.x-B.x),fbL=fBot[0]||topL,fbR=fBot[1]||topR,ftL=fTop[0]||fbL,ftR=fTop[1]||fbR;
 const z=Math.max(1,item.z||1),near=z<115,mid=z<260,span=Math.hypot(fbR.x-fbL.x,fbR.y-fbL.y),postCount=Math.max(2,Math.min(12,Math.round(span/(near?24:mid?36:58))));
 // Plano de malla muy tenue: mantiene silueta a distancia sin coste excesivo.
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=near?.20:mid?.14:.09;ctx.fillStyle='#d7e0e6';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(fbL.x,fbL.y);ctx.lineTo(fbR.x,fbR.y);ctx.lineTo(ftR.x,ftR.y);ctx.lineTo(ftL.x,ftL.y);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
 ctx.strokeStyle=near?'rgba(225,233,239,.96)':'rgba(210,220,228,.84)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.1,Math.min(2.8,scaleHint*.82));
 for(let i=0;i<=postCount;i++){const t=i/postCount,b=mixPt(fbL,fbR,t),u=mixPt(ftL,ftR,t);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(b.x,b.y);ctx.lineTo(u.x,u.y);ctx.stroke();}
 // Hilos horizontales: hacen reconocible la malla incluso a media distancia.
 if(near||mid){ctx.strokeStyle=near?'rgba(176,190,200,.60)':'rgba(176,190,200,.42)';ctx.lineWidth=.75;const rows=near?5:3;for(let r=1;r<rows;r++){const u=r/rows,a=mixPt(fbL,ftL,u),b=mixPt(fbR,ftR,u);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a.x,a.y);ctx.lineTo(b.x,b.y);ctx.stroke();}}
 if(near){ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(164,178,188,.42)';ctx.lineWidth=.8;for(let i=0;i<postCount;i++){const t0=i/postCount,t1=(i+1)/postCount,a=mixPt(fbL,fbR,t0),b=mixPt(fbL,fbR,t1),c=mixPt(ftL,ftR,t0),d=mixPt(ftL,ftR,t1);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a.x,a.y);ctx.lineTo(d.x,d.y);ctx.moveTo(c.x,c.y);ctx.lineTo(b.x,b.y);ctx.stroke();}}
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(232,237,241,.82)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,Math.min(2.4,scaleHint*.82));ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(ftL.x,ftL.y);ctx.lineTo(ftR.x,ftR.y);ctx.stroke();
}

function drawPhysicalLimitGuidesOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch){
 if(!renderLimitGuides)return;
 for(const side of [1,-1]){
   const A=wallGeometryAtS(a.s,side),B=wallGeometryAtS(b.s,side);
   if(!A.enabled&&!B.enabled)continue;
   const va=wallPointCamera(A,false,cam),vb=wallPointCamera(B,false,cam);
   drawCamLine(va,vb,pitch,side>0?'rgba(255,82,180,.88)':'rgba(65,220,255,.88)',Math.max(1,Math.min(2.2,180/Math.max(50,segZForGuide(a,b)))));
 }
}
function segZForGuide(a,b){return Math.max(1,(a.z+b.z)*.5)}
// v5.230.62.8.2 · ESTILOS VISUALES UNIVERSALES DE TÚNEL.
// El SVG describe el aspecto; la geometría, el recorte del terreno y la física no cambian.
const APEX_TUNNEL_VISUAL_STYLES={
 tunnel_concrete:{style:'tunnel_concrete',portalStyle:'portal_rectangular',interiorStyle:'concrete_panels',roof:'#343a3f',wallL:'#767d82',wallR:'#6f767b',lower:'#5d6469',road:'rgba(10,14,18,.27)',portalFront:'#b2b9bd',portalSide:'#899196',portalLintel:'#c4c9cc',joint:'rgba(55,61,66,.72)',light:'#fff3cf',lightGlow:'rgba(255,229,171,.18)',hasLights:true,hasPanels:true,hasServiceWalk:false,lightSpacingM:12,panelSpacingM:8,serviceWalkWidthM:.78},
 tunnel_city:{style:'tunnel_city',portalStyle:'portal_rectangular',interiorStyle:'city_panels',roof:'#3b4247',wallL:'#8c9397',wallR:'#848b90',lower:'#62696e',road:'rgba(9,13,17,.25)',portalFront:'#c1c7ca',portalSide:'#90989d',portalLintel:'#d0d5d8',joint:'rgba(61,67,72,.66)',light:'#fff5d9',lightGlow:'rgba(255,232,180,.21)',hasLights:true,hasPanels:true,hasServiceWalk:true,lightSpacingM:10,panelSpacingM:6,serviceWalkWidthM:.88},
 tunnel_mountain:{style:'tunnel_mountain',portalStyle:'portal_mountain',interiorStyle:'rough_concrete',roof:'#2b3034',wallL:'#5d6265',wallR:'#565b5e',lower:'#464b4e',road:'rgba(7,10,13,.34)',portalFront:'#787f82',portalSide:'#656c70',portalLintel:'#858b8e',joint:'rgba(40,45,48,.74)',light:'#f5e2aa',lightGlow:'rgba(245,218,155,.14)',hasLights:false,hasPanels:false,hasServiceWalk:false,lightSpacingM:16,panelSpacingM:10,serviceWalkWidthM:.72},
 tunnel_underpass:{style:'tunnel_underpass',portalStyle:'portal_underpass',interiorStyle:'underpass_concrete',roof:'#4a5054',wallL:'#83898d',wallR:'#7b8185',lower:'#676d71',road:'rgba(13,17,20,.22)',portalFront:'#aab0b4',portalSide:'#858b90',portalLintel:'#b8bec2',joint:'rgba(70,75,79,.64)',light:'#fff2cf',lightGlow:'rgba(255,231,181,.15)',hasLights:false,hasPanels:true,hasServiceWalk:false,lightSpacingM:13,panelSpacingM:7,serviceWalkWidthM:.72}
};
function apexTunnelVisualProfile(zoneOrS){
 const zone=typeof zoneOrS==='number'?authoredStructureZoneAtS(zoneOrS):zoneOrS,raw=String(zone&&zone.style||'tunnel_concrete').toLowerCase(),key=APEX_TUNNEL_VISUAL_STYLES[raw]?raw:'tunnel_concrete',base=APEX_TUNNEL_VISUAL_STYLES[key],b=v=>v===true?true:(v===false?false:null);
 return {...base,style:key,portalStyle:String(zone&&zone.portalStyle||base.portalStyle),interiorStyle:String(zone&&zone.interiorStyle||base.interiorStyle),material:String(zone&&zone.material||''),hasLights:b(zone&&zone.hasLights)??base.hasLights,hasPanels:b(zone&&zone.hasPanels)??base.hasPanels,hasServiceWalk:b(zone&&zone.hasServiceWalk)??base.hasServiceWalk,lightSpacingM:Number.isFinite(+(zone&&zone.lightSpacingM))&&+(zone&&zone.lightSpacingM)>1?+(zone&&zone.lightSpacingM):base.lightSpacingM,panelSpacingM:Number.isFinite(+(zone&&zone.panelSpacingM))&&+(zone&&zone.panelSpacingM)>1?+(zone&&zone.panelSpacingM):base.panelSpacingM,serviceWalkWidthM:Number.isFinite(+(zone&&zone.serviceWalkWidthM))&&+(zone&&zone.serviceWalkWidthM)>.25?+(zone&&zone.serviceWalkWidthM):base.serviceWalkWidthM};
}
function tunnelCoverColor(st){
 const v=apexTunnelVisualProfile(st&&Number.isFinite(st.s)?st.s:null);return v.style==='tunnel_mountain'?'rgba(69,78,66,.96)':'rgba(91,96,99,.94)';
}
function tunnelApertureHalfWidth(st){
 const env=st.env||{},svgHalf=Math.max(0,(Number(env.structureWidth)||0)*.5);
 return Math.max(svgHalf,env.half+env.curb+Math.min(env.runoff,.95)+.20);
}
function tunnelRoofHeightForState(st){
 const env=st&&st.env||{},sc=String(env.scenery||"");
 return sc==="GRADE_CROSSING"?2.68:(sc==="SVG_TUNNEL"?Math.max(3.2,Math.min(8.0,Number(env.structureHeight)||5.6)):2.78);
}
function tunnelSectionWorldPoint(st,side,height=0){
 const q=st.q,width=tunnelApertureHalfWidth(st);return ribbonWorldPoint(q,width,side,height);
}
function tunnelBasePoint(st,side,cam){
 const P=tunnelSectionWorldPoint(st,side,.02);return worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
}
function tunnelRoofPoint(st,side,cam,height=null){
 const P=tunnelSectionWorldPoint(st,side,height==null?tunnelRoofHeightForState(st):height);return worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
}
function tunnelFloorInsetPoint(st,side,cam,inset=.8,height=.12){
 const q=st.q,width=Math.max(.4,tunnelApertureHalfWidth(st)-Math.max(.1,inset)),P=ribbonWorldPoint(q,width,side,height);return worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
}
function tunnelWallTop(st,side,cam,clearH=5.6){
 const q=st.q,wg=wallGeometryAtS(st.s,side),P=ribbonWorldPoint(q,wg.edge,side,clearH);
 return worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
}
function tunnelTerrainPoint(st,side,cam,height=2.55,out=2.15,raise=.78){
 const q=st.q,width=tunnelApertureHalfWidth(st)+out,P=ribbonWorldPoint(q,width,side,height+raise);
 return worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
}
// v5.230.32 · Un túnel SVG implica terreno por encima del techo. La cobertura es
// compacta y sólo ocupa el vano + unos metros laterales.
// v5.230.50: antiguo sombreado y cubierta longitudinal artificial retirados.
function apexGradeCrossingNearUnder(maxMeters=150){
 if(!trackData||!player||!trackData.gradeCrossing)return false;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,c=trackData.gradeCrossing;
 return Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(player.s,c.underS,L))<=maxMeters;
}
function crossingWorldCam(s,lat,yOff,cam){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),side=lat>=0?1:-1,P=ribbonWorldPoint(q,Math.abs(lat),side,yOff);
 return worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
}
function drawGradeCrossingEmbankments(cam,pitch){return;
 if(!apexGradeCrossingNearUnder(150))return;
 const c=trackData.gradeCrossing,L=trackData.km*1000,half=envProfile(c.underS).half;
 // v5.177: recorte de terreno. Talud más estrecho y bajo para evitar el exceso de “meseta”.
 const slices=[-54,-42,-32,-24,-18,-13,13,18,24,32,42,54],sideBase=half+.98;
 for(const side of [1,-1]){
   for(let i=0;i<slices.length-1;i++){
     const aD=slices[i],bD=slices[i+1];if(aD<0&&bD>0)continue;
     const shape=d=>{const ad=Math.abs(d),r=1-clamp((ad-10)/44,0,1),inner=sideBase+.45,mid=sideBase+4.2+r*3.4,outer=sideBase+8.2+r*4.8,h=.05+r*3.15;return{r,inner,mid,outer,h}};
     const A=shape(aD),B=shape(bD);
     const A0=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+aD,L),side*A.inner,0,cam),A1=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+aD,L),side*A.mid,A.h*.88,cam),A2=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+aD,L),side*A.outer,Math.max(.02,A.h-.35),cam);
     const B0=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+bD,L),side*B.inner,0,cam),B1=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+bD,L),side*B.mid,B.h*.88,cam),B2=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+bD,L),side*B.outer,Math.max(.02,B.h-.35),cam);
     drawCamQuadTriangles(A0,A1,B1,B0,pitch,side>0?'#4f7144':'#496c40',.62);
     drawCamQuadTriangles(A1,A2,B2,B1,pitch,'#3f6139',.62);
     if(Math.max(A.r,B.r)>.22)drawCamLine(A1,B1,pitch,'rgba(30,55,31,.32)',1.0,.62);
   }
 }
 // Muros de ala más cortos para que el túnel no quede enterrado de más.
 for(const side of [1,-1]){
   const q0=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS-12,L),side*(half+.92),0,cam),q1=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+12,L),side*(half+.92),0,cam);
   const t0=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS-12,L),side*(half+.92),2.45,cam),t1=crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+12,L),side*(half+.92),2.45,cam);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(q0,t0,t1,q1,pitch,'#a9afb4',.62);
 }
}

function drawGradeCrossingOverpass(cam,pitch){return;
 if(!apexGradeCrossingNearUnder(145))return;
 const c=trackData.gradeCrossing,L=trackData.km*1000,span=34,step=4.25;
 for(let d=-span;d<span;d+=step){
   const s0=mod(c.overS+d,L),s1=mod(c.overS+d+step,L),q0=renderRibbonFrameAt(s0),q1=renderRibbonFrameAt(s1),e0=envProfile(s0),e1=envProfile(s1);
   const w0=e0.half+.85,w1=e1.half+.85,deck=.46;
   const wc=P=>worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
   const L0=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,1,0)),R0=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,-1,0)),L1=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,1,0)),R1=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,-1,0));
   const L0b=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,1,-deck)),R0b=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,-1,-deck)),L1b=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,1,-deck)),R1b=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,-1,-deck));
   // Tablero: cara inferior + canto + asfalto superior.
   drawCamQuadTriangles(L0b,R0b,R1b,L1b,pitch,'#50565b',.62);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(L0,L0b,L1b,L1,pitch,'#737a80',.62);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(R0b,R0,R1,R1b,pitch,'#686f75',.62);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(L0,R0,R1,L1,pitch,shadeColor(palette().road,2),.62);
   // Bordes blancos para que se lea inequívocamente como pista.
   const li0=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,e0.half*.965,1,.015)),li1=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,e1.half*.965,1,.015));
   const ri0=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,e0.half*.965,-1,.015)),ri1=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,e1.half*.965,-1,.015));
   drawCamLine(L0,li0,pitch,'rgba(245,247,248,.88)',1.2,.62);drawCamLine(L1,li1,pitch,'rgba(245,247,248,.88)',1.2,.62);
   drawCamLine(R0,ri0,pitch,'rgba(245,247,248,.88)',1.2,.62);drawCamLine(R1,ri1,pitch,'rgba(245,247,248,.88)',1.2,.62);
   // Barreras laterales bajas sobre el paso superior.
   const bh=.72,l0t=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,1,bh)),l1t=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,1,bh)),r0t=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,-1,bh)),r1t=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,-1,bh));
   drawCamQuadTriangles(L0,l0t,l1t,L1,pitch,'#c7ccd1',.62);drawCamQuadTriangles(r0t,R0,R1,r1t,pitch,'#c7ccd1',.62);
 }
 // Sombra directamente bajo el tablero, corta y transversal.
 const uq=renderRibbonFrameAt(c.underS),ue=envProfile(c.underS),wc=P=>worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam),h=ue.half+.55;
 const A=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(uq,h,1,.02)),B=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(uq,h,-1,.02));
 const uq2=renderRibbonFrameAt(mod(c.underS+10,L)),C=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(uq2,h,-1,.02)),D=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(uq2,h,1,.02));
 drawCamQuadTriangles(A,B,C,D,pitch,'rgba(12,16,20,.26)',.62);
}
function drawTunnelSegment(seg,cam,pitch){
 const a=seg.a,b=seg.b,env=a.env;if(!env.tunnel)return;
 const zone=authoredStructureZoneAtS((a.s+b.s)*.5),visual=apexTunnelVisualProfile(zone),sc=String(env.scenery||""),isProceduralCrossing=sc==="GRADE_CROSSING",isSvgTunnel=!!(zone&&zone.kind==='UNDER')||sc==="SVG_TUNNEL";
 const roofH=tunnelRoofHeightForState(a),aLL=tunnelBasePoint(a,1,cam),bLL=tunnelBasePoint(b,1,cam),aRR=tunnelBasePoint(a,-1,cam),bRR=tunnelBasePoint(b,-1,cam);
 let aRL=tunnelRoofPoint(a,1,cam,roofH),bRL=tunnelRoofPoint(b,1,cam,roofH),aRRo=tunnelRoofPoint(a,-1,cam,roofH),bRRo=tunnelRoofPoint(b,-1,cam,roofH);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(a.v.l,a.v.r,b.v.r,b.v.l,pitch,visual.road,.58);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(aLL,aRL,bRL,bLL,pitch,visual.wallL,.60);drawCamQuadTriangles(aRRo,aRR,bRR,bRRo,pitch,visual.wallR,.60);
 if(isProceduralCrossing&&!isSvgTunnel){if(seg.z>5&&seg.z<150){const ac=camMix(aRL,aRRo,.5),bc=camMix(bRL,bRRo,.5);drawCamLine(ac,bc,pitch,'rgba(230,235,238,.12)',Math.max(1,Math.min(2,100/Math.max(48,seg.z))),.62);}return;}
 drawCamQuadTriangles(aRL,aRRo,bRRo,bRL,pitch,visual.roof,.61);
 if(seg.z>4&&seg.z<280){const low=.16,aLm=camMix(aLL,aRL,low),bLm=camMix(bLL,bRL,low),aRm=camMix(aRR,aRRo,low),bRm=camMix(bRR,bRRo,low);drawCamQuadTriangles(aLL,aLm,bLm,bLL,pitch,visual.lower,.605);drawCamQuadTriangles(aRm,aRR,bRR,bRm,pitch,visual.lower,.605);}
 if(isSvgTunnel){const rise=Math.max(roofH+.45,Number(zone&&zone.roofRiseM)||0);if(rise>roofH+.015){const c0=tunnelRoofPoint(a,1,cam,rise),c1=tunnelRoofPoint(a,-1,cam,rise),d0=tunnelRoofPoint(b,1,cam,rise),d1=tunnelRoofPoint(b,-1,cam,rise);drawCamQuadTriangles(aRL,c0,d0,bRL,pitch,visual.portalSide,.612);drawCamQuadTriangles(c1,aRRo,bRRo,d1,pitch,visual.portalSide,.612);drawCamQuadTriangles(c0,c1,d1,d0,pitch,visual.roof,.613);}}
 // Juntas/paneles longitudinales definidos por el estilo SVG.
 if(visual.hasPanels&&seg.z<320&&Math.floor(a.s/visual.panelSpacingM)!==Math.floor(b.s/visual.panelSpacingM)){drawCamLine(bLL,bRL,pitch,visual.joint,1.05,.62);drawCamLine(bRR,bRRo,pitch,visual.joint,1.05,.62);drawCamLine(bRL,bRRo,pitch,visual.joint,.9,.62);}
 // Pasillo de servicio opcional: franja elevada dentro del vano, nunca altera colisión.
 if(visual.hasServiceWalk&&seg.z<300){const sw=Math.max(.45,visual.serviceWalkWidthM),aI=tunnelFloorInsetPoint(a,1,cam,sw,.12),bI=tunnelFloorInsetPoint(b,1,cam,sw,.12),aW=tunnelFloorInsetPoint(a,1,cam,.06,.12),bW=tunnelFloorInsetPoint(b,1,cam,.06,.12);drawCamQuadTriangles(aW,aI,bI,bW,pitch,'#a7adb0',.60);drawCamLine(aI,bI,pitch,'rgba(220,224,226,.78)',1.0,.61);}
 if(visual.hasLights&&isSvgTunnel&&seg.z>4&&seg.z<300&&Math.floor(a.s/visual.lightSpacingM)!==Math.floor(b.s/visual.lightSpacingM)){const l=camMix(bRL,bRRo,.16),r=camMix(bRL,bRRo,.84),lw=Math.max(1.15,Math.min(5.0,225/Math.max(48,seg.z)));drawCamLine(l,r,pitch,visual.lightGlow,lw*2.7,.62);drawCamLine(l,r,pitch,visual.light,lw,.625);}else if(seg.z>5&&seg.z<180){const ac=camMix(aRL,aRRo,.5),bc=camMix(bRL,bRRo,.5);drawCamLine(ac,bc,pitch,'rgba(220,220,205,.08)',Math.max(1,Math.min(1.8,95/Math.max(48,seg.z))),.62);}
}

// Portal de túnel UNIVERSAL: abertura real (sin polígono central), jambas,
// dintel y dos alas que apoyan el talud exterior en sus extremos. Ambos motores.
function apexForEachPortalFace(cam,emit){
 for(const p of apexTunnelPortalDescriptors()){
  const {q,w,h,topRise,insideSign,insideDepth}=p,visual=p.visual||apexTunnelVisualProfile(p.t),portal=String(visual.portalStyle||'portal_rectangular').toLowerCase();
  if(Math.hypot(q.x-cam.x,q.z-cam.z)>365)continue;
  const mountain=portal==='portal_mountain'||visual.style==='tunnel_mountain',underpass=portal==='portal_underpass'||visual.style==='tunnel_underpass',frame=clamp(w*(mountain?.105:underpass?.065:.078),mountain?.92:.68,mountain?1.55:1.25),lintel=clamp(Math.max(mountain?.62:.46,topRise-h),mountain?.62:.46,mountain?1.45:1.15),top=h+lintel,
    returnDepth=Math.min(mountain?3.4:2.7,Math.max(1.35,insideDepth*(mountain?.68:.52))),bottom=-.14,
    radial=(lat,y,along=0)=>{const side=lat>=0?1:-1,P=ribbonWorldPoint(q,Math.abs(lat),side,y),a=along*insideSign;return worldToCamera(P.x+q.tx*a,P.y,P.z+q.tz*a,cam);},
    geom=(x0,x1,y0,y1,col,along=0)=>emit(radial(x0,y0,along),radial(x1,y0,along),radial(x1,y1,along),radial(x0,y1,along),col);
  geom(-w-frame,-w,bottom,top,visual.portalFront);geom(w,w+frame,bottom,top,visual.portalSide);geom(-w,w,h,top,visual.portalLintel);
  const inD=returnDepth;emit(radial(-w-frame,bottom,0),radial(-w-frame,bottom,inD),radial(-w-frame,top,inD),radial(-w-frame,top,0),visual.portalSide);emit(radial(w+frame,bottom,inD),radial(w+frame,bottom,0),radial(w+frame,top,0),radial(w+frame,top,inD),visual.portalSide);emit(radial(-w,h,0),radial(w,h,0),radial(w,h,inD),radial(-w,h,inD),visual.roof);
  const sill=underpass?.14:.18;geom(-w-frame,w+frame,bottom,bottom+sill,visual.lower);
  // Portal de montaña: collar exterior adicional, sin tapar el hueco.
  if(mountain){const ext=frame*.55;geom(-w-frame-ext,-w-frame,bottom,top+ext,visual.portalSide);geom(w+frame,w+frame+ext,bottom,top+ext,visual.portalSide);geom(-w-frame-ext,w+frame+ext,top,top+ext,visual.portalFront);}
 }
}
function apexDrawGenericPortalFaces(cam,pitch){
 apexForEachPortalFace(cam,(A,B,C,D,col)=>drawCamQuadTriangles(A,B,C,D,pitch,col,.63));
}
function apexWebglGenericPortalFaces(cam,pitch){
 apexForEachPortalFace(cam,(A,B,C,D,col)=>webglPushCamQuad(A,B,C,D,pitch,col,.63,-.006));
}

// Sin terrain-base, el terreno procedural tampoco puede cruzar un portal: se
// dibujan sólo las DOS laderas exteriores, nunca un cuadrilátero sobre el vano.
function apexForEachFallbackStructureBank(segments,cam,emit){
 if(apexPlateTerrainGrid())return;
 for(const seg of segments||[]){
  const a=seg.a,b=seg.b,z0=authoredStructureZoneAtS(a.s),z1=authoredStructureZoneAtS(b.s),zone=z0||z1;
  if(!zone||z0&&z1&&z0.kind!==z1.kind)continue;
  if(zone.kind==='UNDER'){
   const make=(st,side,lat,y)=>{const q=st.q;return worldToCamera(q.x+q.nx*side*lat,q.y+y,q.z+q.nz*side*lat,cam)};
   for(const side of [-1,1]){
    const v=(st)=>{const t=authoredStructureZoneAtS(st.s)||zone,w=Math.max((+t.width||0)*.5,envProfile(st.s).half+1),h=Math.max(3.2,+t.height||5.6),r=w+Math.max(8,apexPhase1Config().blendM*.50),q=st.q,x=q.x+q.nx*side*r,z=q.z+q.nz*side*r,natural=apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z,terrainGroundY(st));return{lip:make(st,side,w+.5,h+.48),outer:make(st,side,r,Math.max(h+.48,natural-q.y)),far:make(st,side,r+20,natural-q.y)}};
    const A=v(a),B=v(b);
    emit(A.lip,A.outer,B.outer,B.lip,'#51784c');
    emit(A.outer,A.far,B.far,B.outer,'#466e41');
   }
  }else if(zone.kind==='OVER'){
   const make=(st,lat)=>{const q=st.q,x=q.x+q.nx*lat,z=q.z+q.nz*lat;return worldToCamera(x,apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z,terrainGroundY(st)),z,cam)};
   const w=Math.max(14,envProfile(a.s).half+apexPhase1Config().blendM);
   emit(make(a,-w),make(a,w),make(b,w),make(b,-w),'#4f7650');
  }
 }
}
function apexDrawFallbackStructureBanks(segments,cam,pitch){apexForEachFallbackStructureBank(segments,cam,(a,b,c,d,col)=>drawCamQuadTriangles(a,b,c,d,pitch,col,.58));}
function apexWebglFallbackStructureBanks(segments,cam,pitch){apexForEachFallbackStructureBank(segments,cam,(a,b,c,d,col)=>webglPushCamQuad(a,b,c,d,pitch,col,.58));}
function circuitCurbPair(seed){
 const id=trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:'',j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},vis=j.visual||{},cc=(vis.curbs&&Array.isArray(vis.curbs.colors)?vis.curbs.colors:null)||((j.environment||{}).curbs&&Array.isArray((j.environment||{}).curbs.colors)?(j.environment||{}).curbs.colors:null);
 const a=cc&&cc[0]||'#f3f4f5',b=cc&&cc[1]||'#d93a48';return (seed&1)?[a,b]:[b,a];
}
function drawRoadSegment(seg,cam,pitch){
 const a=seg.a,b=seg.b,env=a.env,seed=Math.floor(((a.s+b.s)*.5)/2.4),V0=a.v,V1=b.v;
 if(!V0||!V1)return;
 const roadCol=palette().road;
 const leftInner0=a.curb.left?V0.lo:V0.l,leftInner1=b.curb.left?V1.lo:V1.l;
 const rightInner0=a.curb.right?V0.ro:V0.r,rightInner1=b.curb.right?V1.ro:V1.r;

 // 1) Terreno inmediato / escapatorias. Las zonas SVG adicionales se dibujan como elementos de mundo.
 if(apexInspectionActive())apexInspectorDrawRunoff(seg,cam,pitch,false);else{
 drawCamQuadTriangles(V0.lx,leftInner0,leftInner1,V1.lx,pitch,surfaceColor(env.runoffSurface,false));
 drawCamQuadTriangles(rightInner0,V0.rx,V1.rx,rightInner1,pitch,surfaceColor(env.runoffSurface,true));
 }

 // 2) Pianos: colores definidos por la paleta interna del circuito.
 const [curbA,curbB]=circuitCurbPair(seed);
 if((!mobilePerformanceMode()||seg.z<mobileControlProfile().curbMax)&&(a.curb.left||b.curb.left))drawCamQuadTriangles(V0.lo,V0.l,V1.l,V1.lo,pitch,curbA);
 if((!mobilePerformanceMode()||seg.z<mobileControlProfile().curbMax)&&(a.curb.right||b.curb.right))drawCamQuadTriangles(V0.r,V0.ro,V1.ro,V1.r,pitch,curbB);

 // 3) Asfalto principal triangulado.
 drawCamQuadTriangles(V0.l,V0.r,V1.r,V1.l,pitch,roadCol);
 const _wetRoad=apexWeatherWetFactor();if(_wetRoad>.06&&seg.z>6&&seg.z<210){const wa=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.16),wb=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.23),wc=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.23),wd=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.16);drawCamQuadTriangles(wa,wb,wc,wd,pitch,`rgba(205,225,238,${(.012+.050*_wetRoad).toFixed(3)})`);}
 if(env.tunnel)renderProfileMeasure('tunnelMs',()=>drawTunnelSegment(seg,cam,pitch));

 // 4) Sombreado central suave, también triangulado.
 if(seg.z>8&&seg.z<(mobilePerformanceMode()?mobileControlProfile().shadeMax:240)){
   const a0=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.30),a1=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.70),b0=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.30),b1=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.70);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(a0,a1,b1,b0,pitch,'rgba(255,255,255,.015)');
 }

 // 5) Líneas blancas de borde.
 if(seg.z>4&&((typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())||seg.z<mobileControlProfile().edgeMax)){
   const e=.016;
   drawCamQuadTriangles(V0.l,camMix(V0.l,V0.r,e),camMix(V1.l,V1.r,e),V1.l,pitch,'rgba(247,248,249,.90)');
   drawCamQuadTriangles(camMix(V0.r,V0.l,e),V0.r,V1.r,camMix(V1.r,V1.l,e),pitch,'rgba(247,248,249,.90)');
 }

 // 6) Línea de pitlane base.
 if(!mobilePerformanceMode()||seg.z<mobileControlProfile().pitMax)drawPitLaneLineOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch);

 // 7) Salida, meta y parrilla. La parrilla pertenece a timing.startS; la bandera a cuadros a timing.finishS.
 if(seg.z>3){
   const L=trackData.km*1000,sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec),gridBase=sf.start,finishBase=sf.finish,relMid=wrapDeltaS((a.s+b.s)*.5,gridBase,L);
   if(relMid<10&&relMid>-125)drawGridSurfaceOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch,gridBase);
   for(const c of trackData.sectorControls||[])if(apexSegmentContainsTiming(a,b,c.s,L))apexDrawSectorSurface(c,cam,pitch);
   const dfA=wrapDeltaS(a.s,finishBase,L),dfB=wrapDeltaS(b.s,finishBase,L);
   if((dfA===0)||(dfB===0)||(dfA*dfB<0&&Math.abs(dfA-dfB)<8))drawStartFinish(cam,pitch,finishBase);
   if(Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(gridBase,finishBase,L))>.5){
     const dsA=wrapDeltaS(a.s,gridBase,L),dsB=wrapDeltaS(b.s,gridBase,L);
     if((dsA===0)||(dsB===0)||(dsA*dsB<0&&Math.abs(dsA-dsB)<8))drawRaceStartLine(cam,pitch,gridBase);
   }
 }

 // Diagnóstico opcional: límites físicos de los muros invisibles (tecla L).
 drawPhysicalLimitGuidesOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch);
}

function drawWallOccluders(cam,pitch){
 // Ya no se usa: la oclusión se resuelve integrando rivales y carteles
 // en el mismo orden de profundidad que los segmentos de pista.
}

function trackPointCamera(s,lat,cam,yOff=.010){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),p=ribbonWorldPoint(q,Math.abs(lat),lat>=0?1:-1,yOff);
 return worldToCamera(p.x,p.y,p.z,cam);
}
function fillTrackQuad(points,cam,pitch,color){
 if(!points||points.length!==4)return false;
 const v=points.map(([s,lat])=>trackPointCamera(s,lat,cam,.010));
 return drawCamQuadTriangles(v[0],v[1],v[2],v[3],pitch,color,.70);
}
function drawGridSurfaceOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch,baseS=null){
 if(!trackData)return;
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 baseS=mod(Number.isFinite(baseS)?baseS:apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec).start,L);
 const midS=a.s+wrapDeltaS(b.s,a.s,L)*.5;
 const rel=wrapDeltaS(midS,baseS,L);
 if(rel>8||rel<-118)return;

 const line="#f3f4f5",boxHalfL=1.42,boxHalfW=.78,sideTh=.085,crossTh=.09;
 for(let slot=1;slot<=22;slot++){
   const off=-8-(slot-1)*4.9,center=mod(baseS+off,L),lane=(slot%2)?-1.95:1.95;
   const da=wrapDeltaS(a.s,center,L),db=wrapDeltaS(b.s,center,L);
   const dm=(da+db)*.5;

   // Solo dos líneas laterales del cajón.
   if(Math.abs(dm)<=boxHalfL+1.35){
     const seg0=center+clamp(Math.min(da,db),-boxHalfL,boxHalfL);
     const seg1=center+clamp(Math.max(da,db),-boxHalfL,boxHalfL);
     if(seg1-seg0>.06){
       for(const side of [-1,1]){
         const lc=lane+side*boxHalfW;
         fillTrackQuad([
           [seg0,lc-sideTh*.5],[seg0,lc+sideTh*.5],
           [seg1,lc+sideTh*.5],[seg1,lc-sideTh*.5]
         ],cam,pitch,line);
       }
     }
   }

   // Únicamente la traviesa delantera de cada cajón.
   const front=center+boxHalfL;
   const xa=da-boxHalfL, xb=db-boxHalfL;
   if((xa===0)||(xb===0)||(xa*xb<0&&Math.abs(da-db)<6.5)){
     fillTrackQuad([
       [front-crossTh*.5,lane-boxHalfW],[front-crossTh*.5,lane+boxHalfW],
       [front+crossTh*.5,lane+boxHalfW],[front+crossTh*.5,lane-boxHalfW]
     ],cam,pitch,line);
   }
 }
}
function pitLaneSpec(){
 const m=trackData&&trackData.spec?raceMetaFor(trackData.spec):null;
 return m&&m.pit?m.pit:null;
}
function pitLaneAtS(s){
 if(!trackData)return null;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,cw=trackData.customWorld||null;
 // Compatibilidad para herramientas que consultan por s. La conducción ya NO depende de
 // esta proyección; el jugador usa nearestPitState() y pitS propios.
 if(cw&&cw.pitNetwork&&Array.isArray(cw.pitNetwork.runs)&&cw.pitNetwork.runs.length){
   const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),p=nearestPitState(q.x,q.z,null);if(!p||p.dist>42)return null;const st=nearestTrackState(p.x,p.z,null),side=Math.sign(st.lat)||-1;return{active:true,rel:mod(s,L),side,width:p.width,centerLat:st.lat,blend:1,pit:{entry:0,exit:L,side,width:p.width,imported:true},env:envProfile(s),source:'EXACT_PIT_NETWORK',pitS:p.pitS,x:p.x,z:p.z};
 }
 const env=envProfile(s),importedPit=cw&&Array.isArray(cw.pitLaneSamples)?guideContinuousSample(cw.pitLaneSamples,s,L,20,30):null;if(!importedPit)return null;
 const side=Math.sign(importedPit.lat)||-1,width=Math.max(4.2,importedPit.width||5.9);return{active:true,rel:mod(s,L),side,width,centerLat:importedPit.lat,blend:1,pit:{entry:0,exit:L,side,width,imported:true},env,source:'IMPORTED_EDITABLE_SVG'};
}
function drawPitLaneLineOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch){
 if(!trackData)return;
 if(trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork.runs&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork.runs.length)return;
 const pa=pitLaneAtS(a.s),pb=pitLaneAtS(b.s);if(!pa||!pb)return;
 const wA=pa.width*.5,wB=pb.width*.5;
 const aOuter=trackPointCamera(a.s,pa.centerLat+pa.side*wA,cam,.008),aInner=trackPointCamera(a.s,pa.centerLat-pa.side*wA,cam,.008),
       bOuter=trackPointCamera(b.s,pb.centerLat+pb.side*wB,cam,.008),bInner=trackPointCamera(b.s,pb.centerLat-pb.side*wB,cam,.008);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(aInner,aOuter,bOuter,bInner,pitch,'#555b61',.64);
 const edge=.055;
 const ai2=trackPointCamera(a.s,pa.centerLat-pa.side*(wA-edge),cam,.012),bi2=trackPointCamera(b.s,pb.centerLat-pb.side*(wB-edge),cam,.012),
       ao2=trackPointCamera(a.s,pa.centerLat+pa.side*(wA-edge),cam,.012),bo2=trackPointCamera(b.s,pb.centerLat+pb.side*(wB-edge),cam,.012);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(aInner,ai2,bi2,bInner,pitch,'rgba(248,249,250,.92)',.64);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(ao2,aOuter,bOuter,bo2,pitch,'rgba(248,249,250,.78)',.64);

 const mid=(pa.rel+pb.rel)*.5,pit=pa.pit,centralA=pit.entry+95,centralB=pit.exit-65;
 if(mid>centralA&&mid<centralB){
   const boxEvery=17.5,n0=Math.floor((pa.rel-centralA)/boxEvery),n1=Math.floor((pb.rel-centralA)/boxEvery);
   if(n0!==n1||Math.abs(pb.rel-pa.rel)>boxEvery*.75){
     const smid=(a.s+b.s)*.5,pc=pitLaneAtS(smid);
     if(pc){
       const outer=pc.centerLat+pc.side*(pc.width*.5-.35),inner=outer-pc.side*1.65;
       fillTrackQuad([[smid-.55,inner],[smid-.55,outer],[smid+.55,outer],[smid+.55,inner]],cam,pitch,'rgba(235,238,240,.82)');
     }
   }
 }
 for(const mark of [pit.entry,pit.exit]){
   const da=pa.rel-mark,db=pb.rel-mark;
   if(da===0||db===0||da*db<0){
     const ss=mod(mark,trackData.km*1000),pp=pitLaneAtS(ss);if(!pp)continue;
     const l0=pp.centerLat-pp.side*pp.width*.5,l1=pp.centerLat+pp.side*pp.width*.5;
     fillTrackQuad([[ss-.11,l0],[ss-.11,l1],[ss+.11,l1],[ss+.11,l0]],cam,pitch,'#f7f8f9');
   }
 }
}


function collectExactPitRenderItems(cam){
 const segs=apexPitSegments();if(!segs.length)return[];const out=[];
 for(const sg of segs){const dx=sg.x2-sg.x1,dz=sg.z2-sg.z1,len=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,nx=-dz/len,nz=dx/len,hw=(sg.width||5.9)*.5,A0=worldToCamera(sg.x1+nx*hw,sg.y1+.012,sg.z1+nz*hw,cam),A1=worldToCamera(sg.x1-nx*hw,sg.y1+.012,sg.z1-nz*hw,cam),B1=worldToCamera(sg.x2-nx*hw,sg.y2+.012,sg.z2-nz*hw,cam),B0=worldToCamera(sg.x2+nx*hw,sg.y2+.012,sg.z2+nz*hw,cam);const far=(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())?apexInspectionFarDistance():720;if((A0.z<.45&&A1.z<.45&&B0.z<.45&&B1.z<.45)||(A0.z>far&&A1.z>far&&B0.z>far&&B1.z>far))continue;const poly3=clipPolyNear([A0,A1,B1,B0],.58);if(poly3.length<3)continue;const z=poly3.reduce((q,p)=>q+p.z,0)/poly3.length;out.push({kind:'pitExact',z:z-.045,data:{poly3,A0,A1,B1,B0,z}})}return out;
}
function drawExactPitRenderItem(item,pitch){
 const p=projectCameraPoly(item.poly3,pitch);if(!p||p.length<3)return;poly(p,'#565c62');const q0=projectLocal(item.A0.x,item.A0.y,item.A0.z,pitch),q1=projectLocal(item.A1.x,item.A1.y,item.A1.z,pitch),r0=projectLocal(item.B0.x,item.B0.y,item.B0.z,pitch),r1=projectLocal(item.B1.x,item.B1.y,item.B1.z,pitch);if(q0&&r0)drawCamLine(item.A0,item.B0,pitch,'rgba(248,249,250,.90)',Math.max(1,Math.min(2.2,(q0.scale+r0.scale)*.005)),.58);if(q1&&r1)drawCamLine(item.A1,item.B1,pitch,'rgba(248,249,250,.82)',Math.max(1,Math.min(2.2,(q1.scale+r1.scale)*.005)),.58);
}

function drawGridMarkings(cam,pitch){
 // Las marcas ya se integran en drawRoadSegment().
}


function camMix(a,b,t){return{x:lerp(a.x,b.x,t),y:lerp(a.y,b.y,t),z:lerp(a.z,b.z,t)}}
function screenTriArea(a,b,c){return((b[0]-a[0])*(c[1]-a[1])-(b[1]-a[1])*(c[0]-a[0]))*.5}
function clipScreenEdge(poly,axis,bound,keepGreater){
 const out=[];if(!poly||!poly.length)return out;
 const val=p=>axis===0?p[0]:p[1],inside=p=>keepGreater?val(p)>=bound:val(p)<=bound;
 for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){
   const A=poly[i],B=poly[(i+1)%poly.length],ain=inside(A),bin=inside(B);
   if(ain)out.push(A);
   if(ain!==bin){
     const av=val(A),bv=val(B),den=bv-av,t=Math.abs(den)<1e-9?0:(bound-av)/den;
     out.push([lerp(A[0],B[0],t),lerp(A[1],B[1],t)]);
   }
 }
 return out;
}
function clipProjectedToViewport(poly,pad=.16){
 let p=poly;
 const px=W*pad,py=H*pad;
 p=clipScreenEdge(p,0,-px,true);
 p=clipScreenEdge(p,0,W+px,false);
 p=clipScreenEdge(p,1,-py,true);
 p=clipScreenEdge(p,1,H+py,false);
 return p;
}
function drawProjectedConvex(points,fill){
 if(!points||points.length<3)return false;
 const clipped=clipProjectedToViewport(points,.18);if(clipped.length<3)return false;
 let area=0;
 for(let i=0;i<clipped.length;i++){
   const a=clipped[i],b=clipped[(i+1)%clipped.length];
   if(!Number.isFinite(a[0])||!Number.isFinite(a[1]))return false;
   area+=a[0]*b[1]-b[0]*a[1];
 }
 if(Math.abs(area)<.10)return false;
 poly(clipped,fill);return true;
}
function drawCamTri(A,B,C,pitch,fill,near=.64){
 // Primero clipping 3D contra la cámara; después clipping 2D contra el viewport.
 // Un triángulo grande y válido ya no desaparece: simplemente se recorta a pantalla.
 const clipped=clipPolyNear([A,B,C],near);if(clipped.length<3)return false;
 const pp=projectCameraPoly(clipped,pitch);if(!pp||pp.length<3)return false;
 return drawProjectedConvex(pp,fill);
}
function camDistSq(A,B){const dx=A.x-B.x,dy=A.y-B.y,dz=A.z-B.z;return dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz}
function drawCamQuadTriangles(A,B,C,D,pitch,fill,near=.64){
 // En esquinas fuertes elegimos la diagonal más corta para no tender un triángulo largo sobre la curva.
 if(camDistSq(A,C)<=camDistSq(B,D)){
   const t1=drawCamTri(A,B,C,pitch,fill,near),t2=drawCamTri(A,C,D,pitch,fill,near);return t1||t2;
 }
 const t1=drawCamTri(A,B,D,pitch,fill,near),t2=drawCamTri(B,C,D,pitch,fill,near);return t1||t2;
}
function drawCamPoly(poly3,pitch,fill,near=.64){
 if(!poly3||poly3.length<3)return false;
 let ok=false;
 for(let i=1;i<poly3.length-1;i++)ok=drawCamTri(poly3[0],poly3[i],poly3[i+1],pitch,fill,near)||ok;
 return ok;
}
function drawCamLine(A,B,pitch,stroke,width=1,near=.68){
 let a=A,b=B,ain=a.z>=near,bin=b.z>=near;if(!ain&&!bin)return false;
 if(ain!==bin){const den=b.z-a.z,t=Math.abs(den)<1e-9?0:(near-a.z)/den,m={x:lerp(a.x,b.x,t),y:lerp(a.y,b.y,t),z:near};if(!ain)a=m;else b=m;}
 const pa=projectLocal(a.x,a.y,a.z,pitch),pb=projectLocal(b.x,b.y,b.z,pitch);if(!pa||!pb)return false;
 if(Math.abs(pa.x-pb.x)>W*2.2||Math.abs(pa.y-pb.y)>H*2.2)return false;
 ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=width;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pa.x,pa.y);ctx.lineTo(pb.x,pb.y);ctx.stroke();return true;
}


function autoTracksideKind(env,seed){
 const sc=env.scenery||"";
 if(env.tunnel)return "none";
 if(["URBAN","MAIN_STRAIGHT","CITY_RETURN","HIGH_SPEED","HARBOR"].includes(sc))return mod(seed,3)===0?"light":"stand";
 if(["FOREST","PIT_FOREST","VALDEBEBAS"].includes(sc))return mod(seed,4)===0?"board":"tree";
 if(sc==="GRADE_CROSSING"||sc==="SVG_BRIDGE")return mod(seed,3)===0?"stand":"tree";
 if(sc==="LIGHTS")return "light";
 return mod(seed,5)===0?"board":"tree";
}
function collectAutoTracksideSprites(cam,pitch,steps){
 const out=[];
 for(let i=0;i<steps.length;i++){
   const st=steps[i];
   if(!st||st.z<10||st.z>260||st.env.tunnel)continue;
   if(i%10!==0)continue;
   const seed=(st.i*17+i*23)|0;
   const kind=autoTracksideKind(st.env,seed);if(kind==="none")continue;
   const side=((i/10)&1)?1:-1;
   const lat=(st.env.half+st.env.curb+st.env.runoff+(st.env.wall?1.0:1.9))*side;
   const sideSign=lat>=0?1:-1,G=ribbonWorldPoint(st.q,Math.abs(lat),sideSign,0),wx=G.x,wz=G.z;
   const v=worldToCamera(wx,G.y,wz,cam),p=projectLocal(v.x,v.y,v.z,pitch);if(!p||v.z<8||v.z>380)continue;
   out.push({kind:'trackside',z:v.z-.01,data:{kind2:kind,p,side,seed,z:v.z,env:st.env}});
 }
 return out;
}
function drawAutoTracksideSprite(item){
 const {kind2,p,side,seed,env,z}=item;
 const s=Math.max(2,Math.min(92,p.scale*5.6));
 if(kind2==="tree"){
   ctx.fillStyle="#173d24";ctx.fillRect(p.x-s*.05,p.y-s*.62,s*.10,s*.64);
   ctx.fillStyle=(env.scenery==="DUNES"?"#556f41":"#2b6a3a");
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y-s*1.52);ctx.lineTo(p.x-s*.46,p.y-s*.30);ctx.lineTo(p.x+s*.46,p.y-s*.30);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 }else if(kind2==="light"){
   ctx.strokeStyle="#cfd8df";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,s*.026);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y-s*1.68);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.fillStyle="#eef6ff";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y-s*1.68,Math.max(2,s*.08),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 }else if(kind2==="board"){
   const w=Math.max(12,s*.74),h=Math.max(7,s*.25),x=p.x-w/2,y=p.y-h;
   ctx.fillStyle="#757c84";ctx.fillRect(p.x-w*.28,p.y,w*.05,h*.95);ctx.fillRect(p.x+w*.23,p.y,w*.05,h*.95);
   ctx.fillStyle="#ffffff";ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);ctx.strokeStyle="#11161b";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,s*.04);ctx.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);
   ctx.fillStyle="#e03c4d";ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h*.16);
   const txt=(mod(seed,3)===0?"APEX":mod(seed,3)===1?"RACE":"GP");
   const fs=Math.max(6,Math.min(h*.44,w/(txt.length*.62)));
   ctx.fillStyle="#12161b";ctx.font=`900 ${fs}px system-ui`;ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="middle";ctx.fillText(txt,p.x,y+h*.58);
 }else if(kind2==="stand"){
   const w=Math.max(16,s*1.04),h=Math.max(10,s*.38);
   ctx.fillStyle="#555d66";ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x-w*.58,p.y);ctx.lineTo(p.x-w*.32,p.y-h*.22);ctx.lineTo(p.x-w*.04,p.y-h*.56);ctx.lineTo(p.x+w*.48,p.y-h*.56);ctx.lineTo(p.x+w*.66,p.y);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle="#9aa2a9";for(let i=0;i<5;i++)ctx.fillRect(p.x-w*.30+i*w*.11,p.y-h*(.48-i*.06),w*.08,h*.04);
   ctx.fillStyle="#2d333b";ctx.fillRect(p.x-w*.62,p.y-h*.08,w*1.24,h*.10);
 }
 // baliza baja de velocidad
 if(!env.wall&&z>18&&z<210&&mod(seed,2)===0){
   ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(238,241,243,.78)";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,Math.min(2,s*.04));ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y-Math.min(22,s*.46));ctx.stroke();
 }
}


function insertTrackNodeSamples(raw,cam,pitch){
 if(!raw||raw.length<2||!trackData||!trackData.pts)return raw||[];
 const step=trackData.km*1000/trackData.pts.length,out=[raw[0]],eps=1e-5;
 for(let i=0;i<raw.length-1;i++){
   const A=raw[i],B=raw[i+1],dir=Math.sign(B.s-A.s)||1;
   if(dir>0){
     let node=(Math.floor(A.s/step+1e-9)+1)*step,guard=0;
     while(node<B.s-eps&&guard++<8){const M=roadStep(node,node-player.s,cam,pitch);if(M&&Number.isFinite(M.z))out.push(M);node+=step}
   }else{
     let node=(Math.ceil(A.s/step-1e-9)-1)*step,guard=0;
     while(node>B.s+eps&&guard++<8){const M=roadStep(node,node-player.s,cam,pitch);if(M&&Number.isFinite(M.z))out.push(M);node-=step}
   }
   out.push(B);
 }
 return out;
}


// --- v5.38 TERRAIN CONTINUITY -----------------------------------------------
// Terreno en tres zonas:
//   1) hombro próximo ligado a la pista,
//   2) transición hacia la cota natural,
//   3) terreno lejano común, independiente de la altura puntual del asfalto.
// Además, en circuitos compactos la extensión lateral se limita usando la
// distancia al brazo NO adyacente más próximo del trazado. Esto impide que dos
// mesetas extruidas desde sectores distintos se crucen en el aire.
function ensureTerrainSpatialProfile(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||trackData.pts.length<3)return null;
 const P=trackData.pts,n=P.length,L=trackData.km*1000,step=L/n,model=trackData.model||(trackData.model={});
 const cached=model.terrainSpatialProfile;
 if(cached&&cached.n===n&&cached.clearance&&cached.nearest)return cached;

 const cell=70,maxSearch=230,range=Math.ceil(maxSearch/cell),buckets=new Map();
 const key=(ix,iz)=>ix+','+iz;
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const p=P[i],ix=Math.floor(p[0]/cell),iz=Math.floor(p[1]/cell),k=key(ix,iz);
   let arr=buckets.get(k);if(!arr){arr=[];buckets.set(k,arr)}arr.push(i);
 }
 const clearance=new Float32Array(n),nearest=new Int32Array(n);nearest.fill(-1);
 const skip=Math.max(7,Math.ceil(82/Math.max(.1,step))),max2=maxSearch*maxSearch;
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
   const p=P[i],ix=Math.floor(p[0]/cell),iz=Math.floor(p[1]/cell);
   let best2=max2,best=-1;
   for(let gx=-range;gx<=range;gx++)for(let gz=-range;gz<=range;gz++){
     const arr=buckets.get(key(ix+gx,iz+gz));if(!arr)continue;
     for(const j of arr){
       let di=Math.abs(i-j);di=Math.min(di,n-di);if(di<=skip)continue;
       const q=P[j],dx=q[0]-p[0],dz=q[1]-p[1],d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;
       if(d2<best2){best2=d2;best=j}
     }
   }
   clearance[i]=Math.sqrt(best2);nearest[i]=best;
 }
 const profile={n,step,clearance,nearest};model.terrainSpatialProfile=profile;return profile;
}
function terrainSpatialAt(st){
 const pr=ensureTerrainSpatialProfile();
 if(!pr||!st)return{clearance:230,nearest:-1,step:1};
 const L=trackData.km*1000,f=mod(st.s,L)/pr.step,i=Math.floor(f)%pr.n,t=f-Math.floor(f),j=(i+1)%pr.n;
 return{clearance:lerp(pr.clearance[i],pr.clearance[j],t),nearest:pr.nearest[t<.5?i:j],step:pr.step};
}
function terrainSideDir(frame,farBlend=0){
 let mx=finite(frame&&frame.ox,0),mz=finite(frame&&frame.oz,0),md=Math.hypot(mx,mz);
 if(md<.15){mx=finite(frame&&frame.nx,1);mz=finite(frame&&frame.nz,0);md=Math.hypot(mx,mz)||1}
 mx/=md;mz/=md;
 let nx=finite(frame&&frame.nx,mx),nz=finite(frame&&frame.nz,mz),nd=Math.hypot(nx,nz)||1;nx/=nd;nz/=nd;
 const t=clamp(farBlend,0,1);let x=lerp(mx,nx,t),z=lerp(mz,nz,t),d=Math.hypot(x,z)||1;
 return{x:x/d,z:z/d};
}
function terrainOuterWidth(st){
 const e=st&&st.env?st.env:{half:7,curb:1,runoff:3};
 return Math.max(4,finite(e.half,7)+finite(e.curb,1)+finite(e.runoff,3));
}
// v5.230.29 · En túneles SVG el terreno NO debe hundirse para alcanzar la cota de la pista.
// La carretera atraviesa el terreno: conservamos la altimetría natural también unos metros
// antes/después de la boca para evitar el embudo artificial que aparecía en la entrada.
function terrainTunnelPreserveAtS(s,margin=42){
 if(!trackData||!Number.isFinite(s))return false;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,cw=trackData.customWorld,zones=cw&&Array.isArray(cw.structureZones)?cw.structureZones:[];
 for(const z of zones){
   if(!z||z.kind!=="UNDER")continue;
   if(wrapRangeContains(s,z.s0,z.s1,L))return true;
   const d0=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(s,z.s0,L)),d1=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(s,z.s1,L));
   if(Math.min(d0,d1)<=margin)return true;
 }
 return false;
}
// v5.230.37 · Si el SVG aporta curvas de nivel alrededor de un túnel, APEX debe
// enseñar ese relieve completo incluso en móvil. No añade búsquedas por frame: sólo
// cambia el alcance de las mismas bandas de terreno ya existentes.
function terrainAuthoredTunnelReliefAtS(s,margin=88){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;
 return !!(cw&&Array.isArray(cw.terrainContours)&&cw.terrainContours.length&&terrainTunnelPreserveAtS(s,margin));
}
function terrainGroundY(st){
 if(!st||!st.q)return 0;
 if(Number.isFinite(st._terrainGroundY))return st._terrainGroundY;
 const qy=finite(st.q.y,0),s=st.s,env=st.env||envProfile(s);
 // Terreno natural suavizado: puede quedar por encima o por debajo de la carretera.
 // La carretera NO se entierra porque el mallado empieza fuera de pista/escapatoria y
 // terrainPointCamera crea el talud desde el borde hacia esta cota natural.
 const offsets=[-320,-220,-145,-80,80,145,220,320];let sum=0,wSum=0;
 for(const d of offsets){const w=Math.abs(d)<=145?1.25:1.0;sum+=elevationAt(s+d)*w;wSum+=w}
 let target=sum/Math.max(.001,wSum)-.18;
 const spatial=terrainSpatialAt(st);
 if(spatial.nearest>=0&&spatial.clearance<105){
   const ny=elevationAt(spatial.nearest*spatial.step);
   // Si hay otro brazo muy próximo evitamos levantar una montaña entre los dos.
   target=Math.min(target,Math.max(qy-8,ny-.30));
 }
 const id=trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:"",preserveTunnel=env.tunnel||terrainTunnelPreserveAtS(s);
 const crossingClear=finite(+apexCircuitVisualFeature('gradeCrossingTerrainClearanceM',0),0);if(crossingClear>0&&env.scenery==='GRADE_CROSSING')target=Math.min(target,qy-crossingClear);
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},tc=j.terrain||{},maxDrop=Number.isFinite(+tc.maxDrop)?+tc.maxDrop:13,maxRise=Number.isFinite(+tc.maxRise)?+tc.maxRise:8;
 // Fuera de túnel mantenemos el límite de seguridad habitual. En un túnel SVG (y en
 // su zona inmediata de entrada/salida) NO forzamos la cota natural hacia la carretera:
 // el túnel debe perforar el terreno, no generar un valle para llegar a la pista.
 if(!preserveTunnel)target=clamp(target,qy-maxDrop,qy+maxRise);
 st._terrainGroundY=target;return target;
}


// v5.230.19 · Altimetría editable mediante POLÍGONOS de nivel SVG.
// `terrain-base` representa que TODO el ámbito exportado es terreno y debe ir PRIMERO en el grupo.
// Los `terrain-contour` se ordenan de cota menor a mayor: base/bajo primero, cota más alta al final.
// Cada `terrain-contour` es un polígono/placa cuya frontera fija una LÍNEA de cota.
// El interior de la placa se ignora: sólo importa el CONTORNO. APEX precalcula una
// malla suave entre contornos al cargar
// el circuito; durante el render sólo hace una consulta bilineal O(1).
function apexTerrainContourList(){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;return cw&&Array.isArray(cw.terrainContours)?cw.terrainContours:[];
}
function apexTerrainContourConfig(){
 const id=trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:"",j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},tc=j.terrain||{},cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;
 const svgRef=cw&&cw.terrainReference,reference=String((svgRef&&svgRef!=='ABSOLUTE')?svgRef:(tc.contourReference||svgRef||'ABSOLUTE')).trim().toUpperCase();
 const scale=Number.isFinite(Number(tc.contourScale))?Number(tc.contourScale):1;
 const offset=Number.isFinite(Number(tc.contourOffsetM))?Number(tc.contourOffsetM):0;
 const influenceM=Number.isFinite(Number(tc.contourInfluenceM))?clamp(Number(tc.contourInfluenceM),40,450):185;
 return{reference,scale,offset,influenceM,plateBoundaryBlendM:Number.isFinite(+tc.plateBoundaryBlendM)?clamp(+tc.plateBoundaryBlendM,4,80):null};
}
function apexProceduralTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z){
 if(!trackData)return 0;
 const ns=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:280,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:260}),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(ns.s),env=envProfile(ns.s),st={s:ns.s,q,env};
 const edge=terrainOuterWidth(st),spread=terrainSpread(st),ground=terrainGroundY(st),bankY=Math.
